Chapter 1301: 1300: We from the Cultivation World Just Entered the Universe

Eleven projections were successively cast into the Supreme Council Hall, surrounding the round table, waiting for the convener to appear.

One of the members, impatient, tapped on the table: "Old Gao summoned us for an emergency meeting - why is he the one who’s late? Isn’t he usually the first to arrive at meetings?"

He was Wang Jinglong, the helmsman of the Wang Family’s conglomerate; the Murong Group was just one of his numerous subordinates.

"Did he tell you the purpose of this meeting? Did they calculate the reason for the explosion of the sun in the Sagittarius Star System?"

The citizens of the Sagittarius Star System had temporarily moved to the Eternal Star System; the conglomerates under him had all complained about the heavy losses of this relocation, asking when the Alliance could move their people back.

"Do you know, Dean?"

The Dean was a vigorous middle-aged man, commanding all the military forces of the Alliance, with a very high prestige in the Alliance.

"It’s said to be related to the assistance for less advanced civilizations, but he didn’t go into details with me."

"Assistance for less advanced civilizations? Wasn’t the matter with Sky Sea Star already resolved?" Wang Jinglong was confused by the Dean’s explanation.

"Sorry everyone, calibrating the projection equipment took longer than expected."

The projection device lightened up, and Dean Gao arrived tardily.

However, this time, it was not just his figure that appeared; an alien guest accompanied him.

Upon seeing an outsider show up, Wang Jinglong’s expression turned displeased: "What’s Old Gao thinking bringing a foreigner here? This is the Supreme Council."

"That’s the person you wanted to meet."

Only then did everyone take a clear look at the thirteenth person’s appearance—they recoiled in shock.

It was that alien named Lu Yang!

The Dean too was shocked; since the establishment of the Supreme Council, no outsider had ever taken part in such a meeting.

Even alternate members weren’t entitled to this!

"Hello everyone, my name is Lu Yang. I heard you wanted to see me, so here I am." Unlike noticing the reactions of the eleven authorities, Lu Yang greeted them with a smile, very courteously.

"You are that alien?" Once Lu Yang’s identity was confirmed, everyone immediately became interested.

The Alliance could be considered the overlord of the universe, and these eleven supreme authorities ruling the Alliance were privy to countless secrets and truths, and there were hardly any beings who could grab their attention.

Lu Yang was that exception.

It made sense that Lu Yang was brought by Dean Gao, surely Dean Gao must have found Lu Yang through an Ether Computer.

"If possible, I would prefer you call me a Cultivator, not an alien."

"Cultivator?" The council members pondered this term and even used their intelligent brains to search for it, but had never heard of it.

"Explaining Cultivators might be quite complicated; you can simply understand it as us using a special kind of power to make ourselves stronger."

"We call this special power Spiritual Energy, and Spirit Stones contain a large amount of Spiritual Energy."

Saying this, Lu Yang produced a Spirit Stone from his hand, scanned it, and projected its structure onto the Supreme Council.

"Is this a Spirit Stone?"

The council members had never seen such a structure before; they thought they had traversed the universe, had boundless domains, and seen all kinds of cosmic materials, but they had not expected to encounter an undiscovered natural material.

"There are many Spirit Stones in my world, and we all use Spirit Stones for Cultivation."

"As for the name of the civilization I come from, um." Lu Yang hesitated when mentioning the name of his civilization, "Let’s temporarily call it the Cultivation World; we can change it if there’s an official name later."

"Temporarily?" The Dean noted the implications behind Lu Yang’s wording.

"Mr. Lu, has your Cultivation World never contacted other civilizations?"

Only a civilization that has never been in contact with others would face the situation of not having a named civilization.

Lu Yang was somewhat embarrassed being asked by the Dean, but still chose to be honest.

"To tell you the truth, our Cultivation World has only recently been able to come to the universe; before this, our Cultivation World didn’t know what the universe was like."

The members of the Supreme Council were all stunned upon hearing this; the Cultivation World was too primitive to have just stepped into the universe.

"Mr. Lu, that Black Canopy Boat of yours is quite impressive; it seems it can traverse wormholes and perform spatial jumps." Wang Jinglong eyed Lu Yang skeptically, wondering how a civilization capable of such feats could have only just entered the universe?

"Oh, you mean that boat? That is also just recently developed, we in the Cultivation World didn’t have such a thing before."

Lu Yang was honest and forthcoming, which in turn made Wang Jinglong feel overly suspicious, which wasn’t good for the image of the Alliance.

Unable to suppress his curiosity, Wang Jinglong had one last question: "I see, Mr. Lu, you have extraordinary methods, capable of dealing with the Fierce Beasts of Sky Sea Star, and can stop bullets; how do you do that?"

"If I were to explain, it would take three days and three nights to clarify."

It was the truth; to explain these methods, he would need to explain the entire Cultivation system from start to finish to the members of the Supreme Council who had no previous exposure to Cultivation; it would be even more difficult.

"You can understand that these are techniques only accessible to Cultivators after comprehending the essence of the universe."

Comprehending the essence of the universe? Wang Jinglong wondered, he hadn’t heard such terminology for a long time, it sounded like something only backwards, farming civilizations that believed in nature would say.

"I know what Mr. Wang is thinking, our Cultivation World is indeed quite backwards, so I’d like to request the Alliance to send some people, just like with Sky Sea Star, to help our Cultivation World and provide us with technical support, it wouldn’t be too much trouble, just a few people would suffice."

The members looked at each other, surprised by Lu Yang’s request.

However, Lu Yang’s request was reasonable and his requirements were not high; it was just to send a few people there.

The Alliance certainly wasn’t short on personnel.

"May I ask Mr. Lu, can you tell us where the Cultivation World is located?"

Lu Yang nodded, "This is simple: please, Dean Gao, bring up a star chart."

"Ether, show the star chart."

A comprehensive star chart known to the Alliance appeared before everyone, with the Alliance right at its center.

The star chart was somewhat larger than what Lu Yang had seen in the library, but it still didn’t encompass the location of the Cultivation World.

He had no choice but to mark the location of the Cultivation World outside the star chart: "Roughly, here."

"Mr. Lu, we will need to discuss and decide on this matter," the Dean stated, already having a resolution in mind, but it was inappropriate to make a decision in front of Lu Yang.

"Alright." Lu Yang tactfully exited the Supreme Council.

After Lu Yang left, the Dean closed his eyes and thought for a moment before asking, "What do you all think?"

"I think there’s no problem, he doesn’t seem to be lying," said one of the members.

"No objections," said Wang Jinglong.

"Old Gao, what do you think?"

"Why not ask Ether?" Dean Gao suggested.

"Right, we indeed should consult Ether on this matter."

"Ether, regarding Mr. Lu’s proposal for the Alliance to send aid to the Cultivation World, what do you think?"

Ether quickly provided an answer:

"Send me."

Chapter 1302: 1301: Your Spacecraft is Too Slow

With the help of Ether, the Supreme Council unsurprisingly passed Lu Yang’s proposal to send professionals to assist the Cultivation World with modernization.

The Interstellar Alliance has its own set of procedures for dispatching personnel to remote areas, not only in the field of technology but also covering aspects such as economics, education, art, and more.

The Interstellar Alliance first planned the round trip, support duration, and various other details, then sent out notifications layer by layer to select volunteers.

Half a month has passed since the start of the process.

Lu Yang knew that the Interstellar Alliance was interested in Spirit Stones and Spiritual Energy, so he made a special gift of a few Top-grade Spirit Stones before his departure.

...

Eternal Star System, Space Port.

The huge silver spacecraft was docked at the Space Port. Its graceful lines and smooth edges, under the dim light, cast soft shadows, as if telling the stories of countless interstellar journeys. People standing below and looking up at the spacecraft felt incredibly insignificant, full of awe and amazement.

"It’s really big." Lu Yang stood underneath the spacecraft, hands in his pockets, and couldn’t help but exclaim. Compared to the spacecraft, his Black Canopy Boat seemed as crude as a broken plank of wood.

This spacecraft was the Star Path, which the support team would take to travel to the Cultivation World.

He received a notification from the Supreme Council that the support team would gather here and head to the Cultivation World. He had come early to visit the Space Port, which he had never seen before.

The Eternal Star System was akin to the capital, and its Space Port was naturally the most advanced of the entire Interstellar Alliance. Larger than many cities, the port handled tens of thousands of spacecrafts daily, docking, resting, and departing through Artificial Wormholes to various Star Systems.

After Lu Yang finished touring the Space Port, the support team took the space elevator to reach the port.

These individuals were outstanding talents meticulously selected by the Interstellar Alliance, famous in their respective fields, and at the very least, each had experience with one support mission.

They carried themselves with a dignified aura, exuding a sense of superiority from head to toe.

For them, going to assist in remote areas was like a chance to bless others with the Alliance’s advanced technologies in all areas.

Once one experienced this feeling, they would undoubtedly yearn to experience it a second time.

Not to mention that the leader of this mission was the notable figure, Dean Gao, the Chief Scientist of the Alliance.

When Lu Yang realized that Dean Gao was leading the team, he was taken aback.

"Why are you here?"

Dean Gao replied seriously, "I’m in self-exile."

"You said our Cultivation World was backward, so heading there counts as self-punishment, right?"

"..."

When Dean Gao volunteered to head to the Cultivation World, the entire Supreme Council was shocked; such an instance of a council member personally partaking in support missions had never happened before, let alone the Chief Scientist like Dean Gao, who controlled the Alliance’s most advanced technologies.

What they didn’t know was that Dean Gao planned to initiate an Ether revolution, primarily because he believed in advancing technology and was convinced that the Cultivation World was far from being as simple as Lu Yang described, and it could indeed promote technological progress.

"Take this." Dean Gao handed Lu Yang a metal box.

"What is this?"

"It’s an Ether Clone. You can consider it a part of Ether that can synchronize with the main Ether through quantum communication."

The metal box was actually the Alliance’s most advanced quantum computer. The screen displayed Ether’s friendly smile: "Mr. Lu, we meet again."

"Can we talk about something? Can you change your expression next time?"

"Ah?"

The support team was surprised to see Lu Yang chatting and laughing with the legendary Dean Gao.

"That’s Lu Yang, right?"

"Yes, that’s him. I heard he’s one of the most powerful people in their Cultivation World."

Although it was rare for those being assisted to communicate as equals with the Interstellar Alliance, considering the way Lu Yang dealt with the film crew, the support team could somewhat understand.

Lu Yang glanced at the spacecraft, "Are we taking this thing there?"

Dean Gao was a bit surprised, "What else? You think we’d use your Black Canopy Boat?"

Dean Gao was thinking practically; a Black Canopy Boat could only fit five or six people. There was no way the support team could rely on the Black Canopy Boat to get to the Cultivation World.

Lu Yang slapped his forehead, realizing his oversight; he had forgotten to tell the Alliance that they didn’t need a spacecraft, as he had a way to take people with him.

"How long does it take to get to the Cultivation World on your spacecraft?"

"About seven to eight years," Dean Gao calculated the time. "By then, we’ll be immersed in nutrient solution to reduce bodily function consumption to a minimum, essentially stopping time."

This was a common method used by the Interstellar Alliance for long voyages.

Just as Dean Gao was about to say something else, Lu Yang suddenly drew the Green Peak Sword, startling Dean Gao who thought Lu Yang was about to attack.

Lu Yang opened up the Green Blade Sword Small World and took the entire spacecraft and the aid team inside.

The relaxed and casual demeanor of the aid team vanished in an instant, replaced by undisguised shock and panic.

"What happened?"

"Where is this place?"

"Did we just travel through?"

One second they were at the Space Port, and the next, they found themselves in an unrealistically beautiful world with lush greenery and clear waters. They rubbed their eyes several times, trying to confirm that the surreal landscape before them truly existed.

It did indeed exist.

"There’s a positioning system on the ship, bring it out and see where we are!"

"Get out all other tools as well, record the data of this place!"

"Dean Gao, where are we?"

Dean Gao was also stunned, he had made ample mental preparations before departing, determined not to panic no matter what.

Yet before they even set off, something he couldn’t understand had already happened?!

Lu Yang jumped into the Green Blade Sword Small World.

Seeing Lu Yang approach, Dean Gao hurriedly asked for an explanation.

"Oh, this place? This is a Small World within my sword," Lu Yang explained casually.

"Small World?" Dean Gao heard this term for the first time.

"It’s a space artificially created by someone. Don’t you know about that?"

Dean Gao’s mouth fell open in amazement. He recognized every word for ’artificially creating space,’ but had no idea what they meant when strung together.

"Why did you bring us here, just to show off your Small World?"

"Your spacecraft is too slow. I’ll use my boat to take you there; it will only take a month."

If it was just Lu Yang himself, he could use the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype to reverse time and return to the Cultivation World, but it wasn’t just about him — there was also the aid team and the Ether Submachine. Therefore, Lu Yang had to resort to sailing back honestly.

"A month?!" Dean Gao’s jaw dropped, the speed was ridiculously fast.

"Can’t I stay out of there? Your boat can fit people, right?" Dean Gao pleaded sheepishly.

Dean Gao was itching badly; last time he boarded the Black Canopy Boat, the journey was too short and by the time he realized, they had already reached the destination. This time, he was determined to study it properly.

"That’s fine."

Dean Gao explained the situation to the aid team to calm them down.

Hearing about Lu Yang’s method, the scientists in the aid team also wished to witness it and wanted to be on the boat. But how could the boat possibly fit so many people? Lu Yang thought of a compromise and opened a window in the Green Blade Sword Small World so that the people inside could see the outside.

Only then did Dean Gao and Lu Yang come into the outside world.

The tower’s navigator called to Star Path: "Star Path, time is up, please leave the port."

"Repeat, Star Path, time is up, please leave the port."

His colleague, with a dazed expression, poked the navigator: "Stop repeating, where’s the Star Path?"

"What do you mean?"

The navigator looked up in confusion, only to see the enormous Star Path had vanished without a trace, leaving only a light boat heading into the vast darkness of space.

Chapter 1303: 1302: Welcome to the Cultivation World

An inconspicuous little boat is traveling at high speed in the universe, and it is hard to imagine that such a small boat actually carries hundreds of people.

The star domain ahead seems to have been compressed into a thin line; behind, the view is completely invisible; stars on the sides appear to be tilted toward the direction of the spacecraft’s movement. At this moment, it’s as if the black canopy boat has become the center of the universe, with everything revolving around it.

Dean Gao swallows hard, such a scene existed only in theory, which means the black canopy boat has almost reached the speed of light.

In the next moment, without any warning, the black canopy boat disappears and jumps to a position far ahead.

"Space jump technology..."

The last time Dean Gao took a ship from the Eternal Star System to the Sagittarius Star System, he arrived in the blink of an eye and did not see the operation of the black canopy boat. This time the journey is longer, and he can get a good look, yet he can only make out that the black canopy boat can achieve both light-speed travel and spatial jumping.

As for the underlying principles, he couldn’t understand them at all.

The spacecraft of the Interstellar Alliance could theoretically reach the speed of light, but due to material limitations, they could only remain theoretical.

Their most common method for long voyages is to construct artificial wormholes in various star systems and travel through wormholes.

After receiving permission from Lu Yang, two scientists ran out from the Green Blade Sword Small World, taking measurements with some unrecognizable instruments of data they couldn’t understand.

It was their first experience with light-speed flight; every set of data was priceless, and any set could suffice for a doctoral student to graduate several times over.

"May I ask, Mr. Lu, who built this spacecraft?"

"It was constructed by my ancestor and an elder. They just happened to make it recently, one was responsible for making the ship sturdy and the other for making it fly fast."

"Ancestor?" Dean Gao’s heart stirred, typically, someone who could be called an ancestor would be very old by now, and it would be unlikely for them to make any major scientific breakthroughs at such an age.

"How old is Mr. Lu’s ancestor?"

Lu Yang looks up, calculating: "Almost 100,000 years old, I’d say."

It’s true that Ancestor Guiyuan is from 100,000 years ago, but he has spent 80,000 of those years inside the Time Immortal’s little box. Spending one day inside the box meant outside a year would pass, so it’s hard to say exactly how old Ancestor Guiyuan is.

"Cough cough cough—"

Dean Gao suddenly started coughing fiercely, almost collapsing, frightening Lu Yang who quickly grabbed hold of him.

"Are you alright?"

Dean Gao could only cough, unable to speak.

100,000 years, what difference is there from being immortal?!

It’s a good thing Mr. Lu didn’t tell the Supreme Council everything about the Cultivation World. If they knew about this, they’d probably all want to move their families to the Cultivation World.

Does that mean Mr. Lu is also not as young as he appears?

The conversation between Lu Yang and Dean Gao was heard clearly by the support team staying in the Small World.

They looked bewildered, thinking they were experiencing auditory hallucinations due to not acclimatizing to the space environment.

It seems the Cultivation World is not as backward as described by the Alliance...

...

After more than twenty days of travel, the black canopy boat arrives near the Cultivation World.

The trip from the Cultivation World to the Interstellar Alliance took over a month, as Lu Yang was making stops along the way to draw a map of the universe. The return journey didn’t require this much trouble.

Dean Gao stares blankly at the scene before him.

Numerous boats similar to the black canopy boat are navigating the universe; many individuals, without relying on spacesuits and clad in ancient attire, are leisurely moving through space. They greet the people on the boats, drink, and make merry, presumably Cultivators like Lu Yang.

Whether it is those black canopy boats or the Cultivators, upon reaching a certain point in space, they vanish into thin air or new black canopy boats and Cultivators appear out of nowhere in that space.

It’s as if there is an invisible door there, connecting to another space, with people constantly passing through this door to traverse space.

"An artificial wormhole?"

This operation seems very much like an artificial wormhole, but its appearance is completely different from an artificial wormhole.

"Mr. Lu Yang, what is this?"

Lu Yang realizes that a spatial channel has been established, which wasn’t there when he left.

"This must be a passage constructed by another of my ancestors using the embryonic form of a Spatial Dao Fruit."

The Vast Sea Daoist Lord built many space passages near the Cultivation World, to facilitate cultivators in exploring the outside world.

"Space Dao what?"

"The Embryonic Spatial Dao Fruit, you could interpret it as his profound research into space."

Finally returning to the Cultivation World, Lu Yang’s tone even lifted a bit, as he urged the Black Canopy Boat to fly quickly.

Dean Gao vaguely saw something cubical in the distance and couldn’t help but wonder, whose Rubik’s cube was thrown here?

Yet after the Black Canopy Boat made several spatial jumps, the cube grew larger, and its true form entered into sight, casting a shadow over Dean Gao’s stunned face.

The rescue team living in the Small World also witnessed this scene, with expressions of disbelief, they murmured to themselves.

"My heavens, what is this thing?"

"How can such an astronomical object exist in the universe?!"

"A perfect cube; this is unscientific, unscientific indeed!"

At this moment, they forgot all they had learned, utterly astounded by the existence of the cube.

The Ether Submachine habitually calculated the size of the "cube," almost crashing when faced with the result. The knowledge it stored told it that such an object absolutely couldn’t exist in the universe.

But the fact was right there.

What is this cube?

Lu Yang stood at the bow, spreading his arms to introduce it to everyone.

"Ladies and gentlemen, welcome to the Cultivation World."

Is this the Cultivation World mentioned by Lu Yang?!

This completely overturned everyone’s understanding. Previously, they guessed the Cultivation World to be a planet, at most a larger planet with a high gravity coefficient to have nurtured a life form like Lu Yang.

But where is this a planet; any planet, no matter the size, would seem as tiny as dust in the face of the Cultivation World!

Lu Yang piloted the spacecraft to the entrance of the Cultivation World, where the box opened.

Dean Gao saw the planets moving at an astonishingly fast speed through the opening of the box, in complete defiance of natural laws, as planetary orbital speeds could not possibly be this fast.

But only when he got closer did he realize, it was Cultivators pushing the planets!

The rescue team exploded: "Am I seeing things, a single person altering the orbit of a planet?!"

"Is this what Cultivators are?"

"Could these few individuals be stronger than Mr. Lu, the strongest in the Cultivation World?"

"Look at Mr. Lu’s expression; they don’t seem like the strongest to me."

Lu Yang was also curious about these cultivators, especially since they were wearing official attire, "What are you all doing?"

The cultivators recognized Lu Yang and stopped their work, "Ah, it’s Lu Yang."

"The New Year is almost here, and the Dynasty decided to connect the stars to form a few shapes, like the Azure Dragon, Vermilion Bird, White Tiger, Xuan Wu and the like, to make it look nice."

"Who is this person behind you?"

The official cultivators of the Great Xia noticed the differently dressed Dean Gao.

"This is the chief scientist I specially invited from another civilization. Well-versed in both astronomy and geography, he knows a great deal and has come to help with the construction of our Cultivation World."

"So, it’s a grand scientist, welcome, welcome," several official Great Xia cultivators said with a smile.

Dean Gao’s mind wandered, with no response.

Chief scientist?

Knowledgeable in both astronomy and geography?

Understanding a great deal?

What do I know?

Chapter 1304: 1303: When the Local Natives Think You Are Pitiful

Lu Yang bid farewell to the few official cultivators from Great Xia and steered the small boat into the Cultivation World.

"Mr. Lu, you don’t seem surprised about those people moving planets?"

"Surprised? It’s quite unexpected to have such entertainment during the New Year, quite creative."

"No, I don’t mean that. I mean, are there many cultivators capable of moving planets?"

"Are there many...? Well, did you see anyone rowing boats or flying when we were coming here?"

"I did."

"Everyone you saw can move planets at will."

Dean Gao couldn’t help but take a sharp intake of breath.

The moment he entered the Cultivation World, Dean Gao couldn’t help but shiver a little, as if he had eaten a mint, his mind feeling clearer than usual.

Thinking of the mysterious energy often mentioned by Lu Yang, he asked, "Is this Spiritual Energy?"

Lu Yang laughed: "Do you feel the difference? If you had your original body, you’d feel even more comfortable."

The spaceship zoomed all the way and landed at the Seek Dao Sect’s mountain gate. Taking out the Green Peak Sword, he released the entire support team.

After a series of shocks, their initial arrogance was gone, replaced only by excitement.

"Is this the Cultivation World?"

"Finally, we’ve arrived."

"Can everyone here fly?"

"This is the Sect I belong to, the Seek Dao Sect." Lu Yang introduced to everyone with a hint of pride in his tone.

"We meet Elder Brother Lu!"

The two junior brothers guarding the mountain gate recognized Lu Yang and sounded a bit excited.

"These are guests I’ve invited, let them in for a bit."

"Yes."

The two junior brothers opened a door in the Sect Protection Array, gesturing for everyone to enter.

Passing through the mountain gate and walking past the bamboo forest, the serene bamboo forest seemed to be the perfect retreat for the reclusive; the river meandered and encircled the bamboo, nourishing it with spiritual water.

Mist rose from the river surface and gradually condensed into a human figure, blocking the way of the group, startling them - what kind of monster was this?

"Senior River Spirit, these are guests," Lu Yang said with a clasped hand greeting.

"Mmm, I heard what you said at the mountain gate." The River Spirit’s gaze fell on the Ether Submachine that Lu Yang was carrying.

"What is that thing you’re holding, I feel some connection to it?" The River Spirit said, sounding somewhat uncertain.

"What are you?!" The Ether Submachine was even more surprised than the River Spirit. Water vapor condensing into human form? What principle was that?

It also felt something strange, something indescribable, and something it had never felt before.

"This is something I brought from the outside world. It’s a Primordial Spirit Clone and might have some connection with you as both are probably Primordial Spirits, and there seems to be some resonance between you."

"A Primordial Spirit Clone, I see."

Primordial Spirits are extraordinarily rare, and the River Spirit had not seen another of its kind.

Of course, that also had to do with its inability to leave the riverbank.

In fact, most Primordial Spirits cannot leave their main bodies; the Ether Computer is an exception.

"Bring it over when you’re free, let’s have a chat," the River Spirit said with interest in the Ether Computer.

"Alright. By the way, Senior River Spirit, has Eldest Senior Sister and the others returned yet?"

"As far as I know, you’re the only one who has returned from this exploration."

"I’m the first one back?" Lu Yang was surprised; he had returned early, not having met anyone else.

He wanted to take Dean Gao and the others to meet Eldest Senior Sister to show how he spared no effort for the development of the Cultivation World and surely Sister Mengmeng would admire him greatly.

Now it seemed he had thought too highly of himself.

"Elder Brother Dai is here, right?"

"He is."

"That’s fine."

Since he was already back at the sect, it was time to show Elder Brother Dai that not every expedition of his resulted in a fiasco.

Dean Gao quietly asked only after they were far from the River Spirit, "Mr. Lu, who was that just now?"

"That was the River Spirit, a Guardian of our sect. Don’t be fooled by his fierce appearance; he’s actually quite nice. The first time I met him, there was a small misunderstanding, and he chased after me with an axe. As you can see, he doesn’t hold grudges against me anymore."

"A sect guardian? Is he strong?"

"You remember those cultivators from the moving planets? The River Spirit could handle them with just one hand."

Dean Gao took a sharp breath in surprise. The River Spirit was that powerful?

Doesn’t that mean the entire Interstellar Alliance is no match for him?

And this is just a part of the Cultivation World.

Wait, Mr. Lu mentioned that there are few in the Cultivation World more powerful than himself. Just how strong is Mr. Lu?

Dean Gao suddenly realized that what Lu Yang showed in the Interstellar Alliance was just the tip of the iceberg of his true strength!

Entering the core area of the Seek Dao Sect, the support team noticed the disciples fighting on the arena. A brief exchange between two disciples was like a cataclysm, with space itself trembling.

This wasn’t just a figure of speech; the space was literally shaking. What kind of powerful collision was this?!

The disciples of the Seek Dao Sect noticed Elder Brother Lu Yang’s support team, curiously examining their strange and foreign apparels. Out of etiquette, they didn’t stare, instead sneakily observing with their Spiritual Sense.

"Never seen these clothes before, did Senior Brother Lu bring these people from the outside world?"

"The structure of their bodies is so strange, they’re covered in metallic bumps from head to toe, only their heads are like ours."

"Could it be that demon cultivators have crafted puppets by stuffing human brains inside them? That way, the magic artifacts would have Spiritual Wisdom."

"Wow, how pitiful. They must have encountered demon cultivators. No wonder Senior Brother Lu brought them back, he’s looking for someone to save them."

Thinking this, the Disciples of the Seek Dao Sect cast sympathetic glances at the support team, which made them feel quite puzzled.

The disciples of the Seek Dao Sect were giving them the kind of look they were familiar with—it was the way they usually looked at indigenous people.

Not everyone could be brought before Dai Bufan, so Lu Yang asked Dean Gao to follow him into the Task Hall, while the rest waited outside for a while.

As more cultivators ventured out, and the volume of intelligence to be processed increased, Dai Bufan, who had previously enjoyed a leisurely life, once again became as busy as he was in the beginning.

"Elder Brother Dai here?"

Elder Brother Dai was startled by the voice, his heart instinctively jumping to his throat.

How did Lu Yang return? Did he cause more trouble?

"C’mon in."

Lu Yang entered with a well-behaved smile, holding the Ether Submachine, and ushered Dean Gao in.

"Elder Brother Dai, I’m back."

Dai Bufan looked at Dean Gao warily, sensing that the latter was just a mortal. But based on his experience, the man must be someone significant. "Who is this immortal?"

Lu Yang: "..."

What am I in your eyes, Elder Brother Dai?

"Elder Brother Dai, I encountered a power called the Interstellar Alliance during my travels. This is Dean Gao Huanyu, a guest I’ve invited."

Pausing, Lu Yang then added, "He’s not an immortal."

"Oh, a guest. Please, have a seat." Elder Brother Dai relaxed, his worries for nothing.

"So it seems you have quite the harvest this time?"

Lu Yang recounted everything he witnessed in the Interstellar Alliance, as well as inviting the support team back to help develop the Cultivation World.

"Good, good, good! Junior Brother Lu, you’ve done well!"

After hearing this, Dai Bufan’s eyes shone, repeating ’good’ three times and joyously slapped his thigh.

Phenomenal technology, and the Ether Computer laden with the entire Interstellar Alliance’s knowledge, not to mention talents in every field. Just by listening, Dai Bufan could imagine how prosperous the Cultivation World would become once it acquired all this!

Chapter 1305: 1304: It’s Normal for the Aid Team to be Aided

"Dean Gao, right? Welcome to our world," Dai Bufan said, grinning from ear to ear. Junior Brother Lu really has some skills, directly bringing over the most talented person from the Interstellar Alliance.

Now, they in the Cultivation World need talents like Dean Gao the most.

"Who would have thought that Dean Gao, at such a young age, could achieve so much? Incredible."

Dean Gao looked at Dai Bufan, who appeared to be in his early twenties, and fell into deep thought.

How long has this one been alive?

"According to what Junior Brother Lu just described, the knowledge reserve of the support team brought by Dean Gao is astounding. Combining this with the Hundred Skills of Cultivation is not something our Seek Dao Sect can handle alone!" Dai Bufan analyzed.

"I propose we inform Emperor Xia, Old Dragon Emperor, and others to mobilize the entire power of the Cultivation World."

"I’ll follow Elder Brother Dai’s lead," Lu Yang said. Consulting Elder Brother Dai and introducing Dean Gao to him also included this consideration.

...

"Have you encountered Demon Cultivators?" Several Disciples of the Seek Dao Sect couldn’t suppress their curiosity and actively approached the support team at the entrance of the Task Hall for a chat.

The support team had memory chips installed, and on the way here, Lu Yang had loaded the language of the Cultivation World into the memory chips, so they could understand what the Disciples of Seek Dao Sect were saying.

However, they didn’t understand the actual meaning behind the Disciples’ words.

"Demon, cultivator?"

"Isn’t your body modified by demons, full of metal scraps, just like a puppet?"

"Hey, isn’t that Senior Brother Li? Senior Brother Li, come over here and see, these people were all brought back by Junior Brother Lu from outside, and it seems like their bodies have been modified by demons."

Li Haoran happened to be passing by and came over when he heard someone calling him.

"Are there still Demon Cultivators these days?" Li Haoran was very surprised. Weren’t Demon Cultivators all turned into milestones by Man Gu?

Finally, the support team understood what Li Haoran and the others meant. One of them stepped forward to explain: "Our bodies have nothing to do with Demon Cultivators. These are mechanical bodies from the Alliance, and we voluntarily modified our bodies. It’s very convenient since any problematic part can simply be replaced."

Just as he was speaking, the man suddenly stiffened, lost his balance, and fell to the ground, startling everyone.

"What’s wrong, Old Ji?"

Old Ji was lying on the ground, and after several failed attempts to get up, said, "I don’t know what’s the matter, it seems like my body is not under my control."

"What does the self-diagnosis program say?"

"The self-diagnosis program reports an unknown fault and recommends returning to the factory for repairs."

"Return to the factory for repairs?!" This is the Cultivation World; how could it possibly return to the factory for repairs.

"Go find Dean Gao; he must have a solution."

"Make way, let me see what’s going on here." Li Haoran weaved through the crowd, crouched down, and examined Old Ji’s condition, a look of surprise on his face.

"Your mechanical body is having a rejection reaction with the Spiritual Energy. Haven’t you encountered Spiritual Energy before?"

As he spoke, Li Haoran pressed on Old Ji’s chest, extending countless fine lines and runes from his palm.

"All done."

Li Haoran stood up and dusted off his hands.

"All done?"

To onlookers, it seemed nothing more than Li Haoran giving Old Ji a pat.

Old Ji tried to get up and found that he could move again. The self-diagnosis program also reported no detection of applause.

He moved his arm, stunned.

"What’s wrong?" his companion asked, concerned.

"My arm, not just my arm, but my legs too..." Old Ji said, somewhat incoherently.

Suddenly, he sprang up forcefully, jumping over ten meters high!

The support team’s mouths fell open, and their heads bobbed up and down, following Old Ji’s jumping.

Old Ji’s body wasn’t outfitted with a bouncing function.

"Elder Ji, hold on a second, what’s going on here?"

"I don’t know, I could jump as soon as I got up."

Jump as soon as he got up? When Elder Ji was lying on the ground, what he touched was...

The rescue team looked towards Li Haoran together.

"I just crudely modified his ingenious body material slightly to let him adapt to the environment of the Cultivation World and did a bit of Refining in passing." Li Haoran initially didn’t want to explain, as this kind of thing was just a trivial act for him.

"Can we do the same as Elder Ji?" The rescue team’s eyes lit up; just by briefly touching Elder Ji, his body underwent a dramatic change; what a miraculous skill!

Li Haoran nodded, "Of course, the quality of the material of your mechanical bodies is good; they are just lacking spiritual energy guidance. Give me some time, and I can make you all fly."

"Fly?"

Hearing that they could fly, the rescue team clamored around Li Haoran competing to be first: "Me first, me first."

"I’ll give it a try for you guys."

...

"Dean Gao, this way, please."

Dai Bufan, Lu Yang, and Dean Gao walked out of the Task Hall together.

"So, it can fly without additional devices!"

Although the Interstellar Alliance has bodies capable of free flight, they are extremely costly, far beyond what the rescue team could afford.

Moreover, their bodily functions have been enhanced all around; flying is just one aspect.

It’s hard to believe that simply being touched by Li Haoran a few times could cause such drastic changes to the body.

"I can fly higher than you!"

"Then let’s see who flies higher!"

Dean Gao, seeing the rescue team flying all over the sky, fell into deep thought and remained speechless for a long time.

What happened during the time he was inside?

"Mr. Li, you must be the most incredible scientist in the Cultivation World."

"Mr. Li, such brilliant skills; even our Dean Gao can’t modify us to be like this."

Li Haoran, surrounded and praised by the rescue team, felt somewhat embarrassed; he decided to show the rescue team some real skills.

"Actually, your ingenious bodies can be further modified. If you ask me, keeping the brain is superfluous; just remove the brain and let only the soul attach to this ingenious body; it will be even more comfortable than now!"

The once lively rescue team suddenly fell silent upon hearing this; the method sounded a bit radical...

"Cough!" Dean Gao intentionally coughed loudly, and upon seeing Dean Gao emerge, the rescue team promptly stood in line properly.

"Since you are all guests here, just have fun," Senior Brother Dai tried to smooth things over.

"I apologize to Mr. Dai."

"Junior Brother Li has reached the Body Integration Realm, congratulations." Lu Yang noticed that Li Haoran had safely passed the Void Refinement Stage and become a Combined Great Ability.

Li Haoran clasped his hands together, utterly unwilling to accept Lu Yang’s kindness: "If you don’t boast about your cultivation level in front of me, I’ll be thankful to Heaven and Earth."

Lu Yang laughed heartily and patted Li Haoran’s shoulder: "Junior Brother Li, you’re being too modest. When have I ever boasted to you that I am a Semi-Immortal who can also use two types of Dao Fruit Outlines?"

Li Haoran: "..."

Dai Bufan called for a Flying Boat to take the rescue team and Lu Yang to the Imperial City.

On Great Snow Mountain, Qinghe, who was playing chess with Chasing Moon True Person, sensed something and turned to look towards Sky Gate Mountain.

"What’s the matter, Qing He?"

"It’s strange, I just sensed the aura of Lu Zhuguo?"

Chasing Moon True Person repeatedly shook his head: "Impossible, you must be overthinking it. If Lu Yang had returned to Seek Dao Sect, there’s no way he wouldn’t come to Sky Gate Mountain to find you."

"That’s right, let’s continue the game. Wait, isn’t the position of the chess pieces different from before?"

Chapter 1306: 1305 Dai Bufan: Praising Too Early

Great Xia, Imperial City, Imperial Palace.

Emperor Xia was reviewing memorials to the throne, with a legal reform proposal regarding cultivators venturing beyond the realm set to his right.

Besides the White Horse Dynasty, the cultivators had discovered several star systems inhabited by sentient beings, with developmental levels similar to that of the White Horse Dynasty.

Several unforeseen issues had arisen during the exploration, and there were gaps to be filled concerning these matters.

Three figures passed through the threshold of the Daming Hall in the sunlight, with Dai Bufan saying loudly, "Your Majesty, we have come to pay our respects."

Emperor Xia looked up, not surprised by Lu Yang’s arrival, as Dai Bufan had already informed him before their departure that Lu Yang was returning with significant gains, and representatives from other major forces would meet in the Daming Hall.

"Is this the person from beyond our domain that Lu Yang has invited back?"

Emperor Xia descended from the steps and ordered a seat to be given, while he noted the peculiar body structure of Dean Gao but did not ask in detail during their first meeting.

"I am Gao Huanyu, Chief Scientist and Dean of the Science Academy of the Interstellar Alliance. It is an honor to meet Your Majesty."

"So it is Daoist Gao, oh no, perhaps it would be more appropriate to address you as Dean Gao. It must have been tough travelling from afar."

"And me, I am called Ether," said the metal box in Lu Yang’s hand.

"And who might this be?" If not for Ether speaking, Emperor Xia would have barely realized it was alive.

"The Ether Computer of the Interstellar Alliance, also a Primordial Spirit," explained Lu Yang.

"Someone bring a chair for this Ether... Daoist Ether."

Soon, a eunuch brought over a chair and placed the Ether Submachine upon it.

"Hurry up and tell me, what exactly is the Interstellar Alliance like?" Emperor Xia noticed the extraordinarily complex structure of the Ether Submachine, far beyond the skill of ordinary people, yet this was paradoxically made by ordinary people, sparking his curiosity.

"It’s a bit difficult to describe directly, Your Majesty, please look here," said Dean Gao.

At that moment, the Ether Submachine projected a virtual star map into the air, displaying the entire universe explored by the Interstellar Alliance.

Dean Gao drew a circle, gesturing to a region: "These star systems are the range of our Interstellar Alliance, with a total of eight hundred Main Stars and three thousand auxiliary stars."

"Our capital is here, on the Eternal Star of the Eternal Star System."

"What are these?" Emperor Xia pointed to the small black dots that kept appearing and disappearing near the Eternal Star.

"Spacecraft, which allow us to navigate through space," Dean Gao indicated for the Ether Submachine to focus on the area where the spacecraft were most concentrated.

The map zoomed in, and soon the projection of the Space Port appeared before Emperor Xia, who viewed the massive structures with great respect for the Interstellar Alliance.

The merchant association could slightly modify the Flying Boats to transform into spacefaring vessels, but that was a method only cultivators could use.

He had heard that the Interstellar Alliance was all ordinary people, and ordinary people were actually capable of venturing into space, a task that must have taken the efforts of many people over dozens of generations.

Then the map’s focus shifted to the Eternal Star, zoomed in, and the streets of the Eternal Star were displayed.

The streets were busy with traffic, teeming with people; skyscrapers lined both sides, and colorful billboards stood on the rooftops, everything was bustling to the extreme, sights never before seen in the Cultivation World.

"Who is he talking to?" Emperor Xia pointed to a pedestrian on the map who seemed to be talking to himself while walking.

"He’s probably conducting business with someone from another star system. This uses quantum communication technology that disregards spatial distance for delay-free conversation," Dean Gao laughed, proud of the invention.

"What about this?" Emperor Xia pointed to the most frequently appearing large metal boxes, which were hanging in the air, similar to the Flying Cars invented by Lu Yang.

But the Interstellar Alliance certainly did not rely on Flying Swords for propulsion.

"Solar-powered vehicles."

"And this?"

"Artificial Intelligence nanny."

"And this?"

"Memory chip factory, fully operated by Artificial Intelligence."

"Ordinary people can live so luxuriously?!" The more Emperor Xia learned about the Interstellar Alliance, the shorter his breath became.

He stood up excitedly and began to pace around the Daming Hall.

The gains brought by Lu Yang were too significant, beyond his expectations.

And this was just the creation of ordinary people; if it could be combined with the Hundred Skills of Cultivation, who knew what heights could be reached.

"Establish the Heavenly Craft Department, we must gather all Artifact Refiners, Array Masters, and Engravers to form the Heavenly Craft Department!"

"Dean Gao, would you consider joining Great Xia’s Heavenly Craft Department?"

"What’s this about joining your Heavenly Craft Department? Let me hear it too." Zhu Tian walked into the Daming Hall with a casual demeanor.

"It seems I’m the first one here, huh?" Zhu Tian grinned, noticing Lu Yang, "Has Big Brother Lu Yang returned?"

He looked around and noticed an empty chair, took the Ether Submachine off it, and sat down himself.

"That’s quite thoughtful, reserving a chair for me."

"Hey hey hey, that’s my chair!" the Ether Submachine protested angrily, wishing it could jump up if not for its physical limitations.

"This little thing can talk, interesting." Zhu Tian chuckled, flicking its ’head’ and making the Ether Submachine spin several times.

"Mr. Lu, he’s bullying me, you have to stand up for me!" the Ether Submachine cried to Lu Yang, startling Zhu Tian.

What’s the background of this little thing, a Primordial Spirit possessed by a metal box?

Lu Yang reminded, "Nation Master Zhu, this is an Ether Computer, a guest who has come from the Interstellar Alliance."

"Oh, a guest, my apologies," Zhu Tian said with a grin and patted the Ether Submachine, "But it’s also your fault, why didn’t you say you were a guest earlier to avoid this?"

The Ether crazily searched its database, thinking about how to verbally counterattack Zhu Tian.

Then Old Dragon Emperor, Snow Emperor, and Ming Yu arrived at Daming Hall successively, and after understanding the situation with the Interstellar Alliance, they too realized it was a rare development opportunity.

Zhu Tian suddenly realized, somewhat indignant, "You Great Xia, where did you get a Heavenly Craft Department from, and even trying to recruit Dean Gao while we were away, Old Jiang, have you no shame, stealing people to your Great Xia first!"

Emperor Xia knew he was in the wrong and remained silent.

Then Zhu Tian quickly changed his tone and praised Lu Yang, "However, it really is thanks to Big Brother Lu Yang that he brought back so much from a single trip. Once digested, it’ll definitely lead to a qualitative leap in the Cultivation World!"

"Yes, yes, thanks to friend Lu Yang," Old Dragon Emperor and the others also generously praised, not just for the sake of Lu Yang’s face, but because they truly felt that way.

How many Cultivators have gone out to explore, and which among them brought back gains comparable to those of Lu Yang?

Dai Bufan chuckled to himself upon hearing this, Junior Brother Lu had finally brought him some pride.

"No good, the Alliance is in danger!"

Suddenly, the Ether Submachine shouted, causing the atmosphere to become serious.

"What happened? What’s going on with the Alliance!" Dean Gao’s expression turned grave, how could the Alliance be in danger?

But the Ether Submachine would not lie; it maintained synchronization with the mainframe through quantum communication technology.

The Ether Submachine then dropped a bombshell, "An unknown force has attacked the Alliance, incredibly powerful, the Alliance stands no chance!"

"Unknown force?"

Dean Gao looked incredulous. The Alliance had been developing, continuously absorbing surrounding civilizations, never encountering such a formidable enemy before. If not for the existence of the Cultivation World, he had always thought the Alliance was the most powerful force in the universe.

Yet now an unknown force had appeared, and the Alliance was completely outclassed.

The Ether Submachine projected the Alliance’s battle situation.

At that moment, the Alliance was a battlefield with incessant gunfire, engaged in fierce star wars. The unknown force’s spacecraft charged through, while Alliance warships crumbled upon contact, completely outmatched.

The Alliance was on the brink of extinction!

Lu Yang’s expression changed; why did the unknown force’s spacecraft look like it was from the Candle Sky Civilization?

Soon, the Ether Submachine confirmed Lu Yang’s speculation, "The unknown force has declared that they come from the Candle Sky Civilization, they are Cosmic Dominators, conquering in the name of Heavenly Emperor."

But right after that, the projection disappeared, and the Ether Submachine no longer responded.

"Dean Gao, what’s going on with the Ether?" Lu Yang asked.

Dean Gao furrowed his brows, "The battle on that end must be too intense, the Ether must have concentrated all its computation power to the mainframe."

Hearing this, Lu Yang slowly stood up, looking around at everyone, "Gentlemen, I believe we should assemble our forces and urgently provide aid to the Interstellar Alliance!"

"That’s right, I agree!" Zhu Tian responded immediately.

"I agree too!" Emperor Xia added right after.

"I agree."

"Agree."

The representatives of the five major forces did not hesitate and agreed to send troops to assist the Interstellar Alliance.

"Gentlemen..." Dean Gao was very touched, not expecting that they would propose to help the Alliance without any hesitation.

Ssszzz—

The Ether Submachine buzzed twice, restoring contact with the mainframe. Its tone was somewhat indescribable, as if hesitating whether or not it should share the news.

"That, uh, it seems, Mr. Lu, you don’t need to provide assistance to our Interstellar Alliance anymore."

"What do you mean?" Lu Yang was puzzled. Wasn’t the Alliance in imminent peril, so how come suddenly, it’s no longer in danger?

"The Candle Sky Civilization discovered the Top-grade Spirit Stones you gifted us and changed their mind. They said they’re coming to subjugate your Cultivation World instead."

Upon hearing this, Dai Bufan quickly clutched his chest and nearly passed out.

He thought this time Lu Yang had become more sensible upon his return and was quite pleased, praising him inwardly, but it turned out the biggest bombshell was waiting for him!

Lu Yang noticed Dai Bufan’s reaction and hurriedly turned to explain:

"Elder Brother Dai, listen to me, I had nothing to do with this, it was just out of politeness that I gave a few Top-grade Spirit Stones, it’s Candle Sky Civilization being too greedy..."

"Elder Brother Dai, wake up!"

Chapter 1307: 1306: The Difference Between Civilizations

Interstellar Alliance, Sun Chasing Belt.

The Sun Chasing Belt is the outermost perimeter of the Interstellar Alliance’s territory, and can also be considered the Alliance’s first line of defense, where the Sixth and Eighth Warship Armies of the Interstellar Alliance are stationed.

Today is Sunday, and the Warship Army, as usual, is holding a holiday celebration, with only a few people arranged to remain on duty in the watch room. Long stints of interstellar patrol can easily lead to fatigue, making it necessary to hold such events regularly.

Seeing their comrades singing and dancing on the monitor, the on-duty soldier yawned and complained to the person next to him, "Sometimes I wonder what’s the point of our daily patrols in this godforsaken space. Might as well have the Ether Computer take over our patrols."

"Forget it, you wouldn’t say that if you were out of a job."

The person replied nonchalantly, "If I’m out of a job, then I’m out of a job. I can live on government relief, can’t die from that."

Suddenly, a large red exclamation mark appeared on the big screen, and a jarring alarm sounded, "Attention, unknown signal source detected!"

"Attention, unknown signal source detected!"

The two duty soldiers exchanged puzzled looks. In this day and age, there are still unknown signal sources?

Could it be some unknown planet’s space probe that has floated here, with a message like "Let’s be friends" written on it?

But soon, the signal displayed on the radar dashed their fantasy.

The radar screen was plastered with dense signal sources, the numbers flickered and finally fixed at the number one thousand five hundred.

This meant that one thousand five hundred unknown objects were approaching the Sun Chasing Belt.

Their pupils constricted sharply, sensing that something was terribly wrong: "Quick, report to the commander!"

The holiday celebration was swiftly canceled, and the two Warship Armies entered an emergency battle-ready state. The commander brought out the space telescope and finally saw what the unknown signal sources looked like.

"What is... that?"

Rather than spacecraft, it might be more accurate to describe them as giant meatballs, their surfaces wriggling, like one thousand five hundred enormous caterpillars majestically traversing the starry sky.

The commander had seen many civilizations capable of interstellar travel, which eventually joined the Interstellar Alliance.

But none of them had spacecraft as bizarre as the ones before him.

Or is it that these aren’t spacecraft at all, but some kind of interstellar species?

"Report, life system detection has found a large number of life forms inside."

"A large number of life forms?"

"Activate all defense systems, stay alert," the commander ordered, his voice calm and resolute, yet unable to conceal his inner anxiety, "Attempt to establish communication again, we must confirm their intentions!"

Multiple attempts to communicate ended in failure, the unknown fleet seemed to completely ignore any signals sent by the commander, continuing their silent advance.

"Send unmanned ships to block their way, open the energy shields! And report this to the Councilman!"

"Yes!"

In no time at all, hundreds of unmanned ships opened their energy shields, interconnecting to form an energy net, erected to block the unknown fleet.

The commander was fully confident in the energy net; during military drills, he had tested the limits of the energy net, which could withstand the translational motion of a planet for a few minutes before disintegrating—you can imagine the power of the energy net.

However, this net crumbled at the mere touch of the unknown fleet, proving utterly inadequate as a barrier.

"How is this possible!" The soldiers were dumbfounded at the sight, it was utterly unbelievable.

The commander finally determined that the newcomers were hostile and decisively gave the order: "All units, attack!"

Laser guns, particle beams, and missiles shot at the enemy like rain, instantly illuminating the pitch-black space.

...

"Sixth Legion, respond immediately if you hear this!"

"Eighth Legion, respond immediately if you hear this!"

The Councilman kept calling out to the fleets to no avail, beads of sweat forming on his brow in desperation.

"Ether, bring up the Sun Chasing Belt’s surveillance for me!"

However, before Ether could comply, the screen lit up, and on the other end was not any commander.

"So, this is how you operate it, huh? Let me see, there we go."

A humanoid life form stood on the other side of the screen, dressed impeccably, looking quite distinguished.

"Who are you, and where are the Sixth and Eighth Legions!"

"The Sixth and Eighth Legions? Oh, you mean those not very friendly little toys that greeted me?" The humanoid life form spoke relaxedly, snapped his fingers, and the camera cut to space.

The ships from the Sixth and Eighth were being devoured by the writhing flesh walls of the unknown fleet, leaving only half of their hulls intact.

Seeing this, the Councilman’s head felt like it had been struck by a hammer, dizzy and throbbing.

How long had it been since he lost contact with the two fleets, and they had already been annihilated?

Just how powerful was the enemy?!

The camera switched again, this time showing the interior of the unknown fleet, which looked like the inside of a biological body, with the legion commanders devoured by the walls, leaving only their heads.

"Chong En!" the Councilman called out the Commander’s name.

The camera switched to the humanoid life form as a mocking smile curled at the corner of his mouth, "Don’t worry, we simply wish to extract the language of communication with you from his brain."

Only then did the Councilman realize that the humanoid life form had been speaking Alliance language all along.

"What exactly are you people!"

"Us? We are the Cosmic Dominator of the Candle Sky Civilization, under the command of Emperor Fengtian to subjugate all directions. It just so happens that we stumbled upon you."

"As for me, I am the Commander of the Biluo Sky Fleet, Shi Wumian."

"If the Interstellar Alliance is willing to submit to our Candle Sky Civilization and become our vassal, bowing in submission and regularly presenting tributes, you can be spared the scourge of war, how about it?"

The Councilman erupted in anger, "Daydreaming, the Interstellar Alliance will never bow before any power!"

Shi Wumian sighed, "Every conquest encounters the same rhetoric, but isn’t the end result always the same?"

He turned and ordered the Biluo Sky Fleet, "All units prepare, jump to the Eternal Star System!"

...

The Councilman’s expression was as grim as still water. The Candle Sky Civilization was both malevolent and extraordinarily powerful. Two entire Warship Armies of the Candle Sky Civilization could not last more than ten minutes. Even if all the remaining Warship Armies were called back, they would most likely still be no match for the Candle Sky Civilization.

The only plan for now was...

"Ether, all twelve Warship Armies of the Interstellar Alliance are under your control, do your utmost to stop the Candle Sky Civilization invasion!"

The Councilman had always distrusted Artificial Intelligence, feeling secure only when the army was in his own hands. But with the imminent threat, relying solely on his command was impossible to defeat the Candle Sky Civilization; only with Ether controlling the entire army would there be a chance to fight against the Candle Sky Civilization!

...

After the Biluo Sky Fleet jumped to the outskirts of the Eternal Star System, they were immediately met with military interception.

However, the strength of the Candle Sky Civilization was far beyond the Councilman’s expectations. They charged through the Eternal Star System unbridled, invulnerable to any attack, and completely unaffected.

In the face of absolute power, no tactics could bridge the gap.

Such a large-scale battle could not be concealed from the public, and the Ether Computer could not spare the computing power to manage the online discourse.

The people wailed, their once invincible fleet was no more than oversized ants before the Candle Sky Civilization.

"Maybe we should just surrender, being under the powerful Candle Sky Civilization wouldn’t be so bad."

"Yeah, surrendering doesn’t mean a loss, Candle Sky Civilization might even help the Alliance develop."

"Disgusting, spineless, without any bottom line."

"Traitors should die, the Alliance will never surrender!"

Although many people would rather die than surrender, there was an equally significant number advocating for surrender; in their view, this was a completely meaningless war.

"If we don’t surrender, what can we do? Is there anything left to fight with?"

"There is, don’t forget we still haven’t used our Star Annihilation Cannon yet."

"Yes, we still have the Star Annihilation Cannon."

The words "Star Annihilation Cannon" reignited confidence within the Interstellar Alliance. This was their ultimate weapon, capable of unleashing an energy beam powerful enough to destroy a planet in an instant, its sheer might terrifying to all.

Ether Computer lured the Candle Sky Civilization into an ambush and decided to employ the concealed Star Annihilation Cannon.

The stored Anti-Matter met with matter and annihilated rapidly, unleashing a huge crystalline blue energy mass. Under the binding of a magnetic field, the energy converged into a beam, aiming at the command ship where Shi Wumian was located.

As the energy beam of the Star Annihilation Cannon blasted forth, a dazzling brilliance enveloped the entire warship, as if a new star had been born in space.

Everyone held their breath, waiting for the outcome.

As the light gradually faded, the command ship still hovered stably in place, its hull breached with a large, charred hole.

But quickly, the hole sprouted flesh, sealing the breach.

Witnessing this, the Councilman felt as if he had fallen into an abyss, slumped in his chair, his gaze empty and utterly desperate.

It was over, all was lost; even the Star Annihilation Cannon was ineffective. What could the Interstellar Alliance possibly bring forth to combat the Candle Sky Civilization?

"Annihilation Star Weapon, huh? Well, it’s not surprising that the Interstellar Alliance has developed such a weapon, given their level of advancement."

Shi Wumian spoke coldly; they too possessed Annihilation Star Weapons, once considered ultimate weapons.

Since Lord Heavenly Emperor ascended to power, the Candle Sky Civilization had advanced further, and Annihilation Star Weapons were merely powerful weapons.

"Thank the Lord Heavenly Emperor."

If it had been the Candle Sky Civilization of the past, facing the Interstellar Alliance would mean victory, but not an easy one.

Zero casualties in the battle; such an outcome only occurs when there is a disparity between civilizations.

The Biluo Sky Fleet blockaded the Eternal Star System, with Shi Wumian piloting the command ship down to Eternal Star.

Suddenly, several incredibly bright spots appeared on the command ship’s Spirit Stone radar. Normally unflappable, Shi Wumian’s heart skipped a few beats upon seeing these points.

"Does this planet possess the Top-grade Spirit Stones that Lord Heavenly Emperor needs?!"

Chapter 1308: 1307: The End of Heavenly Emperor’s Good Days

Before each departure, the Ninth Layer Heaven Fleet not only carries the mission to campaign in all directions and propagate the greatness of Lord Heavenly Emperor, but also harbors a covert task—seeking Spirit Stones.

The Candle Sky Civilization had its roots in the mining of Spirit Stone veins, harnessing the Spiritual Energy within these stones to enhance organic bodies, thus creating formidable battleships and unrivaled Candle Sky Soldiers.

After the rule of Lord Heavenly Emperor over the Candle Sky Civilization, the utilization of Spirit Stones reached a whole new level!

It could be said that Spirit Stones are of utmost importance, both for Lord Heavenly Emperor and for the Candle Sky Civilization.

Although each battleship was equipped with a Spirit Stone detection radar, and they had been searching for a long time, they found nothing beyond the birthplace of Lord Heavenly Emperor.

At last, they had found Spirit Stones.

And not just any, but Top-grade Spirit Stones!

If fortunate enough to find a vein of Top-grade Spirit Stones, Lord Heavenly Emperor would surely reward them, and the Biluo Sky Army would inevitably become the premier force in the Ninth Layer Heaven!

Originally, Mr. Shi planned to meet with the Councilman to bring him into submission, but now with Top-grade Spirit Stones before him, everything else had to wait!

After disembarking, he rushed to the Science Academy laboratory at breakneck speed based on the radar directions, his personal guards struggling to keep up.

A few scientists were inspecting the energy contained within the Top-grade Spirit Stones using various instruments, completely unaware of the outside world, when Mr. Shi kicked open the heavy alloy doors, startling them.

Mr. Shi had no time to bother about the scientists’ reactions, his gaze intently fixed on the several Top-grade Spirit Stones under the glass cover, his breathing growing heavier.

There was no mistake, these were Top-grade Spirit Stones!

He smashed the glass cover with a punch and took the Top-grade Spirit Stones.

"Mr. Shi."

Mr. Shi’s guard, arriving belatedly and breathless from trying to keep pace, promptly caught up.

"I have a task for you, quickly investigate the origin of these Top-grade Spirit Stones."

"Yes!"

After speaking, Mr. Shi, holding the Top-grade Spirit Stones, returned to the command ship to wait for the news from his guard.

His personal guards were all exceptional Candle Sky Soldiers, remarkable in martial prowess and reliable in their duties.

"Mr. Shi, this is a list of defectors from the upper echelons of the Interstellar Alliance, please peruse."

"Put it over there."

"Sir, this is the territorial extent of the Interstellar Alliance."

"Put it there."

"Boss, this is about the technological advances of the Interstellar Alliance. I think what we need to focus on most are the Ether Computer and the quantum communication technology. Both technologies are unavailable to us, especially the quantum communication that allows real-time communication regardless of distance..."

"Put it over there."

The Biluo Sky Army had waged wars across numerous planets, and department heads each took charge of their tasks; according to regulations, they would submit the found intelligence to Mr. Shi, facilitating his strategy planning.

The Interstellar Alliance represented the highest level of civilization the Biluo Sky Army had encountered; normally, Mr. Shi would eagerly examine such intelligence upon receiving it.

But now, Mr. Shi only felt impatient; the Interstellar Alliance wasn’t going anywhere after all, these matters seemed trivial. What mattered most was the origin of these Top-grade Spirit Stones.

Finally, his guard returned with critical intelligence.

"Sir, we have clarified that these Top-grade Spirit Stones do not belong to the Interstellar Alliance, but come from a place called the Cultivation World."

"The Cultivation World?" Mr. Shi frowned, having never heard of this place.

"Explain in detail."

"It starts with a Cultivator named Lu Yang who accidentally entered a television program..."

Hearing this, Mr. Shi clasped his hands over his mouth, frowning in deep thought.

"You’re saying that the Cultivation World has a large amount of Top-grade Spirit Stones and is considerably backward, just stepping into the cosmos, and still requires assistance from the Interstellar Alliance?"

As the foremost Prodigy of this generation, he feels very gratified to see the younger ones so active.

"Loose Cultivator Gongsun Hai has successively challenged three Golden Core Level Secret Realms, leading each one. On the ancient battlefield’s arena, facing the successive challenges of eight peers, he used Qi to control his sword and overcame strong enemies, successfully defending his position, earning the nickname ’mini Lu Yang’."

"From the East Sea, Zhang Ding was born under the auspices of a descending Treasure Tripod, opened his Third Eye’s Immortal Bone, and acquired three types of Ancient Inheritances. He fought Kun Peng, defeated Dragon Sons, and since his debut, has never tasted defeat, praised by the world as possessing the talent of Lu Yang."

"To temper himself, Long Tianyang joined the Seduction Sect as the sole male disciple. Facing the temptation of Senior Sisters and Junior Sisters, he preserved his virginity, with Qi and Blood reversing, bleeding from seven orifices, finally made a breakthrough and advanced to the Nascent Soul Stage, earning the moniker ’Little Meng Jingzhou.’"

Lu Yang held his forehead, thinking that it was fine for them to make a name for themselves, but why did they have to rub off on him and Old Meng.

But these are all trivial matters, not worth his concern.

"This Zhao Po from the Corpse Chasing Sect is your friend, right?" Daoist Xiantian hands over a piece of intelligence.

"Zhao Po, he’s still in the Void Refinement Stage, isn’t he? What kind of news about him is worth reporting?" Lu Yang wonders, taking the intelligence to read.

"Corpse Chasing Sect’s Zhao Po, developed affection with the Protector Female Dry Corpse, and became Daoist couple... huh?" Lu Yang looks surprised, rubbing his eyes to make sure he’s not seeing things wrong.

Zhao Po, what’s the deal with you, kid? I handed over the Dry Corpse woman to you for this?

"Sword Tower’s Mingtai ascends to the Body Integration Realm, causing an extraordinary event with ten thousand swords paying homage. During the event, he confessed to the Sword Tower Supreme, and the two became Daoist couple?"

Lu Yang’s expression became increasingly odd. He remembered that Mingtai was the embodiment of the Sword Spirit of the Sword Tower Supreme, and after the Sword Tower Supreme and Liu Ningxuan fought, exhausting his soul power, he became Mingtai’s sword spirit, serving each other as master and servant.

Now you two aren’t pretending anymore, directly becoming Daoist couple, huh.

"How nice, to become Daoist couple." Fairy Eternity whispered into Lu Yang’s ear, sounding like a wraith, visible only to him. Neither Daoist Xiantian nor Gu Junye could see her.

"Yes, it can be considered that lovers finally got together." Lu Yang felt it made sense for Mingtai and the Sword Tower Supreme to become Daoist couple.

"The White Horse Dynasty and several other stars have all moved to the Cultivation World, quite the swift action." Lu Yang clicked his tongue in wonder. The intelligence also came with a Recording Orb, which recorded the process of the White Horse Dynasty’s relocation, performed by the joint effort of six Integration Period Cultivators.

If it were just a matter of relocating stars, one Integration Period Cultivator would suffice, but moving a star changes the environment on it, which could be akin to a world-ending calamity for mortals. It requires multiple Integration Period Cultivators to ensure no harm comes to mortals.

"Have all those cultivators who were out also been called back? Makes sense, with great enemies at hand, it’d be troublesome if they ran into the Candle Sky Civilization."

Among the gathered intelligence, there were also mentions of a few old friends of Daoist Buyu.

"Minister of Justice Xu Xin has also broken through to the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, that was quick." Lu Yang mused, his fate was somewhat intertwined with this Minister of Justice. During that time in the Imperial City when he was possessed by Fairy Eternity, he was captured by him.

Perhaps it was fate, or perhaps it was the tense atmosphere in the Cultivation World, but not just one, several cultivators at the peak of Integration were breaking through to the Transcendance Tribulation Stage.

"It’s merely a breakthrough in accordance with the trend. Why is Xuanyuan Sword Master making a big fuss over a trivial matter?" Gu Junye remained unflappable, rarely affected by anything that could impact his Daoist heart.

Even Daoist Xiantian couldn’t measure up to him; he always suspected that Gu Junye was the reincarnation of someone very important.

Lu Yang didn’t want to pay attention to Gu Junye, but with Gu Junye looking at him eagerly, the meaning in his eyes was clear, hoping that Lu Yang would play along.

Lu Yang, still with his head down, could feel Gu Junye’s burning gaze and had no choice but to go along with him by asking, "In accordance with the trend? How so?"

"Everything in the world follows the cycle of reincarnation. In ancient, far ancient, and ancient times, there were always Great World Struggles. Geniuses gathered, heroes competed for crossing, and the Heavenly Dao’s suppression loosened, making breakthroughs incredibly simple."

"Today is simply the recurrence of those Great World Struggles from those eras."

"Opening the Cultivation World is in accordance with the trend, a move to comply with heavenly fate. Although that so-called Cosmic Dominator is not a worry, the common people are ignorant of the Cultivation World’s strength."

"As all things divide into yin and yang, this event is also a good thing. The Cultivation World has been peaceful for too long, lacking external pressure. The emergence of the Candle Sky Civilization provides ample pressure, helping them achieve their breakthroughs."

Lu Yang’s eyelid twitched, Gu Junye’s set of theories was indeed quite intimidating.

In secret, a pair of dark eyes settled on Gu Junye.

The hidden mastermind watched Gu Junye with a mixture of surprise and doubt, trying to see through him.

Logically, there should be some influence from a previous life after reincarnation. Although what Gu Junye said wasn’t entirely correct, he did touch on some ancient secrets hidden within history, secrets that no one had told him about.

"Could it be that he is the reincarnation of the person I possessed?"

Chapter 1313: 1312: The Lame Trick

After finishing a day’s work, Gu Junye returned to his abode.

The dim candlelight flickered faintly, barely illuminating the entire abode. A pond was constructed in the center of it, where a pool of lotuses was planted. His calligraphy adorned the walls, imparting a primitive and imposing air. Next to it stood a bookshelf, displaying ancient tomes.

The entire abode was antique and elegant, reminiscent of styles from ancient times yet distinct, designed by Gu Junye himself.

He took off his shoes and stepped into the lotus pond, sitting cross-legged on a green lotus leaf. As the lotus bloomed, a soft halo radiated around him, making his presence enigmatic and profound.

This was Gu Junye’s daily routine for cultivation. Although the newspaper office was known as a holy land for worldly cultivation, merely delving into the mundane world was not enough; cultivation was also necessary.

One flower represents a world; one leaf, a state of Buddha. Gu Junye, sitting on the green lotus, closed his eyes. At this moment, he didn’t feel like he was sitting on a lotus, but rather dwelling at the supreme heights of myriad realms. Each flower, each drop of water represented a world; the myriad realms revolved around him. His fate and the cultivation world were closely intertwined, the stronger he became, the stronger the cultivation world would become.

An unheralded dark figure appeared in the abode, frowning at Gu Junye who was in the midst of cultivation.

The mastermind behind the scenes observed for a long time, still uncertain if Gu Junye was the person he was looking for.

He had barely escaped death by feigning it. Everything had settled down, the cultivation world had opened, and everyone was in harmony, joyful together. If he acted rashly, he might disturb this peace and tangle again with the Immortal Fairy and Yun Zhi, causing trouble.

He wasn’t fond of provoking issues. Had he known that dealing with the Immortal Fairy would bring so much trouble after her death, he would have chosen a different method to tackle her.

Thus, unless necessary, he would not take action.

But Gu Junye’s identity was still uncertain to him; this uncertainty was a vulnerability that needed his own investigation.

"Boy, do you know who I am?"

It was supposed to be only Gu Junye in the abode, but suddenly a stranger’s voice emerged. Anyone would panic in such a situation, only the degree would vary. However, Gu Junye remained unfazed.

He slowly opened his eyes, seeing the dark figure, whose identity was unknown, and said calmly:

"You’ve finally arrived."

The shadowy figure was instantly shocked. Was it really him?

Hearing the tone, had he recovered all his memories? How could this be possible given his current cultivation level? Could it be that the effect of the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype differed from his estimation?

Immediately turning his surprise into joy, it seemed Gu Junye’s identity was almost certainly confirmed. He could deal with it directly, but to be safe, further confirmation was needed.

"Do you know who I am?"

Gu Junye let out a light laugh, as if mocking the shadowy figure for asking a foolish question.

"You have your answer, don’t you?"

The shadowy figure’s breath hitched, Gu Junye had hit the nail on the head, Gu Junye’s composed demeanor was only possible for a reincarnator who had retrieved memories from a previous life.

He had actually already confirmed Gu Junye’s identity.

Gu Junye formed a lotus gesture with one hand, a mysterious smile hovering on his lips, akin to Buddha smiling at a flower: "I thought that by undergoing reincarnation, I could sever causality and start anew. But it seems the causality between us is too deep, and it cannot be severed even through cycles of reincarnation."

Gu Junye looked at the shadowy figure, sighing deeply: "Ah, so focused on causality, how can you advance further?"

The shadowy figure’s heartbeat slowed, astonished by Gu Junye’s words—it seemed he knew more than the shadowy figure had assumed.

As he spoke, did it imply he wanted to voluntarily sever ties and causality with him? "Do you mean you won’t expose my identity, and you do not mind that I possessed you?"

Gu Junye heard this and burst into laughter, tears streaming down: "Possessed? Merely trapped by the shell of my past life!"

"You have trapped yourself here, unable to extricate yourself and have already been punished; why should I bother with you anymore?"

The mastermind behind the scenes trembled, not expecting such an understanding of possession, and upon reflection, what Gu Junye said made a lot of sense.

Could it be that he, having undergone nine cycles of reincarnation, has reached an unknowable state of enlightenment?

If he were to put himself in Gu Junye’s shoes, with his position and cultivation level, could he remain as unflustered if roles were reversed?

The answer is no; his mindset entirely depends on his cultivation level.
