The mastermind behind the scenes felt deeply ashamed and couldn’t lift his head in front of Gu Junye.

In terms of cultivation level, he was many levels higher than Gu Junye, but when comparing mind states, he was inferior; Gu Junye was far beyond him.

The mastermind behind the scenes deeply envied Gu Junye’s state of mind, this state of boundless freedom and ease, unconstrained by cultivation level—isn’t this what he had been pursuing all his life?

How could he attain such a state of mind? Perhaps it requires acquiring the Recarnation Dao Fruit Prototype and undergoing nine lifetimes of reincarnation, achieving consummation of the Dao Fruit and developing the mind state naturally?

Does that mean he needs to die nine times?

Seeing the mastermind in deep thought, Gu Junye knew what he was contemplating: "If you are free, why not meet with Xuanyuan Sword Master? He is now blocked by the barrier from his past knowledge and refuses to stir up past memories. You had a good relationship with him in a past life; maybe your appearance could help complete his cultivation."

The mastermind was stunned; what did that mean?

"Hold on, who do you think I am?"

"Your beloved bestowed her heart to me and rejected your love. In a critical moment, she took a fatal blow for me and passed away unexpectedly. Although it was an accident no one wished for, you always felt the fault lay with me. You took advantage of my tribulation to launch a sneak attack and try to possess my weak body."

"Netherworld Venerable, I didn’t want to bring this up; why must you ask?"

The mastermind behind the scenes: "..."

No, who the hell is the Netherworld Venerable?!

What is all this about!

The mastermind behind the scenes realized he had been completely fooled by Gu Junye and was terribly tricked.

He stared at Gu Junye fiercely, emitting a ghastly aura, causing the entire abode space to tremble.

The mastermind gasped heavily, face twisted ferociously, debating whether or not to simply kill Gu Junye.

"Boy, you did well!" those five words squeezed through his teeth, as a deathly aura unprecedentedly neared Gu Junye.

While killing Gu Junye would be easy, doing so would also expose the fact that he was still alive, rendering all his efforts in vain.

His hands clenched tightly, creaking, and he ultimately decided to let Gu Junye go.

Now, he just wanted to slap himself; damn it, why did he have to come here and suffer? Wouldn’t it be better to stay by Lu Yang’s side and enjoy himself?

The mastermind couldn’t wait to leave this place; with a cold snort, he disappeared as if he had never been here.

Gu Junye still maintained his seated lotus position, his eyes puzzled and particularly confused.

"Strange, wasn’t I in the middle of cultivation, why did I stop and open my eyes?"

"That’s right, it must be because my mind state has yet to reach perfection; I need to continue my cultivation."

He closed his eyes again, quickly entering a meditative trance, immersing himself in a world contained within a single bloom.

Chapter 1314: 1313: The Fleet Descends, Peril Comes to the Cultivation World!

The Cultivation World had been in a state of war preparation for over half a year, and news of cultivators making breakthroughs followed in quick succession. "mini Lu Yangs" and talents comparable to Lu Yang were springing up everywhere, as if every genius could compete with the famous Lu Yang of the past in arm wrestling.

The so-called "Little Meng Jingzhous" were much fewer in number, as mimicking Meng Jingzhou was a daunting challenge—that every genius didn’t dream of spending a spring night with a Holy Daughter or a Demoness.

Initially, Lu Yang wanted to stop this negative trend, but given his previous fame and everyone taking him as their target, the bad influence was overwhelming and simply unstoppable.

Lu Yang could only sigh about this. Spending his days in the newspaper office reading intelligence reports, after some time, he automatically filtered out the words "mini Lu Yang."

This also reflected the vibrant and aspirational spirit of the current Cultivation World. Everyone was seizing this great opportunity and cultivating as if their lives depended on it.

If one could distinguish themselves in the upcoming battle against the Candle Sky Civilization, that would be perfect.

By now, enough events were unfolding in the Cultivation World every moment to fill the newspapers, and the weekly publications were no longer satisfying the needs of the populace.

Fortunately, Dean Gao’s rescue team had achieved a significant breakthrough in the integration of spiritual energy and technology, making it possible to mass-produce Quantum Communication Devices. However, the cost had not yet come down, so it was common for several households to pool their resources to purchase a single device.

Communication efficiency improved significantly, and it was only logical for newspapers to go from "Cultivation Weekly" to "Cultivation Daily."

"How strange, why haven’t the Candle Sky Civilization’s warships arrived yet?" Emperor Xia and others were puzzled. It had already been over half a year, and Lu Yang took less than a month to return to the Cultivation World from the Interstellar Alliance.

"Could they have encountered some mishap on the way?" Zhu Tian speculated while chatting with Emperor Xia using a Quantum Communication Device.

"Aren’t they supposed to be Cosmic Dominators? What kind of mishap could there be?"

...

The launch of "Cultivation Daily" meant publishing a newspaper every day, which significantly increased the workload. Lu Yang really couldn’t handle it and wanted to sneak away.

But after all, it was he who had told Daoist Xiantian and Gu Junye that he would handle the newspaper matters.

Once spoken, it was not easy to go back on his word. Lu Yang had no choice but to claim that as a Semi-Immortal, he had to take up the responsibility of guarding the Cultivation World and went to the entrance of the Cultivation World to seek some peace.

At the entrance to the Cultivation World, two layers of defense were established. The first line of defense was made up of Body Integration Realm Cultivators, and the second line was maintained by Pass Tribulation Cultivators, with regular rotations in place to prevent surprise attacks from the Candle Sky Civilization.

Pass Tribulation Cultivators were mostly of the older generation, and Lu Yang was not familiar with them, so he blended in with the team of Integration Period Cultivators—fortunately, he knew some people there.

...

Not far from the Cultivation World, space distorted and a fleet of flesh-like ships emerged through Wormholes.

Eager to reach the Cultivation World as soon as possible, Shi Wumian ordered the fleet to accelerate at all costs. In just under a year, they arrived at their destination.

The moment the spatial jump was complete, Spirit Stone detection radars blared incessantly, and the dial was filled with countless red dots.

Shi Wumian excitedly rubbed his hands together. To him, the sound was incredibly delightful—it meant that the Cultivator named Lu Yang hadn’t deceived the Interstellar Alliance, and the Cultivation World had an inexhaustible supply of Spirit Stones!

Even at Lord Heavenly Emperor’s place of origin, the radar hadn’t reacted so violently!

There might be too many Spirit Stones for all one thousand five hundred warships to carry!

He already envisioned returning fully loaded and the rewards Lord Heavenly Emperor would bestow upon him.

Shi Wumian forced himself to calm down, enlarged the radar dial and projected it onto a screen, so that all the commanders could see.

"You see, the Cultivation World possesses an immense amount of Spirit Stones, but the most numerous and highest quality ones are in these five locations."

Shi Wumian pointed with his baton to five unknown regions, where the red dots were densest and even turned purple.

"In a while, each of you will lead two hundred warships through Directional Wormholes to these five places to plunder the Spirit Stones—take as much as you can!"

"I will report each of your performances to His Majesty the Heavenly Emperor afterward."

In such an uncivilized place, there was no need for rules. Just plunder and that’s it.

The commanders were bright-eyed and excited, realizing that this was an excellent opportunity to establish their careers and compete for the title of Commander in the future.

This is Shi Wumian’s philosophy of using people; only when you give your subordinates benefits, will they be willing to risk their lives for you.

Assigning these five Commanders can maximize the merit they accrue.

Directional wormholes, a type of wormhole technology that even the Interstellar Alliance has yet to master. As long as the probe can determine the location, there’s no need to set up a signal receiver at the other end; directly constructing a wormhole can ensure arrival at the destination.

Five directional wormholes appeared in front of the fleet, and the Commanders led their warships into the wormholes. The wormholes closed, and the fleet vanished.

Shi Wumian’s fleet consisted of 1,500 warships, of which he dispatched 1,000, leaving him with 500.

It would be unbefitting for the Commander to personally rob others, what he needed to do was to wait for the results here.

After a while, he began to feel puzzled. According to the rules, the fleets must report back after passing through the wormhole, so why was there no movement from these five Commanders after passing through?

"Fleet One, respond."

"Fleet Two, respond."

"Fleet Three..."

"Fleet Four..."

"Fleet Five..."

The communicator made crackling sounds, but there was no response.

Shi Wumian frowned; was the unique terrain of the Cultivation World affecting the signal?

There were precedents of this before, with communications resuming normally at closer distances.

Shi Wumian did not like things being out of control. He ordered the remaining 500 warships to move closer to the Cultivation World.

...

Fleet One passed through the wormhole with radars nearly bursting at the seams, and the Commander was quite excited; this might be the place with the largest quantity of Spirit Stones out of the five locations.

"Report to the Commander, the area below seems to be an undeveloped remote mountain region, with sparse buildings in the mountains."

The Commander, hearing the report, looked downward. It was true; at a glance, the area was full of mountains with few signs of development. It seemed the people living here were quite primitive.

"Land."

A technician saluted and reported, "Report to the Commander, an abnormal energy field detected, initially suspected to be a natural field formed by the excessive and irregular distribution of local Spirit Stones. The fleet is unable to land."

The energy detection dial showed abnormal data, a situation that had never been encountered before.

The Commander frowned, trouble at a critical moment: "What’s the solution?"

"We could try neutralizing the field with the maximum power of the Energy Beam."

"Permission granted."

Boom—

An immaculate Energy Beam, long charged, descended from the sky and hit the Sect Protection Array of the Seek Dao Sect, causing slight ripples.

Chasing Moon True Person and Qinghe, who were drinking tea on the snowy mountain, along with River Spirit bathing in the river, all looked up simultaneously at the sky at the oddly shaped fleet, resembling caterpillars, feeling puzzled.

Hadn’t two defensive lines been set up at the entrance? How did this fleet get in?

And were they... attacking the Seek Dao Sect?

Inside Captive Peak, the prisoners hearing the commotion all witnessed this scene, dumbstruck.

Through the newspaper, they were informed about the affairs of the Candle Sky Civilization, but they never imagined that the Candle Sky Civilization would dare to attack the Seek Dao Sect.

Guan Shanhai couldn’t help it and burst into laughter.

Chapter 1315: 1314: Is This the Level of the Cosmic Dominator?

Not only were the prisoners of Captive Peak bewildered, but even the members of Seek Dao Sect did not expect the Candle Sky Civilization Fleet to come knocking on their doorstep.

Therefore, their first reaction was not intense emotions like anger, but confusion.

"Isn’t that the Candle Sky Civilization Fleet mentioned in the newspapers? How did they end up here?"

"Have I gone mad from practicing too hard? Why do I feel like they’re not that strong?" Disciples of the Seek Dao Sect discussed among themselves. This was not at all what they had heard.

"It must be a feint."

"Report to the Commander, the maximum power energy beam failed to neutralize the force field."

For some reason, the Commander was a bit restless and inexplicably irritable. The subordinate’s question sounded utterly foolish to him, and he ordered in a cold voice.

"I’m not blind. If one shot isn’t enough, then fire ten. If ten aren’t enough, fire a hundred. If that’s still not enough, mobilize the entire fleet’s beams. I refuse to believe that two hundred beams can’t break through this place!"

No one could stop him from seizing the Spirit Stones and claiming the credit!

"Yes, sir!" The subordinate saluted and then hurriedly contacted each captain to prepare their energy cannons.

Boom—

Ten maximum power energy beams were fired simultaneously, striking the Sect Protection Array of Seek Dao Sect, causing ten ripples, and that was all.

"They dare to attack us with this kind of level?" Elders of Seek Dao Sect were surprised, and even the Grand Elder rose from his coffin, ready to take action.

Are they underestimating the Seek Dao Sect that much?

"Don’t act, let me do it!"

"Why? Just because you’re the big brother, you won’t let your brothers have a chance?"

"The opponent might be hiding their abilities. It’s life-threatening for you to go. I am the Sect Leader, and I swear to defend the Sect with my life!"

Elders and Daoist Buyu vied with each other, competing for this opportunity.

Just as the Commander thought this attack also had no effect, he saw the river in the distant mountainous area reversing its flow upwards, forming a curtain of rain and enveloping the fleet.

"What’s happening?"

The fleet was in chaos. How could this river act like it’s alive, flying into the sky? What principle was this? They had never encountered such a situation in their expeditions against other civilizations.

"Look outside, there’s someone!"

"He seems to have weapons, two axes?"

River Spirit held a golden axe and a silver axe, his veins popping and his presence fierce as he flew up into the sky, surrounding the entire fleet.

The Seek Dao Sect was not unaccustomed to being attacked. Previously, the Master of All Laws from Great Yu had launched a sneak attack on the Seek Dao Sect, but such a weak attack was a first for him.

The Sect Protection Array was a part of River Spirit’s body. The fleet’s attacks were like urinating on him—it was a downright insult to him as the Protector of Seek Dao Sect!

"A flying native? Seems like he is one of the stronger ones among the natives, like that Lu Yang."

"Haha, coming here with two axes, do they really think such primitive weapons can deal with us?"

The Candle Sky Soldiers laughed heartily, reminded of the time when they were conquering a primitive planet and the natives threw spears at their warships wearing beast skins.

The fleet turned their cannons, energy beams gathered, and a shot was fired at River Spirit.

Swish—

A flash of golden light, the energy beam was split in two, and the warships along this straight line were cut neatly in half, the cross-sections smooth and flat, and the wreckage of the warships and Candle Sky Soldiers fell downwards like dumplings.

Dead silence over the fleet communication.

Their warships could only be damaged with a gap by Annihilation Star Weapons and were capable of self-repair, yet they were powerless against a mere single swing of an axe.

The Commander belatedly realized the situation and ordered loudly:

"Retreat! Retreat quickly!"

But when they tried to maneuver their warships to escape, they found the river reversing its current had encircled them, trapping them in a swamp that made it harder to move the more they struggled.

They were horrified. What kind of tactic was this?

"Came here and still want to leave!" River Spirit, holding the two axes, charged at the fleet, chopping it up with ease as if cutting vegetables.

Dean Gao watched this scene, mouth agape for a long time.

He had seen the Candle Sky Civilization Fleet before, and the twelve armies of the Interstellar Alliance were powerless against them.

But in the Cultivation World, the situation was completely reversed. River Spirit alone was crushing these two hundred warships!

The Ether Submachine showed a face full of envy. As a Primordial Spirit itself, could it also become this powerful?

"Calling Fleet Two, requesting support, the might of the Cultivation World natives far exceeds our expectations!"

"Calling Fleet Three, requesting support, the might of the Cultivation World natives far exceeds our expectations..." The commander called desperately for assistance from his colleagues. He didn’t care about the credit anymore; surviving was more important than anything else.

However, no one on the other end of the communication device responded. Instead, there were sounds of panic, screams, and even beastly roars.

The commander of Fleet One was dumbfounded. What was going on with the opponents?

Seeing the River Spirit display her mighty power, the Disciples of the Seek Dao Sect excitedly used their quantum communication devices to video call friends from all over, showing them the majestic side of the Seek Dao Sect.

Some contacted dragon sons and grandsons from the Dragon Palace to boast: "See that? What they call the Cosmic Dominator is nothing but chickens and dogs in front of our Seek Dao Sect."

"Eh, I was just about to call you. The fleet of the Candle Sky Civilization has also come to our Dragon Palace of the East Sea."

"Why did they go over to your place too?" the disciple of the Seek Dao Sect asked in confusion. The Candle Sky Civilization’s warships were so weak, yet they’re splitting their forces?

...

"Report, sir. Massive deposits of top-grade spirit stones have been detected beneath these waters!" a soldier reported to the commander of Fleet Two.

"Underwater veins? Where exactly?"

"Report, on the seabed surface."

"Drop a few shock bombs to induce seabed earthquakes. We’ll shake those spirit stone veins out."

Several high-yield shock bombs were dropped into the sea, sinking continuously, with the surroundings changing constantly. The light went from bright to dark, then from dark to bright again, eventually hitting the roof of the Dragon Palace.

Today was the celebration of the Old Dragon Emperor’s longevity, not a grand birthday, just a simple celebration, where dragon sons and grandsons came to offer their gifts.

With a room full of descendants who were all filial, the Old Dragon Emperor laughed heartily when he saw them.

"Come, let’s toast to the Father Emperor’s longevity!"

The dragon sons and grandsons raised their glasses, with harmonious joy all around.

Bang.

Accompanied by a muffled sound, the ground shook slightly, spilling a few drops of wine from the cups.

The Old Dragon Emperor frowned, somewhat displeased. An accident during the birthday celebration was not a good omen.

He had a premonition and looked up, sensing the presence of the Candle Sky Civilization Fleet.

Transforming into dragons, they soared into the sky.

Seeing this, the dragon sons and grandsons followed behind the Old Dragon Emperor.

The sea region trembled, and sea creatures like shrimp soldiers and crab generals kept away from the area, sensing the presence of the dragons.

The group of dragons ascended, and all spirits made way.

"Report, a severe fluctuation has been detected in the waters below. It appears to be a seabed earthquake caused by the shock bombs."

"Send two warships down to check the situation."

A mere seabed earthquake couldn’t impact their pride and joy - their warships.

However, as soon as the two warships submerged, a huge whirlpool emerged on the sea surface, accompanied by raging winds and a sudden change in weather.

A ferocious ancient dragon, thousands of feet long, clutched the two warships and leaped out of the water, causing thousand-feet-high waves.

Immediately after that, thousands of dragons took to the sea, surrounding the fleet completely, with dragon roars reverberating through the clouds.

The Candle Sky Civilization’s technological development was biased towards bioengineering. The physical forms of their fleet were actually very sturdy meat masses.

The Old Dragon Emperor clutched the two warships as if they were two pieces of meat.

"Drag... dragon!" The Candle Sky Soldiers stammered in fear, as the mythical creature appeared so abruptly before their eyes.

Dragon might suppressed most creatures, including the Candle Sky Soldiers. They felt the terrifying presence of the dragons, and every cell in their body trembled.

The Old Dragon Emperor held the largest warship, which was the command ship of Fleet Two.

There was a hint of puzzlement in the eyes of the Old Dragon Emperor. Is this the warship of the Cosmic Dominator? It doesn’t feel very strong.

The commander stood still, holding his breath, trying not to look at the giant dragon eyes outside the window, and pretending that there was no one on the command ship, hoping to avoid disaster.

Dragon might caused him to break out in cold sweat, and every part of his body was frantically warning him to flee the scene.

But his instincts told him that against a True Dragon, running away would be a sure path to death!

Suddenly, the walkie-talkie crackled: "Calling Fleet Two, requesting support, the might of the Cultivation World natives far exceeds our expectations!"

At that moment, the commander had the urge to kill.

The Old Dragon Emperor roared, the command ship trembled, and the commander could smell the storm of blood emanating from the Old Dragon Emperor’s mouth.

He was so scared that his eyes rolled back, and he passed out.

Chapter 1316: 1315: Say, where are your main forces!

Shadows appeared in the sky above the Great Xia Imperial City; they were formed by the third fleet.

The citizens in the city looked up in panic, astounded that the enemy had reached their doorstep!

If one were to name the three wealthiest places in the Imperial City, it would naturally be the Imperial Palace, the Land Money Trade Association, and the old residence of the Meng Family; the third fleet aimed for these places.

However, before the third fleet could take any action, the Imperial Guards, Chamber of Commerce Tributors, and Meng Family Elders, these three forces, made their move all at once.

The commander of the third fleet was jolted, "How can so many people fly?"

"Attack! Do not let them get close, everyone attack!"

Two hundred shots of maximum power energy beams were fired, hitting the cultivators of the three parties and instantaneously, dense smoke covered the area, with rolling dust and smoke.

Seeing these natives foolishly not avoiding the attack, the commander turned from shock to glee.

"Stupid natives, facing an unknown attack without even dodging!"

He somewhat regretted ordering a full attack; using energy beams on these primitives was too wasteful.

From within the smoke came the voice of the Imperial Guards: "Be careful, they might use smoke bombs to launch a surprise attack!"

As the smoke cleared, the three forces stood back to back forming a circle, wary of the fleet’s attack.

After all, the Candle Sky Civilization’s way of attacking was unknown; carefulness was never too much.

They summoned various weapons and used [Rules] to confine this area’s space.

Yet, as they waited a while, the real attack from the fleet never came.

It seemed that the other party only had smoke bombs?

They cautiously approached, discovering that like in a time stop, all the soldiers of the fleet were frozen in place; some with mouths agape, some fallen on the ground, some in mid-leap, but none could move.

"Do they not have a way to counter the power of [Rules]?" Meng Family Elders puzzled, as normally in Unification Cultivator battles, the use of Rules was customary to probe and discover each other’s depth.

Why did the other side become immobile just at the probing stage?

The Chamber of Commerce Tributors felt somewhat embarrassed; they day-to-day dined, drank, and cultivated all provided by the Commerce, all for acting at crucial moments to protect the Commerce.

They thought this surprise attack by the Candle Sky Civilization would be their chance to fully commit.

But who could have expected the Candle Sky Civilization was so weak, they even felt guilty taking the Commerce’s monthly provisions.

"What? Candle Sky Civilization can’t even deal with the power of [Rules]?" The events changed too fast, and the city’s citizens hadn’t snapped out of their panic yet.

Someone’s quantum communication device vibrated, a friend from the Western Paradise Temple was calling.

He answered the device, and Sha Mi greeted: "Benefactor, good deeds, let you see a big scene; Candle Sky Civilization’s warships have come here!"

People around heard this, gathering around and huddling to see the situation at Western Paradise Temple.

They saw Ming Yu Abbot displaying the Unmoving Ming King Dharma Image, his Dharma body radiated golden and thousands of feet tall, filled with the glow of Buddha Nature, compelling reverence and worship.

Ming Yu Abbot then displayed the Thousand-Armed Guanyin Dharma Image; the two Dharma Images merged, the Unmoving Ming King Dharma Image sprouted a thousand arms, one hand grasping a warship.

Two hundred warships were caught in the hands of Ming Yu Abbot, like children holding toys.

The fleet soldiers looked woodenly, kneeling on the ground, bowing and prostrating to the Unmoving Ming King, reciting the Unmovable Ming King Sutra.

Yet prior to this, they had never even heard of the Unmovable Ming King Sutra.

The commander of the fourth fleet was so scared he couldn’t even stand up; all their attacks landing on the Unmoving Ming King Dharma Image caused no harm, and the soldiers, no longer obeying commands, collectively converted to Buddhism.

"Calling the fifth fleet, we’ve encountered a monster here!"

Hiss—

On the other end of the communication device, the image of the fifth fleet appeared, the commander of the fifth fleet giving a grim smile: "Monsters? Are they more terrible than what we have here?"

Facing the fleet’s surprise attack, the Demon Emperor Zhu Tian decisively transformed into his original Di Jiang form, headless and tailless, his body entirely blood-red and round, with six wings on his back.

If Zhu Tian’s true form were only the size of a palm, anyone would praise its cuteness.

But the problem was that his true form was larger than mountains, his six wings unfolded could block out the sun, and the patterns on their surface were as wide as battleships.

Their attacks couldn’t even singe Zhu Tian’s hair.

One after another, Demon Kings and Demon Emperors took to the skies, transformed into their original forms, and demon energy steamed up above Demon City, bearing the scent of despair.

The citizens of Demon City cheered, praising Demon Emperor Zhu Tian for his wise and valiant leadership, which pleased him greatly.

Zhu Tian transformed back into human form, kicked open the fifth commander’s ship, and grabbed the commander by the collar, demanding harshly:

"Tell me, where are your main forces!"

The commander was bewildered by Zhu Tian’s question: "Main forces? We are the main forces!"

Zhu Tian would not be deceived by such a clumsy lie, he sneered and slapped the commander: "Still dare to lie to me, such a weak fleet dares to call itself the main force, you can’t even lie properly!"

"You must be a scouting troop, tell me, where are the main forces!"

The commander despaired: "We really are the main forces."

Seeing that the commander was so stubborn, Zhu Tian did not hold back and activated the Survival Of The Fittest Dao Fruit Outline, directly controlling the commander.

However, the commander’s answer remained the same, they were indeed the main force, and they were the elite troops of the Candle Sky Civilization.

Zhu Tian was somewhat perplexed and called Emperor Xia, the Old Dragon Emperor, Ming Yu, the Snow Emperor, and Lu Yang, the representatives of these great powers.

Beneath the Old Dragon Emperor’s dragon scales, vibration occurred—it was the quantum communication device ringing. He casually put down the command ship, and his dragon claws carefully touched the call button, afraid of breaking the device.

"This is the situation. The commander of the fleet insists they are the main force. According to my analysis, he might have been subjected to some kind of psychological suggestion or hypnosis, believing in his subjective judgment that they are the main force and thinking he is telling the truth."

Emperor Xia and others nodded in agreement, feeling that Zhu Tian’s analysis made sense; the Cosmic Dominator surely has more capabilities, and they should not be taken lightly.

The Snow Emperor, seeing the background of Zhu Tian and others—war-ravaged yet praised by the people as heroes—felt somewhat annoyed: "Why don’t the battleships come to our Extreme Northern Lands?"

Emperor Xia and Zhu Tian silently watched the Snow Emperor, even the gaze of Ming Yu was somewhat peculiar, their intentions unsaid.

Standing at the entrance to the Cultivation World, Lu Yang twitched upon hearing Zhu Tian’s analysis: "Is there the possibility that the Candle Sky Civilization isn’t as powerful as we imagined?"

He had been holding back this thought for a long time.

"It shouldn’t be, after all, their leader is called the Heavenly Emperor." Zhu Tian rarely disagreed with Lu Yang, thinking the title Heavenly Emperor held great significance, one he wouldn’t dare take for himself.

Lu Yang seemed to notice something, turning his head slightly.

"What’s the matter, Brother Lu?"

Lu Yang squinted, focusing his gaze on the boundless starry sky: "A lot of battleships are approaching from over there, seems like they have ill intentions."

Zhu Tian was quite excited: "That must be it, boldly attacking our Cultivation World, definitely their main forces!"

Approaching the Cultivation World later, communications finally returned to normal; Shi Wumian could contact his commanders.

"Mr. Shi, run, the natives here are monsters!"

"Help! Help!"

"Dragon! Dragon! Dragon!"

Communications were cut off intermittently, and it was unclear what was happening on the other end.

Shi Wumian finally realized how dangerous the Cultivation World was and urgently shouted: "Turn around, quick!"

Lu Yang showed Zhu Tian and the others the retreating Biluo Sky Army.

"It seems your main forces scared them off."

Chapter 1317: 1316: The Arrival of the Heavenly Emperor

As Shi Wumian approached and beheld the Cultivation World, a sense of foreboding emerged within him.

It was too large, the celestial body before his eyes was simply too immense—a perfect hexahedron, with stars like specks of dust next to the Cultivation World.

Such celestial bodies shouldn’t naturally exist in the universe; the Cultivation World completely defied his understanding.

When one after another, the calls for help began coming from the command ships, Shi Wumian didn’t hesitate any longer and ordered the remaining fleet to quickly flee the area.

This place was not the beautiful world littered with Spirit Stones but a Demon World teeming with monstrous demons and ghosts!

But how could the Integration Period Cultivators at the first line of defense possibly give Shi Wumian the chance to escape?

"Thinking of leaving after coming to our Cultivation World?!"

"Everyone stay behind!"

Seeing Shi Wumian’s fleet, the Integration Period Cultivators flew into a rage.

From an official standpoint, they had been stationed here for more than half a year, waiting for Shi Wumian’s attack. And when he did, he sent people directly into the heart of the Cultivation World through similar space spells. What was the point of their vigil then?!

On a personal level, their friends were showing off the prisoners and spoils of war they captured using Quantum Communication Devices, yet they who stood at the front line got nothing—not to mention that some among them were promoted to Integration Period during the wartime, all for the purpose of combating the Candle Sky Civilization and achieving great merit!

"Shrinking Ground Into Inches!"

"Heaven and Earth in an Inch!"

"Divine Foot!"

"Boat, come!"

"Senior Brother Lu, let me hitch a ride on your boat!"

The Great Cultivator of the Integration Period employed various techniques, with magic skills and magic artifacts entering the fray in unison. If Shi Wumian’s fleet escaped, they wouldn’t be able to show their faces again!

The Transcendance Tribulation Stage Cultivators at the second defense line chuckled when they saw this. Recalling their own youth, they were beyond the age for seeking fame. They’d let the younger generation handle these affairs.

"Open the wormhole, quick!" Shi Wumian was screaming in urgency.

The technicians, sweating profusely, replied: "Commander Shi, our ships haven’t locked their positions; we can’t open a wormhole."

"Then stop quickly and set up the wormhole right now!"

The fleet ceased moving; upon receiving the orders, five hundred warships resonated with subsonic waves, and a partially visible wormhole manifested.

"Faster, move it!" Shi Wumian noticed the Cultivators advancing almost as if they were teleporting, the distance shrinking rapidly.

"Reporting, the wormhole is fully constructed!"

"You won’t escape! Sky Flipping Seal!"

The radiant golden Sky Flipping Seal broke through the darkened universe, resplendent and full of Buddha Nature’s brilliance.

Just a second before the Integration Period Cultivators arrived, the battleships swarmed into the wormhole. The Sky Flipping Seal missed its target, and the mouth of the wormhole vanished into the vastness of the universe.

"We finally escaped." Shi Wumian had survived by the skin of his teeth, feeling bonelessly limp as if all his bones had been removed.

An alarm went off, startling Shi Wumian: "What’s going on?"

There was no need for his subordinates to answer—the alarm said it all: "Alert, alert, unknown spatial fluctuations detected."

The wormhole that had just closed reopened, and one after another, terrifying Cultivators traversing the stars with primitive weapons emerged in succession, rubbing their hands together with ferocious and excited expressions.

Shi Wumian’s eyes widened in shock—how could they have followed him?

Those versed in space technology among his crew were even more horrified than Shi Wumian. The more they knew about space technology, the more they understood the terrifying nature of these beings.

It was not a big deal that they could cross the stars with their physical bodies, but the problem was these people could damn well tear through wormholes!

Just what were these humanoid lifeforms, anyway?!

Their weapons were more primitive than the next: hammers, axes, swords—all present, with the techniques they could perform being even more defiant of nature.

"Fighting it out!" Enraged, Shi Wumian had never been in such dire straits before. They say don’t pursue a cornered enemy out of fear they might turn and bite.

Today, let these humanoid entities witness the terror of the Candle Sky Fleet!

The five thousand warships transferred to the Cultivation World were all standard warships, equipped with conventional firepower; his five hundred warships were the main force!

"Activate the plasma cutting warships!"

A red beam of light appeared between the two warships; this plasma cutting line is a brand new weapon developed after the Heavenly Emperor took over the Candle Sky Civilization.

The weapons of mass destruction previously controlled by Candle Sky Civilization were aimed at destroying Star Cores and the structure of planets, exploiting the gravitational collapse of planets. However, the plasma cutting line could directly split a planet in half.

The only downside is that energy consumption is too great, but at this point, there was no concern for energy consumption!

"What is that thing?" An Integration Period Cultivator took the initiative to attack, facing the assault of a plasma cutting line.

The incredibly thin red beam pierced through the cultivator, who felt nothing and was bewildered: "What kind of attack is this?"

Shi Wumian laughed loudly, seeing this, natives are natives, daring to face any attack with their strong bodies.

The person sliced by the plasma cutting line would have an exceptionally smooth surface, and initially, wouldn’t even realize their head had been separated from their body.

The cultivator turned his head and noticed the gaze of his companions: "Why are you all looking at me like that?"

A companion pointed at the cultivator’s head.

"Your hair has been shaved off."

"What?"

The cultivator touched his head and indeed, it was true.

It was as if someone took a pair of large scissors and heedlessly snipped at the hair around the cultivator’s neck.

"Damnation, you group of people from the outer realm, so maliciously daring to humiliate me like this, Dark Ghost Fire!"

The dark green ghost fire appeared out of nowhere, like specks of starlight.

Shi Wumian was perplexed; this was a vacuum environment, where did the flames come from!

Dark Ghost Fire attached to the warships, which in essence were like meatballs; as soon as they touched the ghost fire, they ignited, resembling ghost ships.

"Sixteen-Foot Golden Body."

Shi Chan of Suspension Temple leaped down from Lu Yang’s Black Canopy Boat, invoked the Sixteen-Foot Golden Body, and like bowling, toppled several warships in one go.

"Sealing Mountain Handprint!"

"Qiankun Stick!"

"Immobilization Talisman!"

Shi Wumian initially thought his fleet, fighting desperately, would at least go down with these natives in mutual destruction.

But after the first engagement, he understood why the ships from number one to five were wailing, telling him to flee immediately; these natives were monsters in human skin, impervious to any form of attack!

Plasma cutting line, subsonic wave attacks, nanomechanical clouds, gravitational wave bombs... all useless!

It was a completely one-sided situation; the cultivators were initially excited, but as the fight went on, they started to find it boring. These warships could easily be penetrated with a single poke, posing little effort. They even had to be overly cautious during the fight to avoid damaging the spoils and prisoners.

They prepared for battle for half a year, and this was the caliber of their opponent?

Shi Chan forcefully tore open the flagship, intending to capture Shi Wumian alive.

Suddenly, a beam of golden light approached from afar, a golden divine being bathed in holy radiance appeared in the middle of the battlefield, and neither Shi Chan nor the others saw how this person came.

The battle atmosphere was eerie, and both sides tacitly ceased fire.

The golden divine being didn’t deliberately exude any aura, yet his every move showcased endless power, proclaiming his identity as the Master of the Universe to everyone.

When the Heavenly Emperor saw the situation on the battlefield, he shook his head privately; it was a step too late after all, the Biluo Sky Army had already started a war with the Cultivation World. But it wasn’t too late.

He was just about to speak and explain, when unexpectedly, Shi Wumian rejoiced, "His Majesty the Heavenly Emperor joins the battle personally, surrender quickly!"

The remaining warships prepared to surrender saw the Heavenly Emperor’s arrival and reignited their will to fight, launching a new round of attacks, while praising "His Majesty the Heavenly Emperor joins the battle personally" as they attacked.

Heavenly Emperor: "?"

Shi Wumian, I should have come earlier to put an end to you!

Chapter 1318: 1317: What Should I Fear When Even the Immortals Are Not Present

"Dare to launch a sneak attack, kill them!" The Cultivators were enraged.

They had been overwhelmed by the presence of the Heavenly Emperor when he entered the scene and stopped attacking, and the Biluo Sky Army had ceased their assault as well.

Who would have thought that after the Biluo Sky Army stopped, they would dare to sneak attack? It was utterly shameless.

"Cease all hostilities." The Heavenly Emperor reprimanded softly, his position in the Candle Sky Civilization akin to the heavens, possessing the highest supremacy. His word could determine life and death, and his command was the absolute iron law, none dared to defy.

The Biluo Sky Army ceased their attack once again.

The Heavenly Emperor’s gaze swept over the Integration Period Cultivators. Not completely sure before, but now, upon seeing these juniors, he was certain; the ’Cultivation World’ referred to the ’Box World’.

So it seemed, the Box World had indeed been opened.

"I am the Heavenly Emperor, the Sovereign of Candle Sky, there has been some misunderstanding. Where are the Immortals? I will explain in person." The Heavenly Emperor looked indifferently at the group of Integration Period Cultivators, his worry was provoking the Immortals, as for these cultivators, he did not even consider them worth his attention.

Back in the Great Yu Era, Integration Period Cultivators were not even qualified to see him.

He is the Heavenly Emperor?!

Upon learning the identity of the other party, the Integration Period Cultivators showed expressions of surprise; they found that they couldn’t see through the other party at all.

Wait, he can speak the language of the Cultivation World?

Seeing the Heavenly Emperor surprised Elder Brother Lu Yang even more.

Could he be Emperor Wu Yao of Yu?

Lu Yang’s original situation before being switched was quite dire, and he worried that after Emperor Wu Yao switched places with him, it might be dangerous.

It seems he was worried for nothing; Wu Yao was thriving in the outside world, even becoming the Cosmic Dominator.

A newly advanced Integration Period Cultivator, dissatisfied, flew out from the crowd, "What kind of nonsense misunderstanding? Your Candle Sky Civilization led an army to our Cultivation World to plunder Spirit Stones!"

Had it not been for the strength of their Cultivation World, they would have been looted clean by the Candle Sky Civilization long ago!

The brows of Wu Yao furrowed slightly in displeasure; a mere junior at the Integration Period, not even with a firm foundation, dares to bawl in his presence?

With a flick of his finger, the newly advanced Integration Period Cultivator didn’t even get a chance to react and was sent flying directly.

"Utterly ignorant of the enormity of the heavens and earth, how did your elders teach you?"

The crowd was shocked; they did not see how Wu Yao had made his move. As they attempted to catch their flying companion, they too were repelled and sent flying.

Elder Brother Lu Yang caught the person and flew to the front, confronting Wu Yao directly, "Elder, is it not excessive to deal so sternly with an ignorant junior?"

Wu Yao let out a cold laugh, "A Middle Tribulation Stage dares to stand up for others, have your elders come to see me."

Elder Brother Lu Yang held a high position in the hearts of everyone; hearing Wu Yao insult him, they collectively spoke out in reprimand, with several even wanting to take direct action.

Elder Brother Lu Yang raised his hand to signal everyone to hold back, continuing, "Currently, the Immortals are wandering in all directions, unknowable when they will return. My name is Lu Yang, and I can represent the Cultivation World; elder, you can explain this misunderstanding to me."

Something stirred in Wu Yao’s heart; hearing this, could it be that there were no Immortals currently in the Cultivation World?

It made sense, even as an Immortal he desired to leave the Cultivation World to see the outside, the other Immortals likely shared similar thoughts. Once the seal on the Cultivation World was broken, they all left.

"Explain? Fine, but first release my army." Wu Yao’s tone carried a command as he spoke these words.

Since the Immortal was not present, he did not need to hold any reservations, and there was no need to be overly polite with these youngsters. First, retrieve his subordinates, then worry about the rest later.

Otherwise, explaining himself to those who captured his man, as if he was inferior, would be utterly disgraceful.

Even if the Immortal were to return later, it’s unlikely they would fuss over such trifle matters.

Moreover, he had left some tactics in the Cultivation World; should he return there, he could rely on these tactics to contend with the Immortals. The Semi-Immortals of the Cultivation World meant nothing to him.

Lu Yang’s brow furrowed slightly. Considering Wu Yao had indirectly saved him, his demands couldn’t possibly be agreed upon, making Lu Yang’s tone much heavier.

"Senior’s demands are somewhat unreasonable. Your men attacked the Cultivation World; if we hadn’t had some skill, we might have become prisoners at your feet. Now that they’re in our hands, naturally, we will deal with them."

"Unreasonable? I afforded you a modicum of respect, do you truly think you have the right to refuse?"

Wu Yao’s lips curled into a cold smile as he reached out with a sky-obscuring hand to take away the entire Biluo Sky Army.

The hand imprint was as large as the stars, capable of smashing the Star Cores with a snap of the fingers, imbued with the power to bind. The Cultivators were shocked, paralyzed as if a Fixation Technique had been cast on them, watching helplessly as Wu Yao took away the Candle Sky Soldiers!

Boom—

Lu Yang executed the Buddha Land in Palm, clashing with Wu Yao’s sky-obscuring hand imprint.

The two giant handprints canceled each other out, and only then could the Cultivators break free from their imprisonment.

"Transcendance Tribulation Peak?" Lu Yang quickly assessed Wu Yao’s realm. When their handprints collided, he found himself at a disadvantage—not from a lack of skill, but from a gap in cultivation level.

As an Immortal restarting cultivation, Wu Yao’s foundation was so solid he could challenge beyond his level, among those of the Pass Tribulation Peak, he was incredibly strong. That Lu Yang’s handprint hadn’t instantly crumbled had surprised Wu Yao.

"You do have some skill, no wonder you claim to represent the Cultivation World."

Despite his words, Wu Yao still didn’t consider Lu Yang a threat—in terms of cultivation level, status, or vision, he towered over Lu Yang.

"But a young man who doesn’t know the immensity of heaven and earth needs to be taught a lesson!"

With a thought from Wu Yao, the trajectory of the stars shifted and, in an instant, were refined into nodes of an Array Method, forming an unknown formation that enveloped everyone.

A gravity thousands of times heavy instantly compressed their bodies, and it increased exponentially. Magic artifacts were flattened into useless thin discs, and the expressions of those who saw this drastically changed as they hurriedly fled from the Great Formation. Only Lu Yang did not run away.

Wu Yao had set up the Star Array specifically to deal with him. If he fled, the Star Array would follow.

"Fall." Wu Yao said softly.

The refined stars shrunk tenfold in size, yet their hardness increased a hundredfold, tough enough that even those in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage would find it hard to shatter.

The stars fell like rain; with both hands, Lu Yang propped up a star, but more stars continued to fall, shattering the previous ones and targeting Lu Yang.

Soon, Lu Yang was overwhelmed by successive falling stars, his life or death unknown.

"Condense." Wu Yao drove the formation, the gaps between the stars engulfing Lu Yang emanated green light, followed by the closing of the gaps, disappearing out of sight. The stars became one entity, sealing Lu Yang within.

The Integration Period Cultivators watching the battle became incensed, "Elder Brother Lu Yang!"

The Biluo Sky Army was fervently excited, and although they had witnessed the power of the Heavenly Emperor countless times, they were still astonished.

How potent these native powers of the Cultivation World were, yet they still were no match for Lord Heavenly Emperor.

"Hm?" Wu Yao sensed a tremendous force emerging from within the star seal, fiercely assaulting the seal.

The seal trembled, and the accumulated stars burst into powder with a bang. The heavens shattered, the earth split, Lu Yang, with Arhat Fist, forcefully shattered the star seal. His clothes were slightly dirtied, but he was unharmed.

"I’ve endured you for quite some time now, and you were the one who struck first!" Lu Yang said angrily. Since Wu Yao wasn’t giving any face, there was no need for Lu Yang to show him any either!

Chapter 1319: 1318: Lu Yang vs. Wu Yao

"Pass Tribulation Peak?" Wu Yao was quite surprised; Lu Yang’s realm had suddenly surged, elevating him to the Pass Tribulation Peak, and without the slightest instability in his foundation.

Such a brilliant secret technique, is it similar to the Sky Demon Disintegration Technique?

However, these are all trifles of cleverness. Even the most ingenious secret technique has its duration and side effects. The backlash from a technique that stabilizes and improves one’s realm will only be more severe.

Ultimately, this is just a temporary measure, one that won’t last long.

"How does striking first change anything, thinking that reaching Pass Tribulation Peak allows you to stand as my equal?" In Wu Yao’s view, Lu Yang was merely in a futile rage, not worthy of consideration.

Boom—

Both took action at the same time; Wu Yao had intended to take the initiative, but to his surprise, Lu Yang shared the same thought, and the two clashed amidst the stars, their figures blurring, seeming like thousands of illusory shadows dueling in this place.

Their auras turned into star systems, vast and colliding continuously, displaying unmatched divine might. The stars trembled, and the cultivators and warships, like fish in the tide, could only drift along, unable to control themselves.

Shi Wumian was shocked. Since the Heavenly Emperor’s reign began, he had never encountered an opponent worthy of his second move. Even the terrifying power displayed by the Cultivation World paled in comparison to the Heavenly Emperor; yet now, the Heavenly Emperor had actually met an equal.

Shi Chan and others were also astonished; they were fortunate to have witnessed a battle of Pass Tribulation Peak powerhouses, but the impact of this scene was unparalleled.

A battle between Semi-Immortals could go no further than this.

"Quickly contact the elders!" Shi Chan said, now was not the time for admiration; the priority was to call for help.

Those in the second line of defense at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage received the distress signal, but they couldn’t determine their position.

Integration Period Cultivators could tear open wormholes as they had chased closely behind the fleet that had just left; they could find the wormhole’s entrance by following the residual spatial fluctuations. But after so much time, the leftover spatial fluctuations had long since vanished, and the stars that were used for positioning had changed their trajectories in this battle.

They could not find the way and could only watch the battle through quantum communication devices, gasping in amazement.

"This is a battle at the Pass Tribulation Stage?!"

"Every aspect has been honed to the extreme."

"The state of consummation that Immortals talk about before achieving their way probably looks something like this."

"Heavenly Gang Emperor Fist!"

"Arhat Fist!"

Behind the two of them, the Heavenly Emperor’s and Arhat’s illusory figures vaguely appeared, with the Heavenly Emperor holding the sun, moon, and stars in his palm, and the Arhat chanting the Original Buddhist Scripture.

Then Wu Yao found his hair falling out in clumps.

"Greetings, Bald Heavenly Emperor." Lu Yang bowed respectfully.

"You’re courting death!"

Wu Yao, utterly furious, had always valued his appearance, and becoming bald was more unbearable than being killed, especially with subordinates watching here, and even the Cultivation World witnessing this scene through the communication device!

Wu Yao’s hair danced wildly, and astonishingly, it grew back, to Lu Yang’s great surprise.

His Arhat Fist was enhanced by the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype, so regrowing hair could only be countered using a Dao Fruit Prototype.

Meaning, Wu Yao definitely possessed a Dao Fruit Prototype.

Lu Yang wasn’t surprised that Wu Yao had a Dao Fruit Prototype. Who was Wu Yao? An Immortal reincarnated, proficient in the Hundred Skills of Cultivation, his life legendary and rich in experience; it would have been surprising if he hadn’t formed a Dao Fruit Prototype.

However, Lu Yang still wasn’t sure of the effects of Wu Yao’s Dao Fruit Prototype.

Surely it’s not just about having long hair, right?

"Little Yang fought well, just keep hitting him like that!" Fairy Eternity cheered for Lu Yang in the Spiritual Space, waving her flag and throwing two punches into the void. Wu Yao’s disrespectful words towards Little Yang had long irritated her.

He deserved it.

Fairy Eternity’s attire was even more revealing than usual, not resembling the style of the Cultivation World but more like that of a cheerleader from the Interstellar Alliance.

While Lu Yang was learning about the basic situation of the Alliance in the library of the Interstellar Alliance, Fairy Eternity wasn’t idle either; she chose to read magazines with fewer words and more pictures.

It wasn’t clear whether it was Fairy Eternity’s cheering that was effective, or if Lu Yang was just riding the high of battle, but his fighting became more fluid and smooth.

"Refine!"

Wu Yao summoned the stars from the sky, using the Artifact Refining Technique to refine them into Immortal Treasures and revisited his old trick by constructing a double-layered Gravity Great Formation with the stars as nodes, trapping Lu Yang in the middle, immobilized. Then the stars fell in layers, suppressing Lu Yang once again.

Being able to naturally integrate Artifact Refining Technique with Array Methods was a rare talent in the world.

Wu Yao had already figured out the limit of Lu Yang’s fist’s power; to exchange blows again would only damage his image. It was better to suppress Lu Yang with Array Methods.

The Stars Gravity Array was even more potent than the previous one and would certainly not allow breaking through with Arhat Fist.

"Be careful!" Those senior cultivators at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage couldn’t help but shout when they saw this scene. This move was extremely clever; probably only those who were experts in both Array Methods and Artifact Refining, or purely Body Cultivators, could break this seal.

And Lu Yang was neither.

However, Lu Yang seemed to be unaffected by the seal, as he swam through the Stars seal freely like an earth spirit born from the soil, naturally affiliated with the earth.

Wu Yao frowned, not understanding how Lu Yang was doing it.

The magic skill "Earth Shrinking" allowed one to move freely through soil, untrapped by it.

The Golden Crow’s call echoed through the galaxy, and traces of True Fire appeared on the surface of the seal, conjuring a golden bird’s Illusory Shadow above, which landed on Lu Yang’s shoulder, resembling the extinct Golden Crow.

The golden bird split from one to two, and two to four, in the blink of an eye, forming a complete firebird, making an airtight net of fire, melting the stars wherever it went.

"Three Thousand Weak Water." Faced with True Fire, Wu Yao raised his hand to summon the Ruishui River, with roaring waves transforming into dragon shapes, pouncing towards the golden bird.

The dragon-shaped Ruishui River was sliced into countless droplets of water by the fire net, scattering across the universe, merely slowing the firebird’s flight speed slightly.

Wu Yao was startled, the power of the golden bird’s flame was beyond his expectations. By the time he realized this, the golden flames engulfed the sky, swallowing him, burning his skin and flesh.

Wu Yao shed his outer shell like Jin Chan, leaving behind his skin to escape from death, and fixed his gaze on Lu Yang, no longer underestimating him.

"It seems I have underestimated you. Are you a Semi-Immortal who has condensed the Yin-Yang Dao Fruit Prototype?"

As these words were spoken, everyone was shocked, staring at Lu Yang in disbelief.

In their eyes, Lu Yang was a peerless prodigy who had cultivated to the Middle Tribulation Stage and had methods to temporarily elevate himself to the Pass Tribulation Peak.

But no one had thought that he had quietly condensed a Dao Fruit Prototype?

How many cultivators at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage were stuck at the Dao Fruit Prototype, languishing their whole lives? With the talent Lu Yang demonstrated, everyone believed he would become a Semi-Immortal, but they could never have imagined he had already become one!

Those elders in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage felt that compared to Lu Yang, they had wasted thousands of years in cultivation!

Lu Yang stared at Wu Yao, a hint of a smile on his lips: "I never thought that the senior would discover my deliberate concealment of my Semi-Immortal identity. You have good insight, senior."

Since he had been revealed, Lu Yang simply and generously admitted: "Indeed, I am a Semi-Immortal who has condensed the Yin-Yang Dao Fruit!"

Chapter 1320: 1319: Why Does Your Sword Look So Familiar?

Upon witnessing the magical combat between Lu Yang and Wu Yao, Dean Gao realized the terrifying extent of Lu Yang’s power.

Initially, he thought Lu Yang’s supernatural acts displayed within the Interstellar Alliance were already defying the heavens. But now, a mere collision could deviate the stars, a few moves could annihilate entire star systems. Even to describe these as defying the heavens seemed somewhat inappropriate.

And this was only as a Semi-Immortal. What level of power would a true Immortal possess?

Dean Gao merely imagined an Immortal Battle briefly, and he shivered uncontrollably.

Before, he had only heard that when cultivators refined their skills to the utmost, they could rewrite the cosmic rules, but this concept was too vague and immense for Dean Gao to grasp concretely.

Only today did he realize the terrifying nature of the Cultivation World.

Could these cultivators actually be worried about not defeating the Candle Sky Civilization?

"Merely the prototype of Yin-Yang Dao Fruit," Wu Yao believed that at the time he possessed the Swap Dao Fruit, he was not weaker than an Immortal Jiuchong. He abandoned the Swap Dao Fruit, attempting to condense a completely new Dao Fruit. If he still could not surpass Immortal Jiuchong, that would indeed be a joke.

Wu Yao bit his fingertip, drew talismans in the void, each talisman was worth a fortune, if a talisman master had the luck to study one, they would have new insights into the art of talismans within three days.

Suddenly, the talisman appeared in front of Lu Yang, without any signs, as if it traveled through space.

The talisman exploded in front of Lu Yang, a purple thunder of ten thousand Zhang descended, slicing open Lu Yang’s skin and flesh—this was not a single thunderbolt, but a myriad enveloping the entire star!

Cultivators have a natural fear of heavenly thunder, Integration Period Cultivators stepped back further, trying to distance themselves from the battlefield as much as possible.

"What kind of thunder is this?"

"Could it be the legendary Shaking Heaven Immortal Thunder?!"

Old cultivators in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage were extremely shocked, a talisman drawn out of thin air possessed such power, and Wu Yao was continuously drawing more!

Lu Yang’s injuries recovered swiftly, not even a moment had passed.

He understood now, Wu Yao had used the Space Spell to transport the talisman right in front of him, catching him off guard.

Space Spell was Lu Yang’s weakness, very disadvantageous for him to handle. He certainly couldn’t use Heaven and Earth in an Inch to jump off a cliff to flee. Wu Yao needed to maintain his reputation, and he needed to keep his dignity.

Talismans continuously appeared at Wu Yao’s fingertips, hundreds and thousands of them, all different kinds of Thunder Talismans. If they all erupted at full power, their might would be no weaker than the Immortal Ascension Thunder.

Wu Yao used the Space Spell, transporting all these talismans in front of Lu Yang, forming a paper wall. If one had not seen it with their own eyes, no one would believe such precious talismans could be produced like cabbages.

Among all Immortals, he was the only one capable of wielding Immortal Talismans.

"Explode!" Wu Yao uttered lightly, and hundreds of Thunder Talismans ignited all at once.

Lu Yang harbored the heart of becoming an immortal, not fearing these talismans that were comparable to the Immortal Ascension Tribulation. However, he was not foolish enough to stand still and let the thunder strike him.

As the Thunder Talismans were about to explode, the hearts of the watching cultivators clenched, none of them confident enough to withstand a real Immortal Ascension Tribulation.

Unexpectedly, these halfway burnt talismans reverted like time flowing backward, returning to their undamaged state.

Wu Yao: "?"

Since learning talismans, he had never encountered such a situation.

What kind of technique was this, could it be that the opponent actually mastered time magic, a skill he himself couldn’t grasp?

Wu Yao liked to explore the unknown, striving to be all-powerful, but ultimately, he did not have the talent for Time Magic, having learned only the basics.

Unexpectedly, he encountered an opponent skilled in Time Magic this time.

It was just a technique he wasn’t good at, after all.

Wu Yao’s right arm trembled, seizing a bronze spear—this was a piece of Immortal Gold that he commanded the Nine Heavens Army to scour the universe for, forged into an Immortal Treasure.

He stepped with an unknown rhythm, and in the next moment, he appeared in front of Lu Yang, thrusting with his spear!

"Chaotic Shadow Step!" A Transcendance Tribulation Stage elder who was knowledgeable about history exclaimed, "This is Emperor Wu Yao of Yu’s unique technique, only he knows it!"

"What? He is Emperor Wu Yao?!"

The revelation of Wu Yao’s identity by the Transcendance Tribulation Stage elder caused an uproar. They suspected him to be a cultivator from the Cultivation World based on his fluent use of the language.

But what none of them had expected was that the Heavenly Emperor of the Candle Sky Civilization was actually Emperor Wu Yao!

Following the revelation of his identity, endless questions emerged.

Wasn’t the Cultivation World sealed by the Four Ancient Immortals, barring everyone from leaving? When did he leave the Cultivation World? And how did he manage to leave?

Lu Yang and Wu Yao could answer these questions, but they were both fully committed to the fight, leaving no room to address these issues.

Everyone’s gaze on Lu Yang shifted once again, his talent was terrifying for being able to evenly match Wu Yao, who had re-cultivated after becoming an Immortal!

Such a thing had never been heard of before in history!

Those geniuses who had sworn to catch up to Lu Yang, or those proclaimed as "mini Lu Yang," fell into despair. Lu Yang was so powerful that they lost the heart to pursue him.

At this moment, even if one were to call Lu Yang the greatest genius of all ages, no one would object.

The knowledgeable Transcendance Tribulation Stage elder shouted out in fear for Lu Yang, "This is bad, Chaotic Shadow Step can disrupt the surrounding space, Lu Yang won’t be able to use his Space Spells!"

Just as the bronze spear was about to pierce Lu Yang, he disappeared out of thin air and abruptly appeared behind Wu Yao, without any spatial fluctuations!

"What?!"

Wu Yao’s pupils shrank, how did he accomplish that? It certainly wasn’t a Space Spell, it’s not even something magic can do!

It certainly wasn’t the Yin-Yang Dao Fruit Prototype!

In fact, Lu Yang was using the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype to return to a location in the starry space he had previously occupied during their fight!

This means, Lu Yang can now appear wherever he wants to!

Swords crossed the sky, the sword light soared, capable of severing stars.

Clang!

Wu Yao, with his vast experience, guessed Lu Yang’s intention, he turned his back and held the spear to block this lethal strike!

Wu Yao’s face darkened, he was actually deceived by a junior not even a fraction of his age: "You definitely don’t possess the Yin-Yang Dao Fruit Prototype, what exactly are you, wait, why does your sword look so familiar?"

Wu Yao stared in astonishment at the Green Peak Sword in Lu Yang’s hand, his most cherished Immortal Sword among many Immortal Treasures, forged with great effort!

Only then did Lu Yang remember that the Green Peak Sword given to him by his Eldest Senior Sister was a trophy acquired from Wu Yao.

"Sword, come!" Wu Yao called for his Immortal Sword using his Spiritual Sense.

But the Immortal Sword, not only did it not return to him, but also snuggled closer to Lu Yang.

Lu Yang felt somewhat embarrassed, "Uh, if I say it’s because I have the Sword Spirit Root and the sword naturally feels close to me, would you believe that?"

"You’re kidding! Do you think I haven’t seen a Sword Spirit Root before! Return my Immortal Sword!" Wu Yao roared in rage. He had yet to defeat this junior, losing face already, and now his favorite Immortal Sword also betrayed him; today, only one of them could leave alive!

Lu Yang thought for a moment and decided using the Green Peak Sword against Wu Yao was somewhat inappropriate, so he put away the Green Peak Sword and took out the Inheriting Shadow Sword and Seven Star Sword Set.

"Actually, I also know the Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array, but I won’t use it against you." Lu Yang kindly said, after all, the Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array was also Wu Yao’s creation, no need to add fuel to the fire.

"I don’t need your pity!" Wu Yao exploded in anger.

The Inheriting Shadow Sword and Seven Star Sword Set formed the Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array, enveloping Wu Yao. The powerful force made Wu Yao’s legs bend, and he knelt down with a thump, the posture was quite standard.

"I thought you said you didn’t need me to hold back."

"You’re courting death, kid!"

Chapter 1321: 1320: Creation

The universe once again erupted into a horrific cosmic explosion, causing many stars to fall and turn to dust.

Swordsmanship dazzled brilliantly, spear technique shone dazzlingly; intertwined, they resembled two colliding nebulae, crushing against each other, the terrifying Qi drowning out this area, making it impossible for even those in the Unification Realm to discern where the two combatants were.

"Heavenly Eye Technique!" The only exception was Shi Chan, who used one of the Six Spiritual Powers of Buddhism, the Heavenly Eye Technique, to open the Heavenly Eye, perceiving the boundless worlds in all directions.

"Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array." Lu Yang once again deployed the Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array. Eight Immortal Swords, plus a Sword Washing Pool to take the place of the Green Peak Sword, might as well be considered nine Immortal Swords, their power infinite and unending.

"Trying to use my own move against me?" The previous instance was merely the opponent taking advantage of his anger to launch a sneak attack, succeeding by luck. How could he possibly fall for the same move a second time?

Not to mention, he was the creator of the Sword Array, naturally aware of where the weak nodes in the Dawn Sword Formation were.

He formed mysterious seals with his hands, striking the weak points of the Sword Array.

However, the Sword Array did not collapse as he anticipated; it remained intact.

"Have you altered this Array Method?" Wu Yao perceived meticulously, discovering that the Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array deployed by Lu Yang was different from the one he had created. It seemed even more ingenious?

"Speaking as if the Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array was your original creation." Lu Yang flicked his fingers, and the Immortal Swords streaked across the sky like meteors, crashing toward Wu Yao.

The Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array wasn’t originally created by Wu Yao; he merely found remnants of the Sword Array left by the Qilin Immortal and deduced it from there.

The Qilin Immortal designed this Sword Array to deal with his two wives when apologies were needed, a forced kneeling Sword Array created specifically for such occasions by Yingtian Immortal.

Lu Yang had long since obtained from Yingtian Immortal the methods to enhance the Sword Array, its might surpassing its former glory.

"If that’s the case... Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array!" Wu Yao’s bronze spear flickered, astonishingly transforming into numerous identical spears, replacing swords with spears to form an Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array.

The simplest and most brutal way to counter the Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array is to create a completely opposing one.

The falling Immortal Swords, like large stars, collided with the rising bronze spears, like the proud sun, releasing a radiant and dazzling immortal light.

If the number of Immortal Swords equaled the number of spears, Lu Yang’s Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array would have the upper hand. However, Wu Yao forcibly compensated for the shortcomings in the formation with the number of spears, fighting Lu Yang to a standstill.

Lu Yang raised an eyebrow; Wu Yao suddenly producing so many bronze spears capable of clashing head-on with the Immortal Swords was clearly not an act of magic skill.

"Is this also one of the abilities of the Dao Fruit Outline? Duplication?"

Lu Yang shook his head; it couldn’t be Duplication Dao Fruit Outline, for that would be a lower-tier compared to Guan Shanhai’s Imprinting Dao Fruit, something Wu Yao would certainly not do.

Wu Yao flicked water to form ink, using space as his canvas. Demon Beasts sprang forth onto the canvas, transforming into real Demon Beasts, charging towards Lu Yang.

The Four Holy Beasts, the Four Evils, Dragon, Phoenix, Di Jiang... these Demon Beasts were all fierce entities who reached the Transcendance Tribulation Stage and Semi-Immortal level during Ancient Times, similar to historical afterimages, left their imprints in the world; the Demon Beasts that Wu Yao drew fused with these imprints, indistinguishable from the arrival of their ancient true forms!

Veterans at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage were so anxious they felt their hearts rise to their throats; even through the communication device, they could sense the murderous aura of these Demon Beasts, surely Ancient Demons!

"This won’t be easy." Zhui Yue True Person, who was drinking tea at the Great Snow Mountain, had a grave expression. These Demon Beasts were Creations, without genuine emotions and feelings, and she would suffer greatly if she recklessly clashed with them.

"Qing He, who do you think will win?"

Lu Yang’s Third Senior Sister, Gan Tian, was also at the frontline. Zhui Yue True Person and Qing He were watching the battle remotely through Gan Tian’s Quantum Communication Device.

Qing He scoffed disdainfully: "Of course, Lu Yang will win. Although I’m reluctant to admit it, he’s the disciple chosen by the Elders. Whether his opponent is Emperor Yu or the Heavenly Emperor doesn’t matter; how could the Elders’ disciple possibly lose?"

Zhui Yue True Person’s eyes twitched. Was the relationship between Immortal Fairy and Lu Yang really that of master and disciple? Her Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo did not tell her this.

Such talk should avoid being mentioned, lest she lose Qing He as a tea companion.

"Roar—"

Faced with the assault of a hundred demons, Lu Yang summoned back his Immortal Swords. Eight Immortal Swords guarded the eight directions, and their peerless Sword Qi broke through the Thirty-three Heavens, shocking the universe.

"Kill!"

Lu Yang had never faced such a number of Demon Beasts before and was thrilled by the challenge. He saw the Demon Beasts as whetstones for his blade, aiding his ascension in Swordsmanship.

Sword light shone brightly, tearing through space; every swing meant a Demon Beast was obliterated.

Despite the hundred demons being in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, Lu Yang couldn’t possibly hope to extinguish all the Demon Beasts with just a single person and sword.

He was wounded before long. At this moment, he completely lost his sense of pain, immersed in the pleasure of comprehending Swordsmanship. Even Wu Yao would have to commend his boldness for seeking enlightenment in such peril.

Wu Yao quickly realized something was amiss; not only was Lu Yang injured, but he was also grievously wounded by the Demon Beasts several times. Such wounds could damage his foundation if not tended to, yet Lu Yang’s injuries recovered in an instant!

Wu Yao understood—Lu Yang wasn’t so much bold as he was confident, treating this battle as a crucible for honing his skills!

The more Wu Yao thought about it, the angrier he became. He commanded the Demon Beasts to attack en masse, trying to overwhelm Lu Yang with sheer numbers and deprive him of any chance to heal!

The Demon Beasts piled onto Lu Yang like a mountain, leaving him unable to move even a fingertip, and the eight Immortal Swords were equally suppressed by the swarm.

Boom—

The mountain of Demon Beasts exploded, and the severed limbs and debris fluttered across the universe, turning into ink.

"Self-Detonation?!"

Wu Yao was shocked; he didn’t expect Lu Yang to opt for the most aggressive tactic.

However, what appeared before his eyes was an unscathed Lu Yang, looking nothing like someone who had self-destructed.

Wu Yao frowned: "Talisman recovery, self-detonation recovery... could it be some sort of Dao Fruit Outline related to time that can reverse its flow?"

"No, that doesn’t explain the golden phoenix formed from the Immortal Fire. What exactly is your Dao Fruit Outline?"

With a light chuckle, Lu Yang responded: "Growing hair, duplicating weapons, conjuring a hundred demons. What is your Dao Fruit Outline?"

"Do you think I would tell you!" They exclaimed in unison. Both were cunning; they certainly wouldn’t foolishly reveal the nature of their Dao Fruit Outlines.

Though unspoken, Lu Yang had his suspicions—Wu Yao likely possessed a Creation-related Dao Fruit Outline, capable of crafting various rare treasures to combat, which was quite troublesome to contend with.

However, Lu Yang was not fearful. To counter such a Dao Fruit Outline, the simplest and most direct strategy was close-quarters combat!

Whatever rare treasures Wu Yao crafted needed space to work!

Lu Yang seized this exact opportunity.

Wu Yao intended to avoid battle, but Lu Yang traced his origin across various points in space, making it impossible for Wu Yao to escape!

The eight Immortal Swords were irresistible; a Transcendance Tribulation Stage hit by a sword would surely perish, body and Divine Soul. Wu Yao, exuding a Chaotic Aura, once again brought out his bronze spear. The stars dimmed as he struck, splitting heaven and earth, collapsing space.

After hundreds of exchanges, a chunk of Wu Yao’s shoulder was sliced off, though it quickly grew back.

Lu Yang also spat out a mist of blood, struck with internal injuries by Wu Yao.

But Lu Yang’s recovery speed was much faster than Wu Yao’s.

Utilizing both Trace Origin and Immortal Dao Fruit Outlines, aside from Fairy Eternity, Lu Yang feared no war of attrition!

Chapter 1322: 1321 Creation!

The spectators were all shocked, Lu Yang and Wu Yao had chosen the most primitive and brutal way of fighting, sweeping through everything around them, anything that came close would be ground into powder by the fluctuations of their Qi!

Even the stars were no exception.

Those high-hanging stars, just like in Ancient Times, were often affected by the battles of high-level cultivators, but this time the impact was more severe, the destruction more thorough!

"History records that Wu Yao is proficient in the Hundred Skills of Cultivation, truly a paragon of the Cultivation World; seeing him today, it really is so," an old cultivator from the Pass Tribulation Peak reminisced about the previous battles and exclaimed repeatedly.

The Hundred Skills of Cultivation also depend on talent, not just accumulated over time. Many great cultivators spend their lifetime and cannot master a single skill, but Wu Yao has mastered them all, reaching unprecedented heights in many.

"Even more terrifying is Lu Yang; he is so young, yet can handle Wu Yao’s various combat skills, and now he even holds the upper hand," the old timers felt as if they were being left behind by the era, the future belonged to the younger generation.

But upon further reflection, they thought it was just an illusion, after all, among the young ones, only Lu Yang was outstanding.

"Rumors say that during his Foundation Establishment Stage, he once defeated someone in the Divinity Transformation Stage, it’s no surprise he possesses such combat power now."

Wu Yao became more and more alarmed as they fought, Lu Yang’s recovery ability was horrifying. Could this be the healing power brought by the Dao Fruit Prototype? If not for the lack of Immortal Energy around Lu Yang, Wu Yao would have thought he was facing not a Semi-Immortal but a full Immortal!

What exactly is his origin?!

Thinking this, Wu Yao’s gaze hardened, no matter the origin, he, Wu Yao, in his second life having been an Immortal in his first life, was the strongest of roots, everyone else must give way!

Without this mindset of being the best, how could one talk about becoming an Immortal, let alone achieve it!

Even those in the Body Integration Realm who couldn’t keep up with their movements saw that Lu Yang held the advantage in this battle, with Wu Yao’s energy flagging, and would lose within a hundred exchanges.

"Sky Demon Disintegration!"

With Wu Yao’s furious roar that shattered the starry road, his energy unexpectedly returned to its peak, matching Lu Yang evenly!

He used the Sky Demon Disintegration Technique to temporarily enhance his combat power, despite the backlash, he couldn’t care less now.

He had underestimated Lu Yang from the start, exhausting a great amount of energy, forcing him to use the disdainful Sky Demon Disintegration Technique to enhance his combat power!

Thinking that Lu Yang had also used a similar tactic, Wu Yao felt much relieved after using the Sky Demon Disintegration Technique.

"Sky-shattering!"

He no longer employed those false probing techniques, but at peak condition, he performed the peerless spear technique.

Sky-shattering, it was not only the name of the bronze spear but also the reputation of the spear technique; the spear shatters the vault of heaven, forging a path to the future!
