He used Creation to temporarily elevate the quality of the Sky-shattering Spear to that of a fully-activated Immortal Artifact.

The revealed might of the Sky-shattering Spear caused cultivators in the Body Integration Realm millions of miles away to vomit blood.

The cultivators clutching their chests, spitting blood in shock, couldn’t believe such a distance would affect them.

What state would Lu Yang, who was directly confronting it, be in?

Lu Yang held the Inheriting Shadow Sword, drawing an arc, symbolizing the Consummation of his Swordsmanship Cultivation; the Seven Star Sword Set successively entered the Inheriting Shadow Sword, Ten Thousand Swords Returning to Origin, Sword Qi surged, also reaching a peak state!

This was Lu Yang’s pride, the Immortal Sword Technique!

"Quick, defend!" Shi Chan, using the Heavenly Eye Technique, immediately called out after seeing Lu Yang’s state, urging everyone to raise their defenses.

Everyone hurriedly summoned their defensive magic items in front of their chests.

Boom—

The two most powerful forces collided, Qi and Blood surged wildly, mixed with surging Soul Power, tearing the space into pieces, bursting out countless wormholes, connecting unknown parts of the universe.

"Who won?" the cultivators at the Body Integration Realm hastily inquired. Shi Chan only frowned. The chaotic mist in the center of the battlefield obstructed the view, and even the Heavenly Eye Technique could not penetrate this dense fog of chaos!

The chaotic mist dissipated, revealing both sides of the battlefield.

Wu Yao, clutching the sword wound on his chest, looked at Lu Yang in disbelief, "Is this your swordsmanship? Nothing is uncuttable, even the cultivation level can be cleanly severed!"

This was the power Lu Yang drew from the Immortal Sword Technique, unleashing the potential of the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype. Facing a versatile opponent like Wu Yao, only by truly injuring him could his cultivation be reduced!

Lu Yang originally intended to reduce Wu Yao to the Early Tribulation Stage with a single sword strike, but Wu Yao’s foundation was terrifyingly strong, only allowing a reduction to the Middle Tribulation Stage.

Hearing Wu Yao’s words, all the spectators’ pupils shrank suddenly, doubting what they had heard, "What, Lu Yang can reduce someone’s cultivation level?!"

To what extent must one comprehend swordsmanship to be able to sever someone’s cultivation with a single sword? Counting through the past and present, no supreme swordsman has ever been heard to achieve such a feat.

Swordsmanship reaches Immortality!

Both men fell silent, clashing once again. The sun and moon exploded, and great stars fell, leaving discarded stars everywhere. Wu Yao bled profusely, covered in wounds, his pride not allowing him to bow to a junior.

"Senior Wu Yao, please admit defeat." The Inheriting Shadow Sword pointed at Wu Yao, its cold blade reflecting Lu Yang’s indifferent gaze.

"Admit defeat? Young man, when have you ever seen an Immortal admit defeat!" Wu Yao chuckled proudly, this was the pride of being an Immortal, even if his cultivation was reduced, he insisted on fighting!

In fact, several times Wu Yao was beaten into unconsciousness, relying on his body’s instincts to continue fighting, which moved all onlookers and earned their genuine respect.

Wu Yao became an Immortal not just through talent but also an invincible will.

"Emperor Heavenly Emperor!" Shi Wumian and others wept bitterly.

Should he admit defeat?

This was a recurring thought whenever Wu Yao lost consciousness.

No, who was he? He was Emperor Wu Yao of Yu, Heavenly Emperor Wu Yao, the unprecedented pinnacle of Hundred Skills of Cultivation, how could he possibly admit defeat!

Wu Yao, relying on sheer willpower, forcibly resisted this bout of unconsciousness.

At this moment, he recalled his life experiences, witnessing the darkness of the Great Qian Dynasty in his youth, determined to overthrow it, meeting Guan Shanhai in his youth, both a teacher and a friend, bickering and quarreling, leading an army together, contending for supremacy in the world, defeating several Semi-Immortals, establishing the Great Yu Dynasty, and creating the Imperial Teacher system.

Then he witnessed the strength of Ashy Bean, Yun Zhi’s invincibility, abandoning everything, situating himself in an external world, ruling the Candle Sky Civilization, becoming a true Heavenly Emperor.

Just as Lu Yang was preparing for the final strike, suddenly he felt a chilling sensation. The unconscious Wu Yao burst out with a rich vitality, his realm exploding in growth, returning to the Pass Tribulation Peak!

Not right, his realm was still increasing!

Wu Yao hovered, his eyes opening wide, his gaze electrifying!

Boom—

Immortal Clouds gathered, purple lightning surged like tides, forming a lightning world that invoked fear in the hearts of cultivators.

Immortal Ascension Tribulation!

Wu Yao was actually achieving Immortality at this moment!

Lu Yang quickly retreated, distancing himself from the Immortal Ascension Tribulation area to avoid being affected.

Bathed in the lightning of the Immortal Ascension Tribulation, Wu Yao roared with laughter:

"Boy, you must have thought my Dao Fruit Outline was something of creation."

"Pity you only guessed half right, my Dao Fruit Outline is named ’Creation’, which can both create all things and grant me fortune, turning misfortune into blessings!"

Chapter 1323: 1322: Breaking Through at the Eleventh Hour is Not as Good as Strong Backing

The Creation Dao Fruit, this was the Dao Fruit that Wu Yao decided to condense after much deliberation.

This kind of Dao Fruit can mediate creation, evolve all things, and even bestow creation upon itself, altering its destiny, turning misfortune into fortune.

After being deceived by the Qilin Immortal and battling with Ashy Bean, and realizing the gap between him and Yun Zhi in combat, Wu Yao finally understood one truth—he had been relying purely on sheer luck to survive till now.

Every battle had brought him the desired outcome, fighting with Ashy Bean, gaining the help of Qilin Immortal, and avoiding the power of the Survival of the Fittest Dao Fruit within the Box World seal; after fighting with Yun Zhi, he escaped from a death scenario, transferring to outside of the Box World.

Though the two battles were perilous, the gains were also immense.

This made Wu Yao realize his extraordinary destiny, but relying on such an ethereal thing as destiny was not enough, the most reliable method was to use the Dao Fruit to seal it firmly and enhance it further.

For this purpose, he condensed a Dao Fruit Outline with both "Creation" and "Opportunity" attributes.

However, it is easy to condense the Dao Fruit Outline, but to advance it further is as difficult as ascending to the heavens.

And through battling with Lu Yang, he finally found his opportunity, a battle with his back against the wall, a breakthrough in a desperate situation, under the pressure from Lu Yang, fully ignited the potential of the Creation Dao Fruit Outline, touching the verge of becoming an Immortal.

Becoming an Immortal, it is today!

"Is that the legendary Immortal Ascension Tribulation?!"

"Wu Yao is becoming an Immortal?!"

The meaning of the Immortal Ascension Tribulation for cultivators goes without saying, it is the trial that all cultivators dream of, and also the final obstacle on the path of cultivation, countless Semi-Immortals have perished in the Immortal Ascension Tribulation, even the Sword Tower Supreme failed to pass it, now only a trace of his soul remains.

But no one thinks this challenge would defeat Wu Yao.

The crowd of cultivators can no longer remain calm, no one had expected Wu Yao to become an Immortal at this moment.

If he becomes an Immortal, who could possibly contend against him?

They can only watch Wu Yao Pass Tribulation helplessly, and like all Heavenly Tribulations, the Immortal Ascension Tribulation cannot be interrupted, anyone daring to intervene is courting death.

Lu Yang hastily evacuates this Star System, which has been enveloped by the Immortal Ascension Tribulation, and the range of the Tribulation continues to expand outward.

The sun, the moon, and the stars turn into nothingness under the power of the Immortal Ascension Tribulation, leaving only Wu Yao in the entire Star System.

The mechanical body uncontrollable, even Dean Gao is shivering behind the screen, it is the innate instinct of beings, unavoidable.

The power of Heavenly Tribulation has been deeply etched into the souls of all beings since the Ancient Times, a fear imprint passed on through bloodline heritage.

This is a fear rooted deep within the soul, cultivators of all ranks shudder with fear, witnessing this scene.

Only Lu Yang isn’t much affected, accustomed to the clashes of Immortals, the power of Immortal Ascension Tribulation is just so-so.

Strong as the Immortal Ascension Tribulation may be, it’s just a test for those who are yet to become Immortal, how could it be more terrifying than an Immortal striking.

Lu Yang feels a bit regretful; it’s a pity he isn’t truly at the Pass Tribulation Peak, his power is borrowed, otherwise, he could’ve joined Wu Yao in his Immortal Ascension Tribulation, and both could become Immortal together.

Lu Yang looks at the Immortal Ascension Tribulation, his expression shifting slightly, pondering what effect it would have if he used the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype against the Immortal Ascension Tribulation?

But this suicidal thought flashes by fleetingly, given that the Immortal Ascension Tribulation is definitely of an Immortal Level, it isn’t something a half-finished Dao Fruit prototype could influence.

Immediately, Lu Yang conceives another thought, has the Immortal Ascension Tribulation really existed since ancient times? Could it possibly be man-made?

The captured Candle Sky Soldier seeing this scene, his eyes burning with fervor, vociferously exclaims the eternity and immortality of the Heavenly Emperor, forever enduring in the world.

This is the Heavenly Emperor they envisioned, who, even if temporarily fallen and at a disadvantage, would reverse the world and lead them to victory!

A searing thunder tide descends, overwhelming Wu Yao, the Immortal Ascension Tribulation represents the will of heaven and earth, and can obliterate the root source of living beings.

"Hahaha, Immortal Ascension Tribulation, we meet again, still the same power, not enough though!"

Yet, amidst the endless thunder tide, Wu Yao’s laughter could be heard, his skin split open, his body charred, with old skin shedding and new skin growing, undergoing a complete transformation, Qi and Blood boiling, showing no signs of decay or fear, but rather mocking the inadequacy of the Immortal Ascension Tribulation.

The second round of the Immortal Ascension Tribulation arrives, far more terrifying than the first, tens of thousands of lightning envelop Wu Yao, piercing heaven and earth, shattering nearby star systems, everyone thought Wu Yao would fall, but he still withstands the Heavenly Tribulation, full of vigor!

This is the once Emperor Wu Yao of Yu, soon returning to his peak state, no, even stronger than his peak after becoming an immortal!

Thunder Tribulation evolves countless Thunder Spirits to punish Wu Yao, and Wu Yao counters by transforming blood into ink, creating a myriad of demonic creatures to meet the attack.

The third round, the fourth round...

The power of the Immortal Ascension Tribulation continuously increases, with the universe vast and boundless, only the Thunder Tribulation remains between heaven and earth, Wu Yao stands firm like a Dinghai divine needle.

However, by the later rounds, he could no longer relax as before, battered and bruised, the Thunder Tribulation turns into an endless great fire, a sea of fire burning the heavens, collapsing the void, as if to refine Wu Yao.

In the end, the endless thunder tide vanishes, Wu Yao’s body as black as charcoal.

"Failed?" someone murmurs quietly?

As if responding to the doubts, a cracking sound emits from Wu Yao’s body, although not loud, it resonates throughout the entire universe.

Old skin peels off, revealing newly formed flesh, pristine and smooth yet divine, reborn!

"Turned into an immortal, huh." Lu Yang scratches his head in vexation, "It’s not that he can’t be beaten."

The picturesque Green Peak Sword Small World turns half into a wasteland, Lu Yang moves a portion of the buddha land from the Buddha Country.

Even just a part, it’s still Buddha land from Buddha Country, with the power of faith interconnected, Lu Yang can mold an immortal body, and fight the newly immortal Wu Yao.

But there really is no need.

Lu Yang turned his head to glance at the Eldest Senior Sister who suddenly appeared beside him, not knowing if she had just returned or had been back for a while.

The charred body completely shattered, Wu Yao’s eyes radiating immortal light, capable of illuminating thousands of worlds, his body emits a faint glow, every inch of his skin brimming with immense power, Dao Fruit solidified, finally reaching consummation, able to recreate the world and reshape the universe.

"Young man, if you kindly return my Immortal Sword..."

Wu Yao’s gaze falls on Lu Yang, before becoming immortal, Lu Yang was a worthy opponent, capable of pushing him to become an immortal, but after becoming immortal, he no longer sees Lu Yang as worthy.

Before and after becoming an immortal, there lies a celestial divide.

He then saw the figure standing beside Lu Yang, whose very presence sends a chill down his spine for the rest of his life.

Wu Yao shudders, instantly sobering up, his eyes clear, understanding the situation.

He was deeply attached to the Sword Heart, caught in the excitement of the moment, hadn’t thought much about other matters.

But now seeing Yun Zhi, he recalls that after giving up his Immortal Sword, wasn’t it Yun Zhi who picked it up!

Wu Yao dims his radiance, flies to Lu Yang’s side, cheerfully patting Lu Yang on the shoulder, his voice hearty.

"The thing I admire most is talented juniors like you, I won’t need this Immortal Sword anymore, I’ll give it to you."

"I was just joking before, you didn’t take it seriously, right?"

Chapter 1324: 1323: There are Great Opportunities and Great Creation

The senior cultivators in the Body Integration Realm and the Transcendance Tribulation Stage watching from afar were extremely astonished. Why has Wu Yao’s attitude changed so dramatically after becoming an Immortal?

Is this the mindset of an Immortal? Ascending from the mortal to the Immortal realm, adopting an aloof attitude, letting go of all past grudges and resentments.

"Has Fellow Daoist Lu Yang got two more people with him? Who are they?"

"It’s Eldest Senior Sister Yun Zhi from the Seek Dao Sect." Shi Chan saw Yun Zhi using the Heavenly Eye Technique, his expression turning odd, remembering the time he went to Seek Dao Sect to study and had proactively challenged Yun Zhi only to get soundly defeated.

Could it be that Wu Yao’s change in attitude has something to do with Senior Yun Zhi?

But that shouldn’t be, is Senior Yun Zhi an Immortal?

Those old seniors in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage all had similar thoughts to Shi Chan; they had all sparred with Yun Zhi, acknowledged her strength, but thought her to be a Semi-Immortal, not someone who would make Wu Yao apprehensive.

"Daoist Yun Zhi, long time no see. You’re as elegant as ever after so many years." Wu Yao laughed heartily, and one would think he and Yun Zhi were old Daoist friends who hadn’t seen each other for years.

To this, Yun Zhi simply replied with an "Mm," making Wu Yao’s smile freeze on his face, awkward and unsure of how to continue the conversation.

"Wow, you’re pretty amazing, truly befitting of Emperor Yu, able to fight toe-to-toe with our second-in-command."

Naturally, Yun Zhi’s return was accompanied by Yun Mengmeng.

Wu Yao: "..."

Still at a loss for what to say, Wu Yao thought, what kind of comment is that? In terms of fame or status, Lu Yang couldn’t compare to him, and yet it’s spoken as if it’s his honor to be able to exchange blows with Lu Yang.

Doesn’t this young girl know how to speak?

"Second-in-command, are you alright?" Yun Mengmeng patted and searched Lu Yang to check his injuries.

Lu Yang’s face flushed red, hastily retreating from Yun Mengmeng: "I’m fine, I’m fine, what could possibly be wrong with me?"

A bolt from the blue descended, it was the Thunder Tribulation clone of Yingtian Immortal.

He sensed the birth of an Immortal Ascension Tribulation near the Cultivation World, so he sent a clone to investigate, wanting to know who had ascended as an Immortal.

"Who is this person?" Yingtian Immortal asked Lu Yang.

"Emperor Wu Yao of Yu, or you could say Heavenly Emperor Wu Yao."

Yingtian Immortal understood at once; it was him. Did he know the gates to the Cultivation World were open, returning home to visit?

Noticing the Candle Sky Soldier and Integration Period Cultivators, he realized it wasn’t quite so: "It looks like a lot has happened during the time I was absent."

After speaking, he dissipated the clone, sending the information here back to his main body. So much had happened in the Cultivation World, it was time to return and take a look.

Finally, Yun Zhi spoke, but her words sent a chill down his spine: "Senior Wu Yao, you’ve condoned your subordinates in their theft of Spirit Stones from the Cultivation World. Do you have anything to say about it?"

Wu Yao quickly offered a placating smile: "A misunderstanding, it’s all a misunderstanding, Fellow Daoist Yun Zhi. It was all done by my subordinates without my knowledge. The Cultivation World is my home too; had I known they were pillaging Spirit Stones from there, I certainly would not have allowed them to commit such outrageous acts!"

"You see, as soon as I learned they were going to the Cultivation World to steal Spirit Stones, I set out immediately to intercept them. It’s just that they were too far away, and I was one step too late, they still engaged in conflict with the Cultivation World."

Yun Zhi’s eyes slightly lifted, maintaining that unchanging, deep, well-like expression, leaving Wu Yao unable to decipher her thoughts.

"A misunderstanding? So you’re saying, Senior Wu Yao, that your attempt to reclaim your captive and the resulting conflict with my Junior Brother, as well as injuring my Junior Brother, was also a misunderstanding?"

Hearing this, Lu Yang happened to cough a few times, his voice hoarse and heavy, hinting at serious injury, and also coughing up some blood, substantiating the Eldest Senior Sister’s words.

Wu Yao’s eyelids twitched upon hearing the words, and his voice grew icy, abandoning his previously ingratiating demeanor: "So, you mean to say, Daoist Yun Zhi insists on not letting this matter go?"

"The incident has happened; naturally, there must be an explanation. Wu Yao, Senior, you are welcome to discuss this at Captive Peak within the Seek Dao Sect."

"Don’t go too far!" Wu Yao bellowed in anger, fully aware of what kind of place Captive Peak was.

Who is he? Having cultivated to become an Immortal in two lifetimes, he’s the only one since ancient times to have achieved this. As an Immortal who transcends the mortal world, when has one ever heard of an Immortal being reduced to a mere prisoner? It would be an immense humiliation!

He had previously thought to avoid conflict for the sake of profit, not wishing to provoke a formidable enemy like Yun Zhi over trifles.

But if Yun Zhi thinks she can arbitrarily decide his fate, then she is gravely mistaken.

At this moment, he is at the peak of his life. He is far stronger than when he was Emperor Wu Yao, not only in terms of Mind State but also the disparity within the Dao Fruits. He is a Heavenly Emperor in the truest sense, and if a fight were to erupt, he would fear no one!

Simultaneously, he covertly stimulated the Creation Dao Fruit, predicting the outcome of this potential battle.

"After this battle, I will be able to comprehend true principles, reunite with dear friends and mentors after more than a hundred thousand years, clash with other Immortals, and exchange insights on the Immortal Dao, filling in what I lack?"

"What great fortune, what a grand opportunity this is!"

When the Dao Fruit was merely an outline, his clash with Lu Yang let him vaguely sense that Lu Yang would bring him great fortune. Albeit this premonition was faint, once the Dao Fruit formed, his sensitivity to fortune and opportunity became much clearer, a sort of prediction of the future!

Assured by the results given by the Dao Fruit, Wu Yao was full of confidence. Such gains must not be missed from this opportunity.

The fight must happen now if at all; upon learning of this place, the Thunder Tribulation clone of the Yingtian Immortal will surely return to the Cultivation World. And if Yun Zhi were to join forces with the Four Ancient Immortals, he would certainly be no match.

The bronze spear reappeared, its might extraordinary. The Creation Dao Fruit easily advanced the spear, transforming it from an Immortal Treasure into a true Immortal Artifact.

As the spear danced, it emitted a light that resembled hanging galaxies, demonstrating the vast difference between Immortal Treasures and true Immortal Artifacts with just this one move.

The Creation Dao Fruit unveiled its divine power, with myriad monsters leaping out of the painting-like Fish Leaping Over the Dragon’s Gate, appearing even more formidable than legends suggested. Despite not stepping into the Immortal Level, they already subtly bore the grace of Immortality.

The bronze spear, too, was replicated by the mere thought into millions of copies, hanging throughout space.

Immortal Talismans, Immortal-level Arrays... they overwhelmed the sky, submerging the area.

The Integration Period Cultivators and the Transcendance Tribulation Stage ones were utterly astonished by this scene. Wasn’t the fighting supposed to have stopped? Why has it suddenly escalated again?

Seeing the extent of preparations, it was clear that serious engagement was imminent, but Wu Yao was already an Immortal; who present here was worth his true effort?

"Daoist Yun Zhi, you will regret your decision today!" Wu Yao’s brow bore the mark of an Immortal pattern, controlling all of Creation. Having become an Immortal, his Spiritual Sense metamorphosed into Immortal Sense, the might of which was boundless.

Purple qi rose tumultuously, chaos pervaded, and with creation through Immortal Dao, he attacked Yun Zhi, showcasing the majesty of an Immortal!

"Cloud Falling Palm."

Yun Zhi responded indifferently to Wu Yao’s roar, her single hand shattering his Immortal Creation.

Wu Yao watched powerlessly as the delicate hand fell, unstoppable, regret and despair surging from the depths of his heart.

Was the gap between him and Yun Zhi still so vast?

Boom—

Immortal Creation shattered, and all returned to calm.

Wu Yao, lifted by Yun Zhi, had a vacant look, his clarity gone. At this moment, he finally understood a lesson—not to take one’s dignity too seriously, for there will be times when one is shamed.

Chapter 1325: 1324: Balance of Power at Captive Peak

"What did I just see, she defeated Wu Yao with a single move?!"

The antiquated cultivators at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage had their jaws drop in astonishment upon witnessing this scene. This was Wu Yao, who had ascended twice, yet Yun Zhi had defeated him with one move.

Could it be that their expectations were mistaken, and Yun Zhi was not a Semi-Immortal, but an Immortal?

That couldn’t be right either. The commotion of Immortal Ascension Tribulation is immense; how could Yun Zhi possibly ascend to immortality without any noise?

Regardless, the facts lay before them, and they had no choice but to believe them.

To think they had been concerned about Yun Zhi’s safety earlier, they should have been worried about Wu Yao’s life instead!

Seeing Wu Yao’s defeat made these old cultivators feel much better. They were defeated by Yun Zhi in one move, and so was Wu Yao; the outcome was the same for all.

"Ah, if I had known Daoist Yun Zhi could defeat Wu Yao, I wouldn’t have lowered my realm to mend my Dao heart!" lamented a certain Tribulation Crossing Great Power who, after being defeated by Yun Zhi, felt ashamed and had no choice but to lower his cultivation and re-cultivate to find his Dao heart.

Such actions should not be done often; they’re addictive, making them hard to quit.

Other Tribulation Crossing Great Powers nodded; they all had their encounters with Yun Zhi.

Those at the Body Integration Realm had never seen such a scene where the unassuming Yun Zhi defeated Wu Yao with a single move; even myths would not dare fabricate such a tale.

Shi Chan silently recited Amitabha; no wonder Yun Zhi Senior could dominate her peers and brutally defeat her. This makes sense.

It’s not that he’s too weak, but that his opponent is too strong.

Yun Zhi grabbed Wu Yao, tore through space, and returned to the Cultivation World. The Integration Period Cultivators lined up like chicks in a game of eagle catching chicks, obeying and following behind.

They finally found the way back.

Lu Yang and Yun Mengmeng followed behind Yun Zhi; even those Integration Period Cultivators familiar with Lu Yang dared not step forward to inquire about Yun Zhi’s situation.

"Strange, how did Elder Brother Lu Yang and Emperor Wu Yao of Yu fight?" Shi Chan muttered under his breath, his eyebrows furrowing. He intended to recall the battle to aid his future cultivation, but he found he couldn’t remember any details of the fight.

Even if he took out the Recording Orb, or accessed the battleship’s surveillance, it only showed white noise; nothing could be seen.

Not just Shi Chan, other Integration Period Cultivators also noticed this anomaly.

However, they only pondered over it briefly before casting the matter aside as if it was nothing significant.

Lu Yang noted this scene thoughtfully; this was also a manifestation of the Immortal’s forgetting trait.

When he and Wu Yao dueled, Integration Period Cultivators could see and remember, but once Wu Yao ascended to an Immortal, congealed the Dao Fruit, the Immortal’s forgetting trait appeared, making them forget Wu Yao’s methods involving the Dao Fruit, even unconcerned about the matter, essentially erasing it from their consciousness.

As the grand battle concluded, the Cultivation World could finally set its heart at ease; after they had captured the Candle Sky Battleship, the war against Semi-Immortals outside had commenced.

Now with the battle over, they could finally deal with these prisoners.

After returning to the Sect, River Spirit led a squad of prisoners waiting at the sect’s entrance for Yun Zhi, to await her orders.

Yun Zhi was taken aback, somewhat puzzled: "Is Elder River Spirit waiting for me?"

River Spirit nodded: "We’ve encountered some trouble. Captive Peak has no space left. These people can’t be imprisoned there."

The warships that had surrendered to the Seek Dao Sect numbered two hundred with tens of thousands of captives. Not to mention Captive Peak was nearly full; even if it was empty, it couldn’t hold so many people.

Yun Zhi’s expression turned slightly odd; she hadn’t expected to encounter this problem.

"Report it and let the Dynasty handle it," Lu Yang suggested.

"Good idea." River Spirit left with the prisoners; he couldn’t leave the range of the Seek Dao Sect, others had to handle it.

If the Grand Elders were so eager to intervene, it would be good to let them handle this.

"Sister Mengmeng, where have you been these days, did you find anything while exploring the universe?" Lu Yang asked curiously.

"Of course." Yun Mengmeng’s eyebrows danced with excitement, her hands flailing, laughing cheerily.

"I and Xiao Zhi searched for several months before finding a planet with living beings."

"That planet revered gourmet food, with many, many delicious things, even the trees are edible. The people there hollow out the trees, live inside the tree holes, able to eat the trees anytime without fear of collapsing them, as the trees continue to grow."

"In that place, being a chef is the most respected profession, and they have culinary battles, ingredient battles, and even grade the ingredients, creating many cooking methods."

"I just took Xiao Zhi around there to walk and eat, it was so comfortable. We only tasted less than half of the delicacies before we left."

Lu Yang was surprised: "That really sounds like a great place."

From Yun Mengmeng’s frantic gestures, it was clear that she was very satisfied with the place.

"Let me tell you, Xiao Zhi has absolutely no talent for cooking. Seeing so many chefs there, she felt she could do anything and started cooking like them, but it was terrible, even worse than my cooking."

"I think she couldn’t handle the setback, that’s why she insisted on dragging me back to the Cultivation World. We haven’t even visited many famous food cities."

Yun Mengmeng pouted, clearly dissatisfied with Yun Zhi’s domineering solo decision.

Yun Mengmeng didn’t notice that Yun Zhi turned her head, staring at Yun Mengmeng with a rather unfriendly expression.

"So Miss Yun is interested in cooking? Should have said earlier, I, Immortal Fairy, will teach her!" Fairy Eternity was very pleased with Yun Zhi’s timely intervention, considering her a loyal servant, and loyal servants should be rewarded.

Yun Mengmeng continued talking to herself: "But it’s also a good thing, it just happened that Xiao Zhi helped the Second-in-command, so I forgive her."

Yun Mengmeng couldn’t see Yun Zhi’s reaction, but Lu Yang saw it clearly and felt he shouldn’t continue listening.

"Cough cough, Sister Mengmeng, I have something to do, I have to go."

"Hey, I haven’t finished yet." Yun Mengmeng pouted.

"I have gained something from this battle and need to retreat for a breakthrough. See you later, Sister Mengmeng!" Lu Yang left Yun Mengmeng, getting further away and plunging into Sky Gate Mountain not to come out.

"Retreat, well then." Yun Mengmeng expressed her understanding; cultivation is most important.

"Come on, Xiao Tian, let’s go find Little Lotus, I’ll tell you." Yun Mengmeng caught up with Third Senior Sister Gan Tian, who had returned from the battlefield.

Wu Yao’s expression was somewhat like Yun Zhi’s, emotionless and resigned.

To think he, the mighty Heavenly Emperor, had to end up as a prisoner, it was sad and lamentable.

He might be the first Immortal in history to be captured.

Wu Yao was dragged by Yun Zhi all the way from the sect entrance to the core area of the Sect, then to the deserted Captive Peak.

"Hmm?" Wu Yao sensed the existence of Captive Peak, his pupils dilating.

Captive Peak was entirely made of Immortal Gold, how extravagant!

"Here it comes." A prisoner on the outermost edge shouted, half his head sticking out, craning his neck to see Wu Yao approaching, then hurried back to spread the news.

Wu Yao was slightly puzzled; the person who ran past just now looked surprisingly like the Master of All Laws from their Great Yu Dynasty?

"Go in."

Yun Zhi tossed Wu Yao into Captive Peak like throwing a stone.

Wu Yao staggered a few steps, then a figure appeared in front of him.

He looked up, and it was actually Guan Shanhai?!

Guan Shanhai was surrounded by a group of people, who looked like the cultivators from Great Yu?

"How are you here..."

The two were both friends and mentors, separated for over a hundred thousand years. Though there had been disagreements, looking back, those disagreements seemed trivial.

Guan Shanhai patted Wu Yao’s shoulders hard, his sturdy figure unusually emotional.

"You finally came, I’ve been waiting for you for a long time."

Wu Yao, touched by the atmosphere, felt equally emotional: "Yes, I have finally come... Hmm?"

Something about that phrase didn’t seem right.

Guan Shanhai embraced Wu Yao, pointing arrogantly at the people opposite them: "Take a good look, you folks from Great Qian, my people from Great Yu are all here, we aren’t afraid of you anymore!"

Wu Yao: "?"

Chapter 1326: 1325: Late Tribulation Stage

"Watch closely, people of Great Qian, my people from Great Yu have assembled, we’re no longer afraid of you!" Guan Shanhai, long suppressed by the two immortals of Great Qian, finally had the chance to raise his eyebrows and breathe out in relief.

"Exactly, our people from Great Yu have gathered, all you old fogeys from Great Qian just wait and see!"

"Today you’ll learn why Great Yu could replace Great Qian!"

"Our Emperor managed to step out of the Box World, known as the Heavenly Emperor in the outside world, becoming the Cosmic Dominator; can Great Qian do that?"

"Every day you strut around flaunting your seniority, but what use is seniority? Strength is everything!"

The cultivators from Great Yu jeered one after another; even though Guan Shanhai was at his zenith within the Great Yu Dynasty, he was ultimately the second-ranking figure. The pillar of their spirit was, without a doubt, Wu Yao.

Among those venerable elders from the late Tribulation Stage guarding the entrance to the Cultivation World, there was a friend of Grandmaster Child, who contacted Grandmaster Child through a quantum communicator, allowing Grandmaster Child to see Lu Yang and Wu Yao’s valiant fighting. Grandmaster Child adjusted the screen’s angle slightly so that the prisoners could also see.

The prisoners from Great Yu looked forward with longing and finally saw Wu Yao arrive.

Even the insightful Wu Yao couldn’t understand the current situation; those jeering ones appeared to be loyal cultivators of Great Yu while the cultivators of Great Qian stood opposite them, with the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and The Controller also there?

Wu Yao’s mind was a chaotic mess, unable to fathom the situation—weren’t there three immortals here aside from him?

Does this mean he was not the first one to be caught and brought here? Were Guan Shanhai and the others captured as well?

Thinking about it, it made sense; even though it was rare for immortals to decide a victory or to capture one alive, Yun Zhi with her magnificent power from the fault, had the capability to catch Guan Shanhai and the others.

Thinking so far, Wu Yao somewhat understood the origin of Captive Peak, he cleared his throat, taking charge of the order.

"Quiet down, everyone. Now that you have become prisoners, it is time to unite together, there’s no need to waste energy fighting amongst ourselves."

"In my opinion, we can join forces..."

"Join forces my ass!" The Controller exploded with profanity, interrupting Wu Yao, abandoning his usual refined and strategic demeanor. He was irate at the sight of Wu Yao.

He and the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord were hiding just fine inside the Sun, but this damned Wu Yao had to place a seal on it, taking them a hundred thousand years to break free—and they couldn’t even speak of it, for it would be too embarrassing.

Now, finally facing the main adversary here, would they still be men if they did not seek vengeance, would they still have the spirit of cultivators!

Wu Yao frowned, not understanding why The Controller was so hostile toward him: "The Controller, if there’s an issue, we can sit down and have a proper talk..."

Before he could finish, the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord stepped forward with a kick: "Everyone get him!"

Guan Shanhai was waiting for Wu Yao to come in, and so were the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and The Controller.

Wu Yao, caught off guard, stumbled awkwardly for several steps but barely managed to avoid falling.

"Are you alright?" Guan Shanhai helped steady Wu Yao. Wu Yao was the spiritual pillar of the Great Yu cultivators; he could not fall, for it would affect their morale.

Wu Yao spit on the ground: "Damn it, it’s one thing that I can’t beat Yun Zhi, but I won’t lose to you old geezers either. Guan, don’t step in, I’ll take care of them!"

"Come on!" The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord stood his ground, with palms up, beckoning provocatively.

When the leaders of the two great forces confronted each other, it was nonsensical for the rest not to join. The two dynasties clashed once again, and in no time, dust filled the air at Captive Peak.

"Wan Fa, take three people with you to provide support on the west road!"

"Si Lei and Si Chen, the two of you cut off their escape route!"

Guan Shanhai and The Controller stood at a higher position, commanding the army. In daily life, they bickered constantly, each boasting that their own dynasty was the strongest. Having enacted scenarios on the sand table hundreds of times, with victories and defeats on both sides, neither was willing to concede to the other.

Now, the forces of both sides had finally assembled; it was time to put theories to the test!

Captive Peak was unable to use spiritual essence or immortal power, as the Dao Fruit had been plucked away, and the physical body had been suppressed to a very weak state. Initially, Wu Yao was not quite accustomed to the condition of Captive Peak and quickly fell into a disadvantage upon engaging with the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, eliciting cheers from the Great Qian Cultivators who were as fierce as wolves and tigers.

Wu Yao, who possessed exceptional comprehension, quickly found his bearings amidst the encouragement from the Great Yu Cultivators. Gradually adapting to the environment of Captive Peak, he steadily turned the situation around. Seeing this, the morale of the Great Yu Cultivators soared, their fierce state not the slightest bit inferior to that of the Great Qian Cultivators!

Wu Yao had now come to terms with the situation; there was no need to be so negative. Why couldn’t the environment of Captive Peak be a form of tempering? Tempering both mind and body, polishing both the internal and external.

Through their exchange, Wu Yao gradually came to understand the Dao Fruit philosophy of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord.

Mortal martial artists often speak of making friends through the martial arts, communicating through the Martial Dao; it was only natural for cultivators to do the same, let alone Wu Yao, who stood at the pinnacle of cultivators as an Immortal.

The philosophy of Devouring Dao Fruit of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord carried the grandeur of one who ruled over all, something Wu Yao did not possess. If it were the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord who took over the Candle Sky Civilization, he would make it rule the universe, rather than plundering everywhere.

Wu Yao was a person adept at learning; otherwise, he would not have mastered the Hundred Skills of Cultivation. He was good at learning, capable of recognizing and correcting his mistakes, reflecting upon himself, realizing just how many deficiencies he had.

Having shortcomings isn’t scary; one simply needs to correct them!

But before making those corrections, it was time to take down this old antique!

Wu Yao threw a punch straight at the facade of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, who swayed and staggered, stepping out of place to leverage the force.

The Great Qian and Great Yu Cultivators all consciously stayed away from the center of the Immortal Battle to avoid being affected.

...

Lu Yang said that he was going into seclusion for a breakthrough, and it was not just an excuse. He gained a lot from his battle with Wu Yao, feeling his realm loosen up, and gaining a deeper understanding of the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype.

The battle was far from being as effortless as it seemed. Wu Yao was a formidable opponent, exerting great pressure on Lu Yang, who intended to use this force to temper himself and achieve a breakthrough.

In fact, he did just that.

Lu Yang assumed the Five Centers Toward Heaven posture, sitting cross-legged like a Buddha statue, immobile, impervious to external distractions that could shake his Dao heart.

One by one, top-grade Spirit Stones were drained, rapidly consumed, turning into empty husks, and soon a small mountain of hollowed top-grade Spirit Stones piled up.

Immortal Fairy yawned, feeling that Little Yang would take a long time to break through this time. She had also expended a lot of energy while cheering and waving the flag during Little Yang’s fight with Wu Yao.

She hugged her pillow drowsily, said goodnight, and fell asleep.

Lu Yang sensed Immortal Fairy’s movements, opened his eyes, said goodnight as well, and continued to seclude himself.

Time passed unknowingly, and Immortal Fairy sensed a change in Little Yang, stretching lazily as she woke up. She noticed a thick layer of dust covering Little Yang, indicating that he had been in seclusion for a long time.

Following that, it was as if time flowed backward around Lu Yang, the dust swirling upwards, returning to its original place. At the same time, a world-shattering aura surged. It was Lu Yang breaking through; now he was multitasking, making a breakthrough in his realm while also being able to stimulate the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype, a sign that his understanding of the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype had deepened.

The Abode shook continuously, washed over by the aura. Had it not been for the Array Method that had been set up in advance, it would have been shattered by now.

Eventually, the world-shattering aura faded, and Lu Yang slowly exhaled and inhaled, his demeanor faintly bearing traces of the Immortal Dao.

After an indeterminable amount of time, Lu Yang opened his eyes and stood up, to the applause of Immortal Fairy.

"Congratulations, congratulations, you’ve reached the Late Tribulation Stage, Little Yang is truly impressive."

Chapter 1327: 1326: Where is Yun Zhi’s Hometown?

Breaking through to the Late Tribulation Stage, Lu Yang felt no great fluctuation in his emotions, having experienced the immense power of this stage countless times before. He needed no adaptation; he could control this power immediately upon breakthrough, even stronger than those veterans of the Late Tribulation Stage.

The most satisfying part of this breakthrough was the change in the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype, which was vaguely hinting at consummation, making its use far more efficient and fluid.

Lu Yang walked out of his abode, and what entered his view was a sea of flowers. He remembered these were the flower fields planted by Yun Mengmeng and Qinghe, covering the entirety of Sky Gate Mountain with twelve flower patches in total, always blooming whenever he exited seclusion to celebrate his breakthrough.

Yun Mengmeng lay in the flower field, legs crossed, humming an unnamed tune, relaxed and comfortable.

Hearing the movements from the abode, she flipped up from the ground energetically. Although she clearly lay amidst the flowers, her clothes were as fresh as new, not a speck of dirt on them - if anything, she only carried the scent of flowers.

"Second-in-command, you’ve made another breakthrough, and you’ve been closed off for quite a while this time."

"Has it been long?"

"Yes, it’s been almost half a year, and there have been big changes in the outside world."

Lu Yang noticed that Yun Mengmeng held a flat device in her hands, which she was looking at while lying on the ground.

"What’s this?"

"This? This is a Quantum Communication Device, the latest model. It can make calls, video calls, and even connect to the internet." Yun Mengmeng had recently taken a liking to this device; there were many fun things online.

"Look at the livestream I’m watching."

Yun Mengmeng activated the Quantum Communication Device, and on the screen appeared Ancestor Guiyuan.

Ancestor Guiyuan and the other three founders of the Immortal Sects stood with Great Snow Mountain behind them, and the backdrop was the cosmic starry sky.

"Daoist friends, hit the follow button. If the follower count exceeds fifty billion, I’ll tell you how the Guiyi Tao Fruit Prototype was comprehended."

The follower count was visibly increasing, rewards on the screen flooded in, and people were kneeling to request other Semi-Immortals to show their faces and give lectures; the atmosphere was lively.

"Who is this old man, why so many followers?"

"Seeking answers. This account is just three days old. How could it have so many followers? Did they buy them?"

"This Daoist is not from our Cultivation World, right? This is a real big shot of our Cultivation World. Search ’Celestial Master Guiyuan’ online; it will certainly amaze you."

"...What is the Ancestor up to?"

"He’s livestreaming their exploration of the universe."

Lu Yang looked bemused. No matter how he looked, Great Snow Mountain felt familiar - wasn’t that the one behind Sky Gate Mountain where Chasing Moon True Person lived?

He turned his head toward the back mountains; the space was empty, Great Snow Mountain had disappeared.

Yun Mengmeng scrolled further, and there was a livestream by the Transcendence Tribulation Stage Supreme Elder of Control Qi Sect.

"As the number of viewers in the livestream room exceeds ten million, I will take you inside the sun for a tour. Oops, the communicator melted..."

The livestream was interrupted.

"This is called the internet, transferred here from the Interstellar Alliance. Oh right, Second-in-command, you were the first one to go to the Interstellar Alliance; you know about these."

"Now, the Cultivation World, Interstellar Alliance, and Candle Sky Civilization are all connected to the internet."

Lu Yang touched his forehead, torn between laughter and tears. It seemed the Cultivation World had made big strides in development, and these old forerunners were quite open to embracing these novelties, even enjoying themselves.

In the past, his seclusion periods were short, and upon reentry, he noticed no significant changes outside; everything remained as it used to be. This time around, coming out of seclusion, he finally understood what the predecessors meant when they said "the world transforms dramatically, and everything changes."

"Is Eldest Senior Sister around?"

"A few days ago, Xiao Zhi went back to her native home; she hasn’t returned yet."

"Where is Miss Yun’s hometown, anyway?" Immortal Fairy asked, always hearing about Miss Yun going back to her hometown, but never knowing where it actually was.

Immortal Fairy was very curious about Yun Zhi’s hometown, wanting to know what kind of remarkable place could have nurtured such a prodigy like Yun Zhi.

"It’s in Shu State..." Yun Mengmeng replied instinctively, then quickly covered her mouth. Xiao Zhi had repeatedly warned her not to reveal the location of their hometown, or else she would be in trouble.

Yun Mengmeng covered her mouth and vigorously shook her head, whining something that, because her mouth was covered, Lu Yang couldn’t hear clearly, but it seemed to be something like "I didn’t say anything."

"Oh, I can’t say?" Immortal Fairy smirked, feeling that digging deeper into this matter would be interesting.

"I command you as the Chief Housekeeper, speak!"

Yun Mengmeng continued to shake her head vigorously, torn between Xiao Zhi and the Chief Housekeeper.

"The Four Ancient Immortals have come back, why don’t you go listen to their travel achievements, Chief Housekeeper?"

"There’s no hurry, it’s just showing off in front of people. Where is Miss Yun’s hometown?"

"The two immortals of Great Xia also came back."

"Oh, is that so? But where is Miss Yun’s hometown?"

Yun Mengmeng tried to divert the topic, but Immortal Fairy, whose curiosity was piqued, was not so easily appeased.

"Fairy, don’t make it hard for Sister Mengmeng, can’t you see she doesn’t want to speak?" Lu Yang stepped in to help Yun Mengmeng.

"Hey, Little Yang, whose side are you on!" Immortal Fairy crossed her arms and glared, having almost persuaded Yun Mengmeng to reveal the location.

"Everyone here is family, there aren’t sides."

"Don’t you want to know where Miss Yun’s hometown is?"

Lu Yang fell silent for a moment; he too was curious about where Eldest Senior Sister’s hometown was.

"...I do want to know, but if Sister Mengmeng doesn’t want to say, don’t make it difficult for her. Fairy, if you really want to know, just wait for Eldest Senior Sister to come back and ask her."

Immortal Fairy huffed and turned her head away, knowing that asking Miss Yun would surely result in no answer.

Seeing the two argue, Yun Mengmeng didn’t know how to handle such a situation, feeling flustered. The Second-in-command clearly wanted to help her by directly opposing the Chief Housekeeper.

The Second-in-command was so good to her; should she reciprocate somehow?

She bit her lip lightly, and finally made up her mind, stammering, "Actually, I can take you to my hometown, my mother-in-law will definitely welcome you."

...

Peach Blossom Secret Realm.

This is Yun Zhi and Yun Mengmeng’s hometown, filled with peach trees and fragrant peach blossoms, resembling a mortal’s vision of paradise.

Dust-free Eldest Senior Sister visits her mother-in-law.

Although called mother-in-law, her appearance was of a woman in her early twenties, sitting with Yun Zhi, they looked less like elder and junior, more like sisters.

The mother-in-law set down the Enlightenment Tea, smiling warmly, "Sounds like there have been many changes outside, the Cultivation World has opened, entering the actual universe, even witnessing several Immortal Battles happen."

"Mother-in-law knows you’re very capable, but you must be careful out there, don’t let anyone hurt you."

Yun Zhi nodded noncommittally, unsure whether to say ’I’ll be careful’ or ’No one can hurt me.’

"Speaking of which, that boy Lu Yang you often mention sounds very interesting, and seems to have a good relationship with Mengmeng, when will you bring him here for me to meet?"

"It’s not that I don’t want to bring him, but the Junior Brother is diligently cultivating, often in seclusion, and can’t find the time to come over."

Chapter 1328: 1327: Does Eldest Senior Sister Often Mention Me?

"Often in secluded cultivation?" The grandma was somewhat surprised as she took a bite of an unknown fruit, rumored to be a specialty from some Food Planet.

"Aren’t you saying a lot of changes in the outside world are related to that little guy named Lu Yang? He seems to be very active in the outside world. How can someone like him often be in secluded cultivation?"

Yun Zhi remained composed as she explained, "Actually, Junior Brother is a very honest person; he was involuntarily dragged into those incidents, it’s not his intention."

"I just picked out some interesting things to talk about; he spends most of his time in secluded cultivation."

"He’s in a critical period of cultivation progress. It’s time for him to focus on cultivation. If he slacks off, that momentum will dissipate, which is not conducive for his future cultivation."

"So that’s how it is." The grandma suddenly understood; this Lu Yang really is an honest and down-to-earth kid.

Yun Zhi sighed in relief, having managed to appease the grandma for now.

Every time she came home, she would inevitably talk about interesting changes in the outside world, and that would always arouse the grandma’s curiosity about Junior Brother.

The two of them continued to chat about various household matters; time flew by.

Just when grandma started talking about how Yun Zhi used to secretly read under the covers as a child, making Yun Zhi a bit embarrassed, a bustling noise came from outside.

"What’s happening?" The grandma wondered, successfully distracted, and went to check outside.

"It must be Mengmeng returning; it’s always lively whenever she comes back."

Yun Zhi also stood up and walked out with the grandma, only to see a flock of beautiful women surrounding and endlessly gawking at Yun Mengmeng and Lu Yang.

Yun Zhi: "..."

She rarely showed a trace of perplexity, blinking to confirm she wasn’t seeing things.

Why is Junior Brother here?

"Do you know the person who came in with Mengmeng?" The grandma noticed Yun Zhi’s unusual expression.

"It’s Lu Yang."

"He is Lu Yang?" The grandma laughed, finding it quite coincidental. She was just talking about Lu Yang, and there he was.

Although Yun Mengmeng had warned him before entering the Secret Realm that the people there would be enthusiastic, Lu Yang hadn’t expected them to be this eager.

At that moment, Lu Yang was extremely embarrassed; no one had informed him beforehand that this place was all females—it was like they had never seen a man before, tugging at him as if he were a giant panda.

"Esteemed Daoist, please don’t tug at my clothes."

"We don’t have physical contact between men and women, don’t, don’t touch me." Lu Yang found his cultivation level utterly useless at the moment and could only feebly protect himself with words.

Lu Yang was quite puzzled. Why were all these females in Eldest Senior Sister’s hometown?

However, the situation and setting left no room for further thought.

"Hey, hey, stop touching."

Lu Yang turned to Yun Mengmeng for help, but Yun Mengmeng was also powerless against her own family members and ended up being surrounded as a victim too.

"Who is this guy, Mengmeng?"

"What’s your relationship with him?"

"How have I been teaching you all this time? Is this how you treat a guest? Disperse!" The grandma reprimanded, her stern tone making the women back away.

"So you must be Xiao Yang," the grandma said with a friendly smile.

"Grandma, you’re finally here!" Yun Mengmeng, seeing the grandma as if spotting a savior, quickly stood by her side.

"Second-in-command, let me introduce you, this is the Clan Leader of our Yun Clan."

"My name is Yun Bai, everyone calls me grandma."

"Nice to meet you, grandma." Lu Yang followed suit, noticing the silent, seemingly displeased Eldest Senior Sister standing beside him and began to regret whether he should have been beguiled by the Fairy to come here.

Lu Yang quietly moved closer to Grandma, as doing so gave him a sense of security.

"You child, why don’t you greet others when you see them?" Grandma chided.

"Junior Brother, come into the house."

This was the first time Lu Yang saw such an obedient Yun Zhi, a stark contrast to her usual demeanor, and the thought made him shiver uncontrollably.

Everyone entered and took a seat in the house, and Grandma personally poured tea for Lu Yang, the guest.

"Have a taste, this is our Peach Blossom Secret Realm’s specialty, Peach Blossom Tea."

Curled dry petals of peach blossoms unfolded and bloomed inside the cup, releasing a gentle aroma of peach blossoms. This scent was more pleasing than any peach fragrance Lu Yang had ever encountered—not because it was more fragrant, but because it was extremely comforting to smell.

Outside Grandma’s doorstep, female cultivators were peeking over the threshold, eager to look inside. Due to Grandma’s imposing presence, they dared not enter and could only stand there.

"Don’t push, don’t push, I was nearly squeezed inside."

"Who’s this person Sister Mengmeng brought? He seems very familiar with both Sister Mengmeng and Sister Xiao Zhi."

"Didn’t they say? It’s Lu Yang, Sister Xiao Zhi’s Junior Brother."

"Keep it down, I can’t hear what they’re saying anymore."

"Xiao Zhi often mentions you, and seeing you today, you really do look like an individual of remarkable talent."

For some reason, sitting here made Lu Yang feel particularly restrained, as if he had become the center of attention.

He cautiously held the teacup as if the tea was too hot, blowing on it gently before taking small sips and savoring the flavor, completely concealing the fact that he was a Great Cultivator in the Late Tribulation Stage.

I must say, this Peach Blossom Tea is indeed delicious.

Hearing Grandma’s words, Lu Yang’s hand trembled slightly as he held his teacup: "Eldest Senior Sister often talks about me?"

"Yes, she praises you for being talented at a young age, hardworking, diligent in your cultivation, and often in secluded cultivation."

Lu Yang felt inexplicably guilty. Is she really talking about me? So this is the image Eldest Senior Sister has of me.

"Should I be like that?"

Lu Yang stealthily observed Eldest Senior Sister’s reaction and saw her sternly warning him with her eyes not to talk nonsense.

Lu Yang straightened his back with effort: "Ah yes, that’s me, cultivation is great, it can increase Cultivation Level. I particularly like to cultivate."

"Yes, Second-in-command is very diligent in his cultivation," Yun Mengmeng interjected. "He’s reached the Late Tribulation Stage and even has a Dao Fruit Outline."

Grandma was somewhat shocked but soon her expression changed to one of admiration: "Late Tribulation Stage, not bad, really not bad."

"Actually, I have wanted to come here for a long time..." Lu Yang stopped mid-sentence, realizing he had almost blurted out his genuine thoughts, and hastily corrected himself.

"What I mean is, right after I finished seclusion, I asked Sister Mengmeng to bring me to see you."

"I also often hear Eldest Senior Sister mentioning you, Grandma. She says her accomplishments today cannot be separated from your nurturing since she was young."

"Nonsense, it’s mostly due to Xiao Zhi’s own talent."

"You’re too modest. You not only nurtured Eldest Senior Sister but also Sister Mengmeng. If anyone says it’s a coincidence, I will be the first to object," Lu Yang said righteously.

The more Grandma listened, the happier she became. This child Lu Yang is truly commendable, no wonder Xiao Zhi speaks so highly of him.

Yun Zhi fixed Yun Mengmeng with a cold stare that would have made anyone else tremble with fear.

Yun Mengmeng did not tremble, but she dared not meet Yun Zhi’s gaze and could only tattle.

"Grandma, look at Xiao Zhi, she’s glaring at me."

"Is that so?" Grandma turned to look at Yun Zhi.

With an unchanged expression and an indifferent tone, Yun Zhi responded, "Not at all, my eyes have always been like this."

Chapter 1329: 1328: Heavenly Spirit Root

"Mengmeng is timid, don’t scare her," the grandmother warned a couple of times before turning around with a smile on her face.

Lu Yang unconsciously sat up straight, not looking like a guest, but more like a criminal on trial. When had he ever seen Eldest Senior Sister speak in such a gentle tone?

Even though her words were still quite cold compared to others, they were noticeably softer than the Eldest Senior Sister’s usual tone.

"Look at me, joking around. Mengmeng told me that you were the one taking care of her in the outside world, and she often brags about you when she comes back home."

"Mengmeng is indecisive, kind-hearted, and easily frightened. When she insisted on leaving the Secret Realm, I’ve been worried ever since."

"Thank goodness she met you right after she left. Otherwise, I really would have been afraid someone might have tricked her."

Lu Yang responded with a smile, "Sister Mengmeng is naive and adorable. Who wouldn’t like her? I guess even the bad guys wouldn’t have the heart to deceive her."

Yun Mengmeng, listening on the side, giggled non-stop.

"It must be tough to be Xiao Zhi’s junior brother, huh?"

Pfft—

While drinking peach blossom tea, Lu Yang unexpectedly spat it out. Why did the conversation suddenly turn to this topic?

"What’s the matter?" the grandmother asked, somewhat surprised. Why such a big reaction?

Lu Yang waved his hands repeatedly, "No, no, it’s nothing. The tea was hot, I drank too quickly."

Yun Mengmeng quickly ran over with a handkerchief to wipe Lu Yang’s mouth and gently pat his back.

"Eldest Senior Sister—in public she’s always so caring. She’s saved me several times, I can’t thank her enough."

Everyone here calls Eldest Senior Sister "Xiao Zhi," which almost led Lu Yang astray.

Seeing Lu Yang was fine, the grandmother continued, "Xiao Zhi has always been different from others since she was young. While the other kids played with beanbags, she read books. While they climbed trees, she would still be reading books. Only Mengmeng played with her a lot. Mengmeng took so much effort to push her up a tree, and she’d still be reading."

"Xiao Zhi isn’t emotionless; it’s just that she’s not good at expressing herself. She keeps things hidden and insists on handling everything herself, not wanting others to worry."

"Yes," Lu Yang nodded in agreement. Eldest Senior Sister was great in every aspect but just reluctant to talk. She wouldn’t even discuss something as significant as Harmonization, insisting on dealing with it alone.

Mid-nod, Lu Yang suddenly felt a dangerous gaze upon him, making his hair stand on end.

With only a few people present and considering the current topic, the source of that feeling was obvious to Lu Yang.

The grandmother wasn’t aware of Yun Zhi’s subtle actions and went on to say, "With such a reliable junior brother like you to help her share some pressure, my heart is at ease."

"Grandmother, let’s eat," Yun Zhi gently suggested, sitting quietly on the side.

The grandmother slapped her forehead, "Oh right, I’ve been babbling for so long, and I’ve only given you tea to drink. Come on, let’s have our meal."

"Right, Second-in-command, let’s go eat," Yun Mengmeng said with high spirits, taking Lu Yang along to the center of the Peach Blossom Secret Realm.

Lu Yang was the first guest to visit the Peach Blossom Secret Realm in nearly a thousand years, and the grandmother had already instructed her people to prepare the meal. During their chat, the food was almost ready.

The gigantic peach tree cast a cloud-like canopy over the center of the Peach Blossom Secret Realm, creating a cool shade.

The Yun Family members set tables, laid out dishes, and poured fruit juices and wines. Approaching this area, Lu Yang could smell an alluring fragrance and felt his Cultivation Level rising imperceptibly.

As of now, Lu Yang was truly in the Late Tribulation Stage, with an astonishing need for Spiritual Energy. To be able to sniff and thus increase his Cultivation Level, one can only imagine how extraordinary this fragrance is.

"No wonder all the inhabitants here are Cultivators." Lu Yang had already noticed that all members of the Yun Family were Cultivators, and their Cultivation Levels were not low.

Living in such a peculiar place, it would be strange if their Cultivation Levels were low.

Lu Yang followed the fragrance and saw that it was emitted by the most conspicuous peach tree.

Lu Yang had never seen such a sturdy peach tree before; he would believe it if someone told him it was hundreds of thousands of years old. The only thing comparable to the size of this peach tree was the true form of the Time Immortal, the Firmament Tree buried below the Five Elements Sect.

One by one, the plump and tender peaches hung from the branches, bending them with their weight. The members of the Yun Family looked like Fairies, flying in the air with their dresses billowing.

They used Jade Ruyi to knock down the peaches and caught them with bamboo baskets, placing them on the banquet table to serve as the main dish of the day.

The members of the Yun Family noticed Lu Yang’s arrival and sneak glances his way while they worked.

As everyone took their seats, there was no distinction of status. Everyone sat casually, and the food was the same for all.

"Try this; these peaches are unique to our Secret Realm and can’t be found outside. I don’t know if Xiao Zhi and Mengmeng have let you taste them before."

Lu Yang wore a weird expression. Of course, he had tasted them before. These peaches leave an unforgettable memory after just one encounter. Aren’t these the Immortal Peach fruits from the Heavenly Root, capable of significantly extending one’s lifespan? During the battle in the Imperial City, he used an Immortal Peach to save Emperor Xia and lured Guan Shanhai out.

Later on, he learned that Emperor Xia was faking illness; it was unnecessary to eat the Immortal Peach. On the way back, he and Eldest Senior Sister divided the Immortal Peach between themselves, even making Fairy Eternity envious as she watched.

But the problem was, didn’t Eldest Senior Sister say that she stumbled upon this Immortal Peach by chance? As soon as one was plucked, the Heavenly Root disappeared.

So how come this Heavenly Root is growing perfectly fine in Eldest Senior Sister’s hometown?

Lu Yang sneakily glanced at Eldest Senior Sister, trying to observe her reaction. Yun Zhi was chatting with Yun Mengmeng. Noticing Lu Yang’s gaze, she tilted her head and said, "Junior Brother, what are you staring at me for? Eat."

For some reason, Lu Yang felt that Eldest Senior Sister was different today.

It was as if she was trying hard to act as usual.

The Yun Family has been in control of the Peach Blossom Secret Realm for a hundred thousand years, having thoroughly researched all the ways to eat peaches. The banquet also featured delicacies brought by Yun Zhi from Food Planet, elevating the taste of the Peach Banquet by a notch. Lu Yang thoroughly enjoyed his meal.

Occasionally, Immortal Fairy would Possess Lu Yang and taste the food, lavishing praise.

Then there were the Yun Family members, who looked as beautiful as immortals, starting to dance halfway through eating, with graceful and captivating movements.

Rather than dancing for Lu Yang, it seemed more like they simply enjoyed dancing themselves.

Yun Mengmeng, itching to join, also danced for a while, receiving a round of applause and creating a joyous atmosphere.

"Immortal, aren’t you curious about why Eldest Senior Sister claimed to not know where the Heavenly Root is?" Lu Yang covertly asked in the Spiritual Space.

"Oh, don’t think too much about it." Fairy Eternity gestured dismissively, seeming exceptionally understanding, "Miss Yun must have her reasons for doing so; we need to be understanding of her."

"Huh?" Lu Yang was puzzled. Wasn’t the Immortal’s curiosity usually very high? What happened today?

"Immortal, leave some for me." Seeing that Immortal Fairy was in a good mood, as she was about to wipe clean the dishes on the table, Lu Yang spoke up.

As the banquet approached its end, Lu Yang leaned in close to Granny and asked the question that had troubled him since he entered the Secret Realm.

"Granny, why are all the members of our Secret Realm women?"

Chapter 1330: 1329: The Roots of Yun Zhi

As soon as the question was asked, Lu Yang suddenly felt the approach of death, but upon more careful feeling, there was nothing; it seemed like an illusion, with only Eldest Senior Sister coming over to pour peach juice for him.

"Junior Brother, you’ve been talking a lot, drink some peach juice."

Then Yun Zhi naturally sat down next to Lu Yang, with unclear intentions.

Lu Yang always felt that there was more to what Eldest Senior Sister was saying, but soon the answer from the elderly lady shifted his attention.

"Our Yun Family has continued to this day, thanks largely to the Immortal River."

"Immortal River?"

"Ten thousand years ago, in the Late Period of Great Yu, amidst unceasing wars, our ancestors sought to escape the chaos and preserve our clan members, and unwittingly found this secret realm after several moves."

"This Secret Realm was full of peach blossoms, so our ancestors named it Peach Blossom Secret Realm."

The grandmother pointed to the Heavenly Root, "In the Secret Realm, we discovered this Immortal Peach Tree which can extend lifespan and increase cultivation level."

"Moreover, there is also an Immortal River in the Secret Realm, said to be a creation by an Immortal from Ancient Times."

"Drinking the river water can give birth to a female baby, who naturally possesses Spirit Root with at least a medium level of cultivation talent."

"Gradually, the Yun Family became a predominantly female clan, and over ten thousand years, no men remained."

"There’s also a stone stele by the Immortal River, but unfortunately, it’s blurry and incomplete, without records of which Immortal created the river," sighed the grandmother, meaning they could not even worship the Immortal.

Lu Yang’s expression grew increasingly strange; so, both Eldest Senior Sister and Sister Mengmeng were born through the Immortal River?

And the most crucial thing was, why did the effects of the Immortal River sound so familiar to him?

Lu Yang suddenly realized everything – why Eldest Senior Sister behaved so abnormally today, why she didn’t mention that the Heavenly Root was in her hometown.

It was all because she was afraid of the Immortal Fairy finding out that the Heavenly Root was here, insisting on visiting her hometown.

Once the Immortal Fairy arrived, Eldest Senior Sister’s origins would be exposed!

To Lu Yang at this moment, the beautiful Peach Blossom Secret Realm seemed dark, filled with risks, a place not suitable to stay long!

He mechanically turned his head to look at Eldest Senior Sister, who still wore her usual expressionless and cold demeanor, seemingly no different from usual, but Lu Yang felt she was very frightening now.

As Yun Mengmeng finished dancing and noticed Xiao Zhi, Second-in-command, and the grandmother all present, she also joined them: "Second-in-command, why are you sweating so much?"

Saying so, she wiped the sweat from Lu Yang’s forehead with a handkerchief.

Lu Yang forced a smile, indicating to Yun Mengmeng not to worry about him: "It’s nothing, I think it’s about time for us to leave."

"What are you talking about leaving?" the grandmother interjected, holding Lu Yang back, earnestly wanting him to stay longer.

"Grandmother, the Junior Brother needs to cultivate."

The grandmother looked slightly displeased: "You’ve just arrived not long ago; cultivating is not so urgent that it can’t wait for a day or two. Cultivation also needs moderation."

"Come, grandmother will take you to see the Immortal River," sensing Lu Yang’s interest in the Immortal River, she abruptly pulled him to go.

"Second-in-command wants to see the Immortal River too? Let’s go, let’s go," Yun Mengmeng called out to Eldest Senior Sister before leaving.

"I’d rather not go," Eldest Senior Sister hesitated, not very keen to follow.

"Come on, let’s go," Yun Mengmeng insistently dragged her along.

The Immortal River meandered like a dragon lying in the Secret Realm, with river channels everywhere; while attending the peach blossom banquet, Lu Yang had noticed the Immortal River, but he hadn’t thought much about it, just feeling this river was filled with an abundance of Spiritual Energy, extraordinary. Given it was Eldest Senior Sister’s hometown, having such miraculous entity was not surprising.

"We’ve arrived," the grandmother led Lu Yang to see the Immortal River and the stone stele beside it.

The stream babbles softly, if not for the intense Spiritual Energy it exuded, no one would connect this small creek with the Immortal River, much less think of its magical effect where drinking its water could produce female offspring.

The stone stele has weathered the vicissitudes of time, even someone ignorant of history can tell that this stele is horrifyingly ancient, older than the histories of the three great dynasties, a relic from the Ancient Times.

The stone stele is engraved with the real name of the Immortal River in ancient script — Mother-child River.

As an expert in Ancient History and ancient script, Lu Yang, at this moment, became illiterate and could not recognize the characters on the stone stele; his only thought was to leave this place.

"Hey, this river looks really familiar." The discordant voice pronounced Lu Yang’s death sentence, behind him, a shadow appeared faintly, as if it had existed through the ages.

Lu Yang’s expression turned to one of despair, Is it still possible to use the Playing Dead Technique now?

Or, is the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype capable of reversing my memory, pretending this never happened?

Lu Yang trembled severely, the shameful ancient past didn’t claim his life, and now was it time for the Eldest Senior Sister to claim it?

The old lady revealed a surprised look: "Are you the Ancient Immortal Fairy Eternity that Xiao Zhi and Mengmeng spoke of?"

"Indeed, it is I." Fairy Eternity puffed out her chest, her expression as proud as could be.

The old lady’s heart stirred: "Immortal just mentioned that this Immortal River looks familiar? Could it be that the Immortal knows the origin of this Immortal River?"

"Let me see." Fairy Eternity floated out, carefully examining the Immortal River while taking an interest in observing Yun Zhi’s reactions.

The moment she entered the Secret Realm, she knew what this place was — the familiar Heavenly Root, the familiar Mother-child River, she finally understood why Miss Yun had prevented her from entering the Secret Realm.

But this act of challenging Miss Yun was somewhat dangerous, she couldn’t initiate it on her own.

However, when Little Yang did it on her behalf, the nature of the act was completely different.

Deserving indeed of being her own Little Yang, she didn’t even need her hint to do the job, worthy of commendation.

Fairy Eternity circled around Yun Zhi, carefully observing Yun Zhi’s reactions, unwilling to miss any small detail.

Yun Zhi’s face was expressionless, but the faint blush that rose on her cheeks betrayed her inner thoughts.

Seeing this, Fairy Eternity’s smile grew wider, irrepressible.

"What is the origin of this Immortal River again?"

Fairy Eternity crossed her arms, thinking hard: "Ah, I can’t quite remember."

"The Immortal doesn’t remember?" The old lady had thought she would learn the origins of the Mother-child River this time.

Once Fairy Eternity came out, it wasn’t that easy to send her back, she still hovered in the air, circling Yun Zhi.

She and Yun Zhi holding leverage over each other, naturally could not disclose the origins of the Mother-child River, but that didn’t prevent her from seizing the opportunity to interrogate Yun Zhi well.

"Come Miss Yun, what should you call me?"

"Fairy Senior." Yun Zhi said quietly, bowing her head as if afraid Fairy could hear clearly.

Fairy Eternity cupped one ear, tilting her head and leaning towards Yun Zhi, laughing with her mouth wide open: "Ah, you still call me senior, give you another chance, what should you call me?"

Her lips trembled, unable to voice the words at her lips.

Lu Yang watched, dumbstruck, an arrogant Fairy, a shy Eldest Senior Sister, am I supposed to be seeing this?

Yun Zhi pulled out a slip of paper, handing it to Lu Yang.

"Junior Brother, this is the coordinates of your hometown I found from the Candle Sky Civilization."

Chapter 1331: 1330: Will of Zombie Planet

"Huh? Coordinates of my homeland?" Lu Yang was momentarily stunned by Yun Zhi’s sudden remark, unprepared to process this information.

"Yep." The blush hadn’t completely faded from Yun Zhi’s pretty face, and her voice was very soft.

It was during the time when Lu Yang was in seclusion that Yun Zhi, having visited the Candle Sky Civilization, found the coordinates of Blue Star in historical records. She had planned to tell Lu Yang after his retreat, but he immediately went to the Peach Blossom Secret Realm after leaving seclusion, upsetting Yun Zhi’s plan.

Now, Yun Zhi just wanted to hurry Junior Brother and Fairy Eternity to Blue Star, rather than stay in the Secret Realm any longer.

Lu Yang, holding the small piece of paper with the coordinates for Blue Star, looked somewhat dazed, not expecting to suddenly learn the location of his homeland.

"So this is Little Yang’s home," Fairy Eternity, as expected, shifted her attention and, no longer teasing Yun Zhi, flew over to Lu Yang’s side.

Due to the universe’s celestial bodies being in constant motion, the location of Blue Star is not fixed coordinates but rather a set of equations. By plugging in the current time, one can compute the present position of Blue Star.

This is a common method used by cosmic civilizations to mark coordinates, which the Cultivation World learned and adopted after entering the universe.

"Then let’s go, back to your homeland for a look." Fairy Eternity waved her hand excitedly, always having heard Little Yang talk about it, she was quite curious about his homeland.

As for Miss Yun, she couldn’t possibly escape now.

Fairy Eternity couldn’t help but want to laugh when recalling Yun Zhi’s flustered appearance.

Ah, exhilarating. After contending with Miss Yun in a battle of wits for so long, she had finally won for once.

"Second-in-command, are you leaving already?" Yun Mengmeng felt a little reluctant, sensing that Lu Yang hadn’t been in the Secret Realm for long and was about to leave.

She was somewhat puzzled, where was Second-in-command’s home? Wasn’t it in the Cultivation World? This all seemed to suggest it was a faraway place.

"Sister Mengmeng, you and Eldest Senior Sister should stay in our hometown and keep grandmother company. I’ll go back to my homeland for a visit," Lu Yang said with a smile.

"Okay."

Lu Yang smiled as he said his goodbyes to everyone from the Yun Family, leaving the Peach Blossom Secret Realm.

The entrance of the Peach Blossom Secret Realm wasn’t fixed, changing constantly; at this moment, it was situated beneath a lake in Shu State.

This particular lake was fairly famous locally, attracting many tourists each year. Today, a local poetry gathering was organized by the lake. They composed and recited poetry, with the hope of leaving their names in the county records.

After the impromptu poetry recitation, praises were sung.

"Brilliant poem, Brother Wang, truly! Hearing this poem, I feel as if my literary bottleneck is loosening," someone commended.

"You flatter me, it was merely an improvised light verse," Brother Wang said, unable to contain his big smile despite knowing it was just flattery.

Suddenly, with a loud splash, the surface of the lake burst open, sending a hundred-foot-high spray of water into the air. A figure shot out, elegant and unrestrained, accompanied by a hearty laughter, vanishing without a trace, startling the gathered literary men.

"Who was that person?"

"It looked like Lu Yang to me."

"I thought it looked like him too."

"Lu Yang? What’s he doing here? And why was he laughing?"

"Could there be a treasure underneath the lake?"

The scholars discussed excitedly, no longer focused on the poetry gathering. This was Lu Yang, after all—a Great Power practitioner from the Cultivation World—who could provide them with a chance that could last a lifetime with just a hint of a fortune.

Thinking this, they dived into the lake, but finding nothing, they reported the event to the County Magistrate.

The local county records documented the incident: Poets at the gathering saw Sword Immortal Lu Yang laugh as he soared from Chongyang Lake. Upon hearing this, the County Magistrate joyfully investigated but found nothing. Afterwards, several Divinity Transformation Stage cultivators, upon learning of this, also came to Chongyang Lake, but in the end, they found nothing as well.

Unaware that his unintentional action would cause such a stir, Lu Yang steered the Black Canopy Boat and flew to outer space.

Thanks to Vast Sea Daoist Lord, several permanent spatial passages have been established between the Cultivation World and the Interstellar Alliance, as well as between the Cultivation World and the Candle Sky Civilization, transforming journeys of several months into mere days and greatly facilitating offline interactions between these regions.

Lu Yang also benefited from these conveniences and soon arrived at the Candle Sky Civilization.

Geographically speaking, Blue Star is closer to the Candle Sky Civilization.
