As he neared his homecoming, Lu Yang felt a mix of nostalgia and excitement.

His experiences on Blue Star were equally extraordinary. The sudden zombie crisis plunged the entire city into danger. Lu Yang struggled for survival in this apocalyptic world, escaping the clutches of zombies time after time. During this period, he rescued a "Doctor" who was investigating the source of the zombie crisis and found out that he himself was the original cause; the hospital director converted him into the first zombie, but he was special, having retained his rationality.

Due to his uniqueness, he gained the ability to control zombies. With this power, he established a safe zone, sheltering a portion of humanity.

Later, both the will of Blue Star and that of the Zombie Planet approached him, each attempting to court him to their side. He chose neither, instead traveling to challenge Corpse Kings across various locations, ruling over all zombies. During this time, he handed the leadership of the safe zone to the Doctor.

After defeating all the Corpse Kings and subjugating all zombies, the Candle Sky Civilization attacked with a spacecraft. At that time, the Candle Sky Civilization was not as powerful as it is now, and they did not place much importance on Blue Star, sending only one spacecraft and a few smaller vessels.

Even so, this still spelled disaster for Blue Star. Fortunately, with his control over both humankind and zombies, he managed to defeat the Candle Sky Civilization’s spacecraft.

He then commandeered the Candle Sky Civilization’s spacecraft and headed to a planet covered in Spirit Stones, the source of the Candle Sky Civilization’s power.

There, he engaged in fierce combat with the Candle Sky Civilization, learning that the Zombie Planet’s will was actually sent by the Candle Sky Civilization to create chaos with the zombie virus, simplifying their planned invasion.

In the end, to stop the Candle Sky Civilization, he sacrificed himself along with all the Spirit Stone mines, and upon reopening his eyes, he was reincarnated into the Cultivation World.

Coincidentally, in both his past and present lives, he was named Lu Yang.

Though in terms of power levels, his past experiences are incomparable to those in the Cultivation World, they are still memories he cannot forget.

With these thoughts, he did not immediately return to Blue Star but chose to stay in the Candle Sky Civilization for a while. There was still an enemy here.

A graceful and attractive girl was passing through the streets, preparing to go home.

"Gu Yu, you’re back so early." Neighbors greeted the girl with smiles, indicating her good reputation in the vicinity.

"Yes, the bread sold out early today, so I’m home earlier." Gu Yu smiled faintly, a breathtaking sight that surely set many young hearts racing, falling head over heels in love.

To the local neighbors, Gu Yu was somewhat of an enigma. She ran a bakery nearby which did well, and she was very warm to the locals. At the same time, she had an otherworldly beauty, and many had expressed their affection towards her, all to be gently rejected. Those with ulterior motives towards her simply vanished without a trace. During holidays, mysterious figures would visit her, showing the utmost respect, clearly important individuals.

People speculated whether she was the daughter of a prominent family who did not wish to return home and instead came here to experience life.

Her house, though externally identical to her neighbors’, was precisely modified. The interior was wrapped in a pulsating flesh, making it a stronghold nearly unparalleled on Candle Sky Star.

She verified her biological information, opened her door, but was met with a stranger lying on her couch, which caused her to tense up.

"Who are you!" Gu Yu shouted, distracting the stranger as countless tentacles sprouted from the couch, entwining the intruder.

Not even a high-level Candle Sky Soldier could hope to escape from this trap.

But mysterious flames erupted from the stranger, incinerating the tentacles cleanly.

Burning away the tentacles seemed to be a trivial matter for the stranger. He slowly stood up, approaching Gu Yu who prepared to attack once more.

"It’s been a while, Miss Gu Yu, or should I say, the will of the Zombie Planet is more appropriate?"

Gu Yu’s pupils dilated sharply. Her information was of the highest confidentiality among the upper echelons of the Candle Sky Civilization. Who was this man, and how did he know her identity!

"Who the hell are you?"

The stranger gazed into Gu Yu’s eyes and slowly uttered the name that had once terrified her into seclusion.

"I am Lu Yang."

Chapter 1332: 1331: Diary

Upon hearing Lu Yang’s self-introduction, Gu Yu’s pupils constricted sharply.

The name Lu Yang was one she didn’t want to hear in her lifetime.

The Candle Sky Civilization, fearing other civilizations would falter their rule, spared no effort searching for those living planets and destroying them.

Gu Yu was one of the few with the highest power in the Candle Sky Civilization, and also the pioneer of this plan.

She spread the zombie virus on those planets, disrupting the order of the stars. The Candle Sky Civilization would then send spacecraft allowing those planets to choose: to submit or to die. But these choices had nothing to do with her, she was only responsible for creating chaos.

She played this trick with such proficiency, it’s unknown how many planets were destroyed because of her.

Until she encountered Lu Yang on Blue Star.

Lu Yang not only dealt with the Candle Sky Civilization’s spacecraft but also counterattacked, striking toward the Spirit Stone Planet, forcing her to finally expose her identity to deal with Lu Yang, and then escaping back to the Candle Sky Civilization.

After fleeing back to the Candle Sky Civilization, an anomaly occurred on the Spirit Stone Planet, a man named Wu Yao suddenly appeared, extremely powerful, successively defeating battleships, and with his own power, subdued the Candle Sky Civilization to become the ruler.

After Gu Yu learned about this, she was terrified, believing there’s no such thing as coincidence. This man named Wu Yao must have an inexplicable relationship with Lu Yang.

After Wu Yao took power, she immediately retired, unwilling to have any relationship with Wu Yao. She disguised herself as an ordinary person, trying to fill her mind with busy life, not thinking about Lu Yang or Wu Yao.

Decades passed, changing multiple identities, Gu Yu thought this matter had passed.

After the Candle Sky Civilization established trade relations with the Cultivation World, Gu Yu heard that there was also a person named Lu Yang in the Cultivation World, a peerless genius. Hearing this name made her shudder, and she did not dare to learn more.

"Miss Gu Yu, don’t misunderstand. My name is Lu Yang, hailing from the Cultivation World." Lu Yang’s relaxed tone made Gu Yu breathe a sigh of relief; it seemed she was alarmed over nothing.

"I don’t know what you want with me..."

"At the same time, I also come from Blue Star."

"!!!"

Gu Yu’s pupils constricted sharply once again, and she retreated repeatedly. These two were actually the same person; was he here to seek revenge on her?

Thinking of Lu Yang’s fame in the Cultivation World, Gu Yu did not hesitate for a moment; she immediately abandoned that body, transferring to another zombie’s body.

The body of this zombie girl was not her true form, but just one of the countless bodies she had.

Just like the planetary consciousness of Blue Star was a Primordial Spirit, Gu Yu was also a Primordial Spirit, with an extremely high level of life and particularly unique abilities, capable of occupying any zombie’s body.

If it were any ordinary Cultivator, they would have a hard time capturing her.

But in front of Lu Yang, a Semi-Immortal, it all meant nothing.

In Lu Yang’s eyes, a small and cute figure smaller than the girl flew out from the girl’s body, it was unknown towards where it was flying.

Lu Yang was unmoved, and softly said: "Trace Origin."

These simple two words defied the laws of the world. Time reversed, as if all the colors of the universe had faded, leaving only the desolate gray-whiteness. The already-transferred Gu Yu was dragged away by some unknown grand force.

The world returned to normal, and Gu Yu’s soul kneeled in front of Lu Yang.

Gu Yu shivered with fright, having never seen such terrifying ability. This was beyond her, beyond anyone’s power to contend with!

She knelt on the ground, continuously kowtowing, tears streaming, "I beg you, please let me go, I have already reformed! If you don’t believe me, go ask the neighbors, they all praise me for being helpful!"

Lu Yang crouched down, patted Gu Yu’s cheek, his expression indifferent: "You better go apologize to those people you killed on Blue Star."

With a sharp cry, the golden divine bird appeared on Lu Yang’s shoulder, dazzling and brilliant, like a miniature sun.

The golden divine bird transformed into the most primitive and pure Gold Crow True Flame, enveloping Gu Yu and burning his essence. Gu Yu writhed in agony, pleading and cursing until he dissolved into nothingness.

This was the Taoist Fruit Embryo Level True Flame feared even by those at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage. How could a Primordial Spirit possibly contend with it?

The once despair-inducing enemy had become so trivial. Vengeance came too easily. It took a while after Gu Yu vanished for Lu Yang to realize his nemesis had been vanquished, and he stood up, finally taking a deep breath.

Lu Yang boarded the Black Canopy Boat and left Candle Sky Star, flying towards the Spirit Stone Planet.

This was the place where he had perished in his previous life, and the first stop after Wu Yao’s transformation—it remained the heart of the Candle Sky Civilization, heavily guarded to this day.

Lu Yang passed through the defensive line with ease, as if invisible, and landed on the Spirit Stone Planet.

The spirit stones of this planet were abundant as the sea, with seven spirit stone veins, all either high-grade or top-grade.

"The layout of these spirit stone veins seems familiar," remarked Immortal Fairy Eternity, first with a sound of intrigue, then one of realization.

"I remember now, this planet was created by the Yingtian Immortal and others."

"They combined several top-grade spirit stone veins, claiming that theoretically, this could give birth to Spirit Stone Essence."

Lu Yang: "..."

No wonder I never saw such concentrated spirit stone veins in the Cultivation World—it turns out they were artificially created by Yingtian Immortal and his peers!

Lu Yang roamed the Spirit Stone Planet, recalling every bit and piece of its history, while the Candle Sky Soldiers passed by without noticing his presence.

This planet brought him nothing but unpleasant memories: piloting a spaceship far from home, fighting with Candle Sky Soldiers on the Spirit Stone Planet, being betrayed by Gu Yu, and igniting the spirit stone veins in despair.

Ultimately, Lu Yang leaped into a mine shaft—a site of fallen rocks, partially melted stones, and traces of black blood, clearly indicating the scene of a battle.

Lu Yang lay on the ground, gazing up at the sky, beneath which were top-grade spirit stones. This was the stance he held just before his death in his past life.

"It should be here." Lu Yang searched around, floating his hands over a pile of ashes.

"What’s this?" Immortal Fairy Eternity asked curiously, it looked like something she had cooked.

Lu Yang had a nostalgic expression as he seriously responded, "A diary. I had a habit of keeping a diary in my past life. From my hometown to the Spirit Stone Planet, I carried it with me until it turned to ashes in the end."

"Trace Origin."

He activated the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype, drawing the scattered ashes from all directions. They gradually came together into the shape of the diary, like a painter adding color, restoring the vivid hues to the ashes.

Lu Yang picked up the restored diary and flipped through it. When he reached the last page and saw the words "Lu Yang’s Final Entry", he smiled faintly, filled with emotion.

Back then, he would never have imagined that there would come a day when he’d hold his diary in hand once more.

"What does it say inside?" Immortal Fairy Eternity poked her head forward curiously, trying to peek, which made Lu Yang quickly close the diary.

"Privacy, you can’t look."

"Hmpf, so stingy," Immortal Fairy Eternity grumbled, but she didn’t use her Immortal Sense to force a peek.

Lu Yang placed the diary in his Identity Jade Pendant for safekeeping.

The diary was already full, and he no longer had the habit of writing in it; it was now merely a memento.

"Fairy, let’s go. I’ll take you to see my old home!" Lu Yang said with a smile. Reclaiming the diary had put him in a great mood, his voice even more robust than usual.

Lu Yang took Immortal Fairy Eternity into his Spiritual Space, activated the Black Canopy Boat, and flew towards Blue Star by coordinates.

Chapter 1333: 1332: Lu Yang’s Former Residence

In the cold, desolate universe, a streak of shuttle light shattered the tranquility, speeding faster than any spacecraft as space twisted around the light, piloted by Lu Yang on the Black Canopy Boat.

The journey home was not boring, with Immortal Fairy by his side.

As they approached Blue Star, Lu Yang grew increasingly anxious and restless; his prior missions to the Candle Sky Civilization to deal with Gu Yu and to the Spirit Stone Planet to retrieve his diary also stemmed from this reason.

Otherwise, Lu Yang could have simply visited Blue Star before addressing these matters.

"Is that your hometown?" Immortal Fairy’s voice snapped Lu Yang out of his thoughts.

Lu Yang swiftly pulled on the Black Canopy Boat, nearly flying past Earth.

The Black Canopy Boat disengaged from light speed and leap mode, appearing out of nowhere. This scene was caught by a satellite, astonishing the old experts and professors at the space agency, almost popping their eyes out.

"Good heavens, what did I see?"

"Did ancient people travel the universe in small boats?"

The space agency erupted in exclamations, thrown into complete disarray. Only then did Lu Yang notice the nearby satellite and realize his recklessness. He stowed away the Black Canopy Boat and leaped into Earth’s atmosphere alone.

"Quick, switch the view, track that humanoid creature’s movements!" the old experts in the space agency shouted, startling their apprentices who had never heard their master’s voice raised so loud.

But soon, they lost track of Lu Yang, to their immense frustration.

Lu Yang darted through the clouds, his descent accelerating like a shooting star across the sky, then slowing down and landing on an airplane.

He sat cross-legged on the exterior of the plane, looking down at the beautiful rivers, mountains, and landscape below, his mind filled with myriad thoughts.

After hitching a ride on the jet stream, Lu Yang jumped off the plane before it landed, and performed a small magic skill, changing his ancient attire into Blue Star’s style.

Looking at the crowd on the streets and the familiar style of buildings and skyscrapers, Lu Yang felt dazed for a moment, as if he had returned to Blue Star before the zombie crisis erupted.

After surviving the zombie crisis, where only a fraction of people survived, and through decades of effort, Blue Star was finally restored to its former state.

Only a small number of people in the Candle Sky Civilization knew of Blue Star’s existence, and given the great distance and lack of worthy conquest value, coupled with Gu Yu’s fear of Blue Star and his decision to seal most of Blue Star’s information before his retirement, the Candle Sky Civilization ceased sending people to Blue Star after the zombie crisis ended.

Lu Yang wandered the streets, somewhat distracted.

Before coming to Blue Star, he sensed that once there, he would meet many people and do many things. Yet, upon arrival, he found himself at a loss, not knowing what to do.

This city was not a random choice. In his memory, this place used to be a safety zone he established, protecting countless survivors.

As time passed, those familiar wasteland structures were gone, and his parents had passed away before the zombie crisis, making him feel like an outsider here.

Following his vague memory, he stopped in front of what used to be his house and softly uttered the name of the new building.

"Doomsday History Museum?"

"Little Yang, is this your home?" Immortal Fairy curiously popped out, invisible to others anyway.

Lu Yang nodded awkwardly: "It should be."

He sneaked past the ticket office and walked into the museum.

Today is a weekday, yet there are still many visitors here, great-grandparents holding their grandchildren’s hands, telling them about that tough and dangerous period.

The grandparent and grandchild duo stopped in front of a large sign: "Look at this Peace Hospital, the zombie virus that brought the world to its knees originated from this hospital."

Peace Hospital had been demolished long ago, leaving only a sign and some ruins at the entrance as a historic lesson, to serve as a wake-up call for future generations.

"That time was really dangerous, grandpa survived thanks to General Lu." The great-grandfather was deeply moved; out of options and starving, he was taken in by the safe zone, which allowed him to survive until now.

During the doomsday era, Lu Yang organized a zombie legion, and people got used to calling him General Lu.

Every time he thought back to those scenes, he wanted to thank Lu Yang face to face, to express gratitude for saving his life, but unfortunately, that was impossible now. Commander Lu fought for Blue Star, died on an alien planet, and his remains were lost.

Lu Yang watched the old man for a while, gently shook his head, and couldn’t remember who he was; too many people had been rescued in the safe zone.

Several teachers were leading a group of elementary school students to visit the Doomsday History Museum, talking about that period of history very familiar to Lu Yang.

Lu Yang found his home, which had become a famous landmark, with the furniture setup and books arranged just as they used to be, although none were the original items.

There was a commotion in the crowd nearby, it seemed a VIP was coming to clear the area, but Lu Yang just stood in front of his former home, hands behind his back, with bodyguards walking back and forth behind him, as if he were invisible.

A wrinkled old woman hunched over, trembling with each step on her cane, refusing anyone’s help. Her entourage watched nervously, fearing she might fall, wondering how they would explain it to their superiors.

This VIP had an astonishing background; no one on Blue Star had a more legendary existence than her.

As a young woman, she was a doctor who investigated the source of the zombie virus in the danger zones and was subsequently rescued by General Lu. Although she didn’t uncover the source of the virus, she founded the safe zones with General Lu and even took over command of the safe zones. General Lu even handed over the zombie army to her.

During the defense of Blue Star, she repeatedly performed heroic deeds. After General Lu led several Corpse Kings on an expedition to another planet, when zombies on Blue Star became uncontrollable, she organized the military, suppressed the zombies, united the seven major safe zones, and led the reconstruction after the disaster.

At the height of her power, she decisively stepped back to let others lead.

The safe zone was not her hometown, but she chose to stay in the safe zones after retirement and visited the Doomsday History Museum periodically.

Her entourage felt a pang of sadness watching her struggle to walk; her illness was worsening, and this might be her last visit to the museum.

The dignitary shakily made her way toward Lu Yang’s former home.

The entourage, seeing a young man still standing in front of Lu Yang’s home, frowned deeply, cursing the bodyguards for their negligence; if anything happened to the dignitary, the spit from all over the world would drown them, the negligent staff.

The dignitary saw the young man’s back, her eyes clouded; she was too old, gravely ill, and her mind was no longer clear. For a moment, she thought the young man overlapped with the image of someone she longed for, and couldn’t help but call out:

"Lu Yang, is it you?"

The young man turned around, revealing a face she didn’t recognize. The dignitary shook her head and chuckled bitterly, feeling her age. The will of Blue Star had said Lu Yang had self-detonated; how could he possibly appear here?

Seeing the elderly dignitary, the young man smiled warmly.

"Doctor, long time no see."

Chapter 1334: 1333: Rebirth

Upon hearing that high-ranking official shout out Lu Yang’s name, the accompanying staff were all abuzz.

Who is Lu Yang? He is the savior of Blue Star. Even if it’s a child who doesn’t study history, a political ignoramus unaware of who the current leaders are, they would know the name Lu Yang.

It could be said that without Lu Yang, Blue Star would have long become a Zombie Planet, or perhaps fallen captive to the Candle Sky Civilization!

But the problem is, Lu Yang, who fought in the stars, has long been dead. How could he possibly appear here, so young, and not even resembling the photos in the textbooks?

Is that high-ranking official senile, blurring fantasy and reality?

Hearing Lu Yang’s response, the elderly doctor with white hair rolled his eyes and fainted straight away, scaring the accompanying staff who quickly rushed to support him.

Lu Yang, however, reached the doctor before them and stealthily infused a tiny bit of Spiritual Energy to awaken him.

"Doctor Xia He, you’re still such a scaredy-cat, eh?"

Lu Yang said with a chuckling smile. When he first met the doctor, she appeared brave but was actually quite timid. To train her courage, Lu Yang often threw her into piles of zombies. Unexpectedly, after so many years, the doctor’s bravery hadn’t increased one bit.

With blurry eyes, the doctor looked at the unfamiliar young face and the familiar exasperating tone, she was sure this person was the damned Lu Yang!

Just that she didn’t know why he had changed his appearance and looked so young.

Anyhow, since she knew Lu Yang, the bastard had always been weird, even telling her before that there were two personas in his head, one called the Will of Blue Star and the other the Will of Zombie Planet.

"If you scare Lu Yang to death, I won’t let you off even as a ghost!" Xia He said through gritted teeth, it had been a long time since she felt so annoyed.

In this world, perhaps only Lu Yang could stir her emotions like this.

"Don’t worry, now ghosts wouldn’t dare to come for me." Seeing the doctor again put Lu Yang in a great mood.

"Seeing you before I die, I can die without regrets," Xia He said smilingly, shaking her head. Her face was so aged it was hard to imagine she was once a striking beauty.

If she had seen Lu Yang ten years ago, she would have felt ashamed for having aged, unwilling to appear before Lu Yang, hoping that in his memory she would remain forever young and beautiful.

But now, as her end was near, seeing Lu Yang was all she wished for.

"I won’t let you die with me around," Lu Yang said, not with sadness but amusement.

Xia He shook her head: "I know my body well, I feel much better after seeing you, it must be the flickering of the flame before extinguishing, I won’t last much longer."

"...You’re not dying, stop lying down." Lu Yang promptly pulled up Xia He, frightening the accompanying staff who were all too aware of her frailty, shocked by such rough treatment.

However, Xia He seemed like nothing was wrong, even her breathing became much smoother.

She was originally a doctor herself, more than aware of the anomaly in her body, this was definitely not the flickering of the flame before extinguishing!

"What, what did you do?"

Lu Yang looked at the cautious accompanying staff with a smirk, "Are you sure you want to discuss this here?"

The accompanying staff certainly didn’t believe that the young man before them was Lu Yang, they thought Xia He had senile dementia and was deceived by someone with ulterior motives.

The evacuation of the crowd had already begun outside, and armed police had the area surrounded, looking for suitable sniping positions.

"Let’s go to my house," Xia He said, yet the accompanying staff showed no intention of letting them through.

Xia He was somewhat troubled. How could she not know what the accompanying personnel were thinking? Even if she gave a firm order, for her own safety, they probably wouldn’t let her through.

"You lead the way, I’ll take you there." Like he was scooping up rice, Lu Yang picked up Xia He horizontally, flew out of the museum, and left the accompanying personnel and armed police watching in stunned disbelief.

The armed police dared not shoot, fearing they might accidentally injure Xia He, so they could only let them go.

Xia He’s eyes widened. This bastard hadn’t been seen for so many years, and he could fly now?!

"Where is your home?"

"Fly east!" Xia He raised her arm and pointed east. Being held horizontally by Lu Yang did not make her feel uncomfortable. On the contrary, she felt a thrill. It reminded her of the apocalypse when Lu Yang would often drag her in and out of zombie encirclements.

After a few directions, Lu Yang quickly arrived at Xia He’s home—a villa.

"You’ve done quite well for yourself over the years, living in a villa now. I’m still living in a cave," Lu Yang teased.

Xia He sighed, "I originally wanted to live in a small house, but the government insisted it wasn’t safe and arranged for me to live here."

"Do you live in this big house alone?"

"Not exactly, the housekeeper and security personnel are inside. Oh no, I don’t have the keys." With Xia He’s status, it was unlikely she’d carry keys.

Before she finished speaking, Lu Yang took a step forward, walked through the wall, and entered the villa.

Xia He was stunned. How did he do that?

"Where have you been all these years?" Xia He asked. Not having seen Lu Yang for decades, he had become even more mysterious.

Lu Yang waved his hand, "No rush, I’ll treat your illness first."

Xia He’s body was riddled with holes, like a leaky sack, her vitality rapidly slipping away. To say she was ill was an understatement—it was like the recklessness of her youth had caused old injuries that, with age, came back to haunt her. When she said she was about to die earlier, it wasn’t a joke. She was indeed close to death.

The fact that she had lived until now was not so much a miracle as it was a stubborn willpower clinging onto life.

Xia He’s body couldn’t withstand much turmoil. Any other cultivator would have to dilute elixirs and gradually condition her body, performing a Washing Marrow, which would take days or even months.

But Lu Yang made it simple. He activated the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype and tapped Xia He’s forehead.

A miraculous glow spread from her forehead to her entire body. Wherever it touched, her skin became soft and youthful, as if she was reborn.

"This, this..." Xia He looked at her body in astonishment, feeling as if she had returned to her twenties, like when she first met Lu Yang, brimming with inexhaustible energy.

She tried to imagine all the oddities of Lu Yang, but this technique still overturned her perceptions.

Xia He had never dreamed that she could return to her youthful appearance.

"How about that, Doctor Xia? Isn’t my medical skill higher than yours now?" Xia He used to tease Lu Yang often, mocking his ignorance of medicine. Now, Lu Yang finally had his chance to mock her back.

"What have you been through all these years?" Xia He’s feelings were complex, as she recalled the Blue Star Will telling her that Lu Yang had died on the Spirit Stone Planet, and how she had cried.

But at that time, she couldn’t show too much grief, as the Blue Star Will still needed her to organize humanity’s efforts against the zombies—a task Lu Yang had entrusted to the Blue Star Will before his death.

Back then, she had to suppress her sadness and always portray a fiery demeanor in front of others. It was this stark contrast that had left her with a lingering illness.

Pah, I cried for nothing. Damn Lu Yang, give me back my tears.

"It all started with a rainy day on the second day of the second lunar month when I went to take the Seek Dao Sect examination..."

Chapter 1335: 1334: Did General Lu Really Come Back to Life?

Lu Yang’s story is very long, so long that it could not be finished even in three days and nights. Xia He listened quietly, her eyes filled with the vivacious expression of Lu Yang.

From Lu Yang’s description, Xia He learned just how vast and magnificent the Cultivation World is, and also learned how rich and colorful Lu Yang’s experiences over the years have been.

Over the years, Lu Yang encountered many dangers, yet Xia He noticed that when Lu Yang talked about these experiences, he was still very excited and took pleasure in them. It was clear that he was quite satisfied with his experiences over the years.

Unfortunately, it was not herself who had accompanied Lu Yang by his side.

"Are you hungry? I’ll have someone bring up some food." Xia He said, though in truth it was not Lu Yang who was hungry, but herself.

Suddenly, Xia He paused, realizing there was a problem.

"What’s wrong?" Lu Yang asked, puzzled.

Xia He pointed to her youthful face, somewhat panicked, "Now that I look like this, will they still recognize me?"

If the security personnel knew about Xia He’s rejuvenation, even the most steadfast among them would become flustered. Even if they could verify Xia He’s identity through fingerprints and DNA comparison, it would surely not be a peaceful process.

"Then I’ll change you back to your original appearance." Lu Yang joked and even pinched his throat, imitating Xia He’s previous voice, "Lu Yang, I’m going to die."

"Don’t mimic me!" Xia He hit Lu Yang furiously, her punches soft and weak, lacking in strength.

"Stop hitting, stop hitting, let me think of a solution." Lu Yang quickly surrendered.

"I’ve got it."

Before long, Lu Yang pulled out a piece of jade carved with exquisite patterns from his Identity Jade Pendant.

"This was a little gift from Junior Sister Tao that I mentioned before. She is skilled in Illusion Techniques, and this piece of jade is engraved with Illusion Array patterns. Once you wear it, others will think you look like your former self."

"Is it really that magical?"

Xia He put on the jade, looked in the mirror, and indeed, she had returned to her elderly appearance, her voice also turned hoarse. When she took off the Jade Pendant, she reverted to her original state.

Only then did Xia He confidently call the maid to bring up some food.

The maid was startled when she saw Xia He, not understanding when Xia He had come home. However, she did not dare to ask and just obediently went to cook.

"Make it richer in nutrients." Xia He instructed, as she normally could only drink a bowl of millet porridge; now that she had reverted to her younger self, she had a hearty appetite.

The maid trembled, sadness welled up in her heart - the day had come, after all, when the old Xia He was having a momentary revival.

She resolved to block out everything and perform at her best in this life, to provide the old Madame Xia with one last delicious meal.

"Did she misunderstand something?" Xia He wondered when she returned, having never seen the maid with such a resolute look in her eyes.

Lu Yang just stood to the side, snickering without pointing it out. This silly girl truly hadn’t changed at all, looking clever but actually quite naive.

"By the way, do you know where Bai Shuang is?" Lu Yang inquired about the whereabouts of the will of Blue Star.

Bai Shuang, the will of Blue Star - this was a name Lu Yang gave her.

"Bai Shuang? I haven’t seen her for a while."

Since Xia He was the person Lu Yang trusted the most, so after Lu Yang’s Self-Detonation on the Spirit Stone Planet in his past life, Bai Shuang returned to Blue Star and sought out Xia He immediately to inform her of the bad news, hoping Xia He would form an army to exterminate Zombies.

After Xia He reinstated the Blue Star, Bai Shuang lent a helping hand. Through mutual aid and due to Lu Yang’s connections, Xia He became a rare good friend of Bai Shuang.

"She said she wanted to find a way to save me and then disappeared, never to be seen again." Xia He sighed, longing to share the good news of Lu Yang still being alive with her.

As the consciousness of the planet, Bai Shuang came and went without a trace, always seeking out Xia He on her own initiative. Xia He didn’t have Bai Shuang’s contact information.

This made things difficult for Lu Yang. He was able to find Gu Yu because the higher-ups of the Candle Sky Civilization were all aware of Gu Yu’s existence. Through interrogation, finding out her whereabouts would have been easy.

But Bai Shuang was extremely elusive, and it was likely that in the vast Blue Star, only Xia He knew of Bai Shuang’s existence, making the search for her exceedingly challenging.

However, Lu Yang didn’t overthink it. Bai Shuang was searching for a way to save Xia He. Whether she found it or not, she would eventually come back to Xia He.

Another half a month passed. During this period, Lu Yang stayed by Xia He’s side, visiting the historical museum, former battle zones, and the library to see how history judged Lu Yang. They reminisced about the bits and pieces of life during the apocalypse, and when they touched upon embarrassing moments, they would exchange a knowing smile, in sync.

"It was you who covered up the fact about me being the original pathogen, wasn’t it?" Lu Yang asked. His acquaintance with Xia He began when she ventured into a zombie danger zone to investigate the source of the virus.

However, the history books stated that the origin of the virus was never found.

Xia He’s smile blossomed: "You are the hero of Blue Star and were the faith of all humanity during that time. Faith cannot be tainted."

Lu Yang nodded but said nothing.

"The Immortal inside you, can we have the pleasure of meeting her?" Xia He asked curiously. In the stories narrated by Lu Yang, the Female Immortal was mentioned the most.

"How can this Immortal honor your mere mortal request to meet me?" Despite her words, Fairy Eternity still emerged from Lu Yang’s body, huffing and looking discontentedly at Xia He, disliking the sight of her either way.

Little Yang was always being affectionate with her and seldom chatted with Fairy Eternity anymore.

"I have seen Immortal Fairy Eternity before, thank you for always taking care of Lu Yang," Xia He said, performing the ancient ceremony impeccably. She learned it specifically from Lu Yang and mastered it after being shown once.

Lu Yang wondered why Xia He had taken an interest in the ancient ceremonies of the Cultivation World. Had she done so just for this moment?

Xia He’s attitude greatly pleased Immortal Fairy Eternity, who hummed with self-satisfaction: "There’s nothing to thank me for. After all, Little Yang is mine, and it’s my duty to take care of him."

"Immortal Fairy Eternity carries the honorable title of Immortal, eternally living freely and at ease, yet you struggle to rebuild your Immortal Body and have to dwell within Lu Yang’s Spiritual Space. It truly is a grievance for you."

Immortal Fairy Eternity was startled, sensing a hidden meaning in the girl’s words.

Hearing Lu Yang’s story, Xia He felt something strange, as if something was off about Immortal Fairy Eternity. Yet that was merely her intuition, something she casually remarked.

Xia He’s bodyguards, aware of the mysterious man by her side, remained on high alert, fearing he might attempt to use the aged Xia to fulfill some unspeakable secrets.

Although Xia was not active in politics anymore, her influence remained steadfast. A single word from her demanded attention from the Blue Star’s high echelons.

As for saying the mysterious man was Lu Yang, the bodyguards naturally didn’t believe it, even though Lu Yang had demonstrated his extraordinary powers at the historical museum.

These so-called bodyguards, in fact, military personnel sent by Blue Star’s high-level officials to protect Xia He, reported the information on Lu Yang to the upper echelons of Blue Star, all of whom remained speechless.

The reason was simple: they had in their possession a report from the head of the Space Agency.

The satellite photos showed an ancient person traveling through the universe in a small boat, identical to the mysterious man beside Xia He.

"Is the person by old Xia’s side really General Lu?"

The reaction within Blue Star’s higher echelons varied, but they all unanimously decided to keep this matter as top secret, not visiting the resurrected Lu Yang nor the waning Xia He.

Chapter 1336: 1335: The Trouble of Bai Shuang

Another month had passed, and these days Lu Yang had been living quite comfortably. The bodyguards no longer watched him like they were guarding against thieves, probably because they had received orders from higher-ups.

Bai Shuang still hadn’t come looking for Xia He, which Lu Yang found odd. If it weren’t for his interference, Xia He would still be alive; logically, Bai Shuang should have shown up by now.

...

Outside the villa, a young man dressed in traditional Chinese attire leisurely walked towards the villa but was stopped by the on-duty soldiers: "Stop, who are you!"

Facing the armed soldiers, the young man in traditional attire showed a hint of a smile and said respectfully: ’Please tell old Lady Xia that I wish to see her."

"Old Lady Xia isn’t someone you can meet just because you want to," the duty soldier frowned and was about to drive the young man away.

Seeing this, the young man in traditional dress sighed alone: "I knew it would be like this."

After speaking, he disappeared in an instant, and the next moment he appeared in the courtyard, prompting the duty soldier to quickly sound the alarm.

"Enemy attack!"

As soldiers tasked with protecting Xia He, all were elites among elites. The moment he forcibly entered the courtyard, several sniper rifles targeted him.

The squad leader stepped forward to intercept, but the young man in traditional attire knocked him down with a single palm, even before he had time to react, despite being the champion of a military exercise competition.

Bang—Bang—Bang—

Three gunshots rang out, aimed at the joints of the young man in traditional attire, but he, already alert, neither dodged nor avoided, and a golden shield appeared on his body to block the three bullets.

"What?!" The sniper was shocked. Even with professional psychological training, the sight still left him stunned for a moment.

Blocking sniper bullets, is this still a human?

Facing the encircling soldiers, the young man in traditional attire moved as if strolling in a leisurely courtyard, knocking them down one by one. He pushed open the villa’s main door with a smile, searched around the villa, but did not find Xia He.

Returning to the living room and turning around, he saw Xia He who had just returned from shopping standing at the main door, along with a strange man carrying many shopping bags.

"Who are you?" Xia He frowned. The soldiers nearby were all knocked down, clearly by the man in traditional attire.

Those accompanying her wanted to stop Xia He, but she insisted on returning to the villa.

"My name is Li Wenjie, I have seen old Lady Xia. My master hopes you can come and have a talk."

"What if I don’t want to?" Xia He asked.

Li Wenjie’s smile did not change: "You better make a trip, after all, your friend Bai Shuang is in my master’s hands. Of course, if you still refuse to go, I will have no choice but to take you forcefully."

The next moment, Lu Yang appeared in front of Li Wenjie, grabbing him by the throat.

"What did you say!"

Li Wenjie, no longer composed, flailed desperately, but it was as if his neck was caught in a vice, making it impossible to struggle free.

Lu Yang stared intently at Li Wenjie, a Foundation Establishment Stage Cultivator from Blue Star.

Li Wenjie definitely wasn’t a cultivator from the Cultivation World; there wasn’t anyone in the Cultivation World who didn’t recognize Lu Yang.

How did Li Wenjie obtain such cultivation skills? If Blue Star had a legitimate cultivation method, he would have discovered it during the zombie crisis.

"Where is your master!"

"At... Kunlun Mountain."

"Lu Yang..." Xia He said softly, her worry for Bai Shuang’s safety now immense.

"I know, let’s go to Kunlun Mountain, I want to see who dares to touch even a finger on Bai Shuang!"

Lu Yang hadn’t felt this angry in a long time, his killing intent pervading the air. No wonder he couldn’t find Bai Shuang, she had been captured!

Lu Yang roughly threw Li Wenjie into the Green Blade Sword Small World, summoned the Black Canopy Boat, embraced Bai Shuang, and sped towards Kunlun Mountain!

...

Deep within Kunlun Mountain, ancient runes stretched out from the earth like eight dragon veins charging in all directions.

As the runes extended, a burly monk with a scarred face built an altar. On the altar was a petite girl devoid of physical form, possessing only a soul, while below knelt praying devotees.

Among them were wealthy businessmen, scientists, and professors; at this moment, they had abandoned their past identities, devotedly worshiping the scar-faced monk and wholeheartedly chanting scriptures.

The scar-faced monk could hardly conceal the joy in his heart. He never expected to encounter a Primordial Spirit on such a remote planet.

If he could subdue it and make it his devotee, it would undoubtedly boost his Spiritual Sense and advance his Cultivation Level!

After his cultivation improved, he might even have a chance to break the Ancient Sealing of Kunlun Mountain!

Unfortunately, this Primordial Spirit was steadfast, and chanting scriptures seemed insufficient to weaken its will. Although he could slowly refine it, he now had a better method.

This Primordial Spirit was friends with a local native, and by bringing that friend before her and letting her witness their death, her resolve would surely waver, allowing him to subdue her in her unguarded moment.

The scar-faced monk revealed a greedy expression—this was his opportunity.

"Little sprite, haven’t you always been babbling about saving your friend? Don’t worry, you’ll see her very soon!"

"How dare you!" Bai Shuang charged off the altar in anger, but the altar was surrounded by ancient scriptures, forming an invisible cage that firmly trapped her.

Bai Shuang deeply regretted it; she had spoken of Xia He in her delirium not long ago, not realizing that the old monk had overheard.

"Of course I dare." The more agitated Bai Shuang became, the more pleased the scar-faced monk was—he knew the significant place Xia He held in her heart, and her witnessing his death would have an even better effect.

Calculating the time, his loyal disciple should have found Xia He by now.

A majestic and wrathful voice echoed through Kunlun Mountain, causing the mountaintop’s snow to burst forth and the ground to shake.

"Really? I wonder who gave you the audacity!"

"Who’s there!" The scar-faced monk spun around, the power contained in the voice making his heart tremble.

The Black Canopy Boat, as an accelerative Immortal Treasure traveling through the universe, moved unbelievably fast, crossing nearly half of Blue Star in the blink of an eye from the villa to Kunlun Mountain.

The scar-faced monk caught sight of the figure on the Black Canopy Boat, his pupils suddenly constricting: "Lu, Lu Yang!"

How could Lu Yang be here!

Lu Yang likewise recognized the scar-faced monk, the evil monk Ban Bu, from Buddha Country, a former Abbot of the Twelve Great Temples’ Sajia Temple who entered the evil path and vanished without a trace, ranking even above Master Ming Yu, a true ancient relic.

Seeing Lu Yang, Ban Bu trembled uncontrollably. Witnessing Lu Yang’s battle with Wu Yao, how could he not know the formidable strength of this rising star, knowing that no one in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage could be his match.

He cursed his misfortune; after dodging around and barely escaping pursuit from Western Paradise Temple, he ran into this place called Blue Star, thinking it was a place of fortune, only to run into Lu Yang.

"Considering this poor monk as an elder, if young friend Lu Yang is willing to spare my life, I am willing to offer all my treasures..."

The devotees stared dumbfounded. In their eyes, Ban Bu was an omnipotent deity; when had he ever been so utterly subservient?

Seeing Xia He weakly tormented by the altar, Bai Shuang called out softly, "Xia He."

Witnessing this scene, Lu Yang’s heart ached immensely, and he exploded in rage, striking Ban Bu with his sword.

The brilliant sword light illuminated the entirety of Kunlun Mountain.

"Die, you old relic!"

Chapter 1337: 1336: How Is There Someone in Your Spiritual Space?

Facing Lu Yang’s furious strike, the Evil Monk Ban Bu had no means to escape; thus he steeled his heart and decided to fight to the death.

He resolutely bit the tip of his tongue, without any hesitation smeared his face with his blood forming bizarre symbols. He had thoroughly researched ancient history; these were signs of some ancient tribe, burning up lifespan Essence Blood.

It was as if countless bells were tolling within the body of the Evil Monk Ban Bu, Buddhist chants echoed, compelling involuntary worship, but upon closer listening, one would realize that these chants were not pure but tainted with malevolent obsessions.

A golden Arhat Dharma Image congealed, majestic and solemn. The disciples, excited upon seeing this, kowtowed with such fervor that their heads bled.

"You young ones, you’ve forced my hand!"

The Evil Monk Ban Bu was bleeding from all orifices, his face twisted and ferocious, the golden Arhat too was roaring. Even when pursued by Ming Yu, the Abbot, he had never used this move which could consume thousands of years of his lifespan. If it weren’t a life-or-death moment, he really wouldn’t want to use it.

But facing Lu Yang’s strike, all was in vain. The golden Arhat was split in two. The Evil Monk Ban Bu had hoped to distract Lu Yang with the golden Arhat and escape; he never anticipated the sword to be so terrifying, incapable of blocking even one move, his expression was still one of horror even in death.

This strike was a convergence of Lu Yang’s Essence, Qi, and Spirit; even the Soul could not escape this sword, cleaved into two halves.

"Disgusting." Lu Yang felt nauseated that such a person lived even a second longer.

As the Soul was about to disperse, Lu Yang caught the two halves and forcefully conducted a Soul Search, discovering everything the Evil Monk Ban Bu did after arriving on Blue Star.

The omnipotent deity had fallen; the disciples, upon witnessing this, hurriedly fled.

"Stop."

With just one word, Lu Yang froze these disciples and with a grand wave of his hand, transported them all to the foot of Kunlun Mountain.

Through the Soul Search, Lu Yang found that these people were just ordinary people who had been brainwashed by Ban Bu, not inherently evil. However, their minds must not have been very clean to be brainwashed by Ban Bu, all swayed by their desires.

How to deal with these people was a problem for another time. It wasn’t easy to meet Bai Shuang, so he wasn’t going to think about these troubles now.

If one truly spoke of an evil person, then Li Wenjie, who forcefully intruded into the villa, certainly counted as one.

He was a disciple of an ancient sect on Blue Star and happened to encounter Ban Bu when he first arrived. He did many things for him, becoming the patriarch of that sect, where he was lawless.

Lu Yang gently tapped the Green Peak Sword, its buzzing sound shattered Li Wenjie within the Green Blade Sword Small World.

"Sister Bai!" Xia He called out, jumping off the Black Canopy Boat and rushing to the altar.

At that moment, she was wearing the Illusion Array Jade Pendant, still taking the form of an old grandmother.

Bai Shuang was stunned by the sight; when she last saw Xia He, she was trembling with a walking stick, her movements unsteady, but now she could run and jump?

"You, what’s going on with you?" The change was too abrupt; Bai Shuang was somewhat unable to react, "And who is he?"

The Evil Monk Ban Bu was the most terrifying enemy she had ever encountered in her life, giving her a sense of oppression far greater than the will from the Zombie Planet Gu Yu, or the Candle Sky Civilization Fleet.

Even her ability to come and go as she pleased was thwarted by the Evil Monk Ban Bu, which left her panicked.

But such an enemy was slain by that young man with a single sword strike.

Xia He removed the Illusion Array Jade Pendant, revealing her youthful appearance, wiping away tears excitedly, pointing to the young man sheathing his sword gracefully: "Sister Bai, Lu Yang is back, he cured me!"

"Lu Yang... He is Lu Yang?!"

Bai Shuang was first taken aback, then stared incredulously with eyes wide open, mouth slightly ajar, unable to close it for a long time.

She suddenly remembered the Evil Monk Ban Bu calling out Lu Yang’s name, and moreover, Xia He couldn’t possibly recognize the wrong person.

"Are you really Lu Yang?"

"Sister Bai, don’t you recognize me?" Lu Yang thought his earlier sword-retracting move wasn’t cool enough, so he pulled it out and put it away again.

Bai Shuang became certain, only Lu Yang would act cool so deliberately.

"You, you self-detonated, didn’t you?" Bai Shuang stuttered as she spoke; she had watched Lu Yang self-detonate with her own eyes, leaving not even ashes behind.

"The body is gone, but the soul remains," Lu Yang said with a grin, patting his chest to show he was healthy and there was no need to worry.

Although Bai Shuang didn’t quite understand how Lu Yang had come back to life, as long as he had, that was all that mattered.

Lu Yang was the first friend she had made; even her name was given by Lu Yang. No matter what, he would always be her best friend.

Bai Shuang missed the days when she lived inside Lu Yang’s body. During the apocalypse, she often stayed within Lu Yang to pull him to the human side. She would argue with Gu Yu; if it weren’t for Gu Yu’s ultimate betrayal, those times would have been the happiest moments of Bai Shuang’s life.

As the array method on the altar dissipated, Bai Shuang returned to her usual spirited state and drifted towards Lu Yang’s body, following her memory, only to be stopped by the tightly closed spiritual space.

"Lu Yang, why can’t I enter your body?" Bai Shuang gently knocked on the spiritual space, which she used to be able to enter and exit at will.

Separated by the spiritual space, Immortal Fairy looked at Bai Shuang with an unfriendly expression: "It’s occupied here."

Bai Shuang was so startled by the sudden appearance of Immortal Fairy that she did a somersault in mid-air: "Lu Yang, who is she?"

Immortal Fairy stood with hands on hips, like a haughty young mistress from ancient times who had moved into her husband’s family’s home: "This place is my home now."

Lu Yang was really getting a headache, courteously inviting Bai Shuang and Immortal Fairy out of his body.

Sometimes he even felt like his body wasn’t his own, as anyone seemed able to drop in whenever they wanted.

Xia He explained from the side, "Sister Bai, honestly speaking, this Immortal Fairy is Lu Yang’s benefactor; we should be grateful to her."

"A benefactor?"

"Let me explain." Lu Yang said, being the person involved, he knew the intricacies of the situation best, which couldn’t be explained in just a few words.

Lu Yang had no choice but to retell the story from the beginning, and Bai Shuang listened with great interest. She never imagined there was such a magical place as the Cultivation World, let alone that Lu Yang could stand out in a world filled with powerful cultivators like dragons.

Bai Shuang felt proud inside; indeed, her judgment of people wasn’t wrong.

"So you’re saying I’m not the will of Blue Star, but a Primordial Spirit?"

"Right. That’s also why that old monk wanted to subdue you. Every Primordial Spirit has tremendous potential. If properly cultivated, reaching the Transcendance Tribulation Stage wouldn’t be difficult."

"So why can’t I do anything now?" Bai Shuang was puzzled. Primordial Spirits sounded so formidable, yet all she could do was run around in human bodies, unable to do anything but chat.

"You lack Spiritual Energy, Sister Bai," Lu Yang said with a resigned smile, I just repeatedly emphasized the effects of Spiritual Energy, and you haven’t paid any attention.

"So you’re saying if I had Spiritual Energy, I could become very, very strong?"

"That’s right... Immortal Fairy, what are you doing?"

Lu Yang noticed out of the corner of his eye that Immortal Fairy was circling the belly of Kunlun Mountain, with a thoughtful expression.

He remembered from Evil Monk Ban Bu’s memories that there was an Ancient Sealing deep within Kunlun Mountain; one reason to subdue Bai Shuang here was also because of it.

Immortal Fairy pointed at several nearby mountain peaks, "This place looks familiar, feels like my home."

Chapter 1338: 1337 North Star

"Your home?" Lu Yang was startled upon hearing this.

The home of Fairy Eternity is none other than the North Star from Ancient Times, which is also the only star she controls.

During the time when the Four Ancient Immortals battled the hidden enemy, Qinghe, in order to protect what belonged to Fairy Eternity, seized the opportunity to send the North Star away. Due to the intense chaos then, even Qinghe couldn’t determine the final location of the North Star.

Moreover, four hundred thousand years have passed since then, and even if one remembered the original location, finding the North Star would be impossible.

This exploration into the universe also aimed to find the North Star, but with no clues, they could only rely on fate.

"I’ll use my Immortal Sense to see." In fact, when Fairy Eternity came to Kunlun Mountain, she found the place somewhat familiar and considered scanning it with her Immortal Sense.

However, seeing Little Yang and Bai Shuang catching up happily, if she were to confirm that this was the North Star, she definitely wouldn’t be able to refrain from telling Little Yang and would ruin the mood of their reunion.

She spread her Spiritual Sense over Kunlun Mountain, confirming her suspicions, and burst into happy laughter.

"This is the North Star!"

"Really?!" Lu Yang was confused, "Then why didn’t you recognize it when you first came to Blue Star?"

Having been on Blue Star for nearly two months now, surely it wouldn’t take Fairy this long to recognize her own home.

"The landscape has completely changed since our time; the North Star used to be just one continent," said Fairy Eternity, gesturing an irregular shape depicting what the continent of the North Star once looked like.

Lu Yang understood. When Qinghe threw out the North Star, it was only ensured that the star as a whole wouldn’t be torn apart by forces; she had no time to tend to the state of the continents.

During its high-speed flight, the continent on the North Star disintegrated, and the landscape changed. No wonder Fairy Eternity couldn’t recognize it.

Bai Shuang and Xia He, baffled by their conversation, wondered how the ancient Fairy Eternity could possibly call this place home.

Lu Yang briefly explained, and they suddenly understood, their interest piqued.

"The home of Fairy Eternity is where?"

"Come on, follow me!" Fairy Eternity waved grandly, exuding an authoritative stance, the kind that would gather protection fees on the streets.

Crossing over seven or eight mountains, they arrived at the deepest part of Kunlun Mountain. Kunlun Mountain’s grand and steep terrain was rarely visited, and the place they reached was unprecedentedly secluded.

"Here?" Xia He and Bai Shuang looking around, saw only a world of white snow and devastation with no vegetation, harsh and extremely cold. Without Lu Yang’s protection, Xia He would’ve already frozen into an ice sculpture. This place hardly seemed habitable.

"Below here," Lu Yang and Fairy Eternity said in unison, pointing downwards.

Nowadays, Kunlun Mountain is vastly different from its form during Ancient Times; following the splitting of continents and changes in terrain, the main body of Kunlun Mountain is buried deep underground, along with the home of Fairy Eternity.

Fairy Eternity formed hand seals and executed the Supreme Immortal Technique, sending Ancient Runes into the ground.

Shortly after, the whole of Kunlun Mountain began to shake violently, and the ground cracked.

At this point, the surface became unsafe, and Lu Yang decisively took Xia He into the sky, with Fairy Eternity and Bai Shuang following willingly.

Flying high, Xia He finally saw the mountains changing form and gasped in astonishment.

The mountain seemed to come to life, breaking through the earth and rapidly growing upwards, piercing through the clouds and rising tens of thousands of meters, impossibly large. In an instant, Blue Star’s highest mountain was replaced, towering many times over the second highest.

At this moment, the space agency was in chaos, with older professors trembling so much that they couldn’t even hold onto their reading glasses, dropping them on the ground.

On the big screen at the space agency was the space view of Blue Star; the changes in Kunlun Mountain were so drastic from space that they completely overturned their understanding.

The most senior expert whispered lowly, voicing everyone’s thoughts: "What has happened to this world?"

Lu Yang and three others landed in front of a stone gate at the foot of Kunlun Mountain, where there were traces of past habitation.

"Hehe, this is Fairy Eternity’s home!" Fairy Eternity stood in front of the stone gate, proudly asking Lu Yang to take a picture. This moment required a fine commemoration.

Originally, he was accompanying Little Yang back home; unexpectedly, Little Yang lived at the North Star.

Click.

The high-definition camera captured a group photo of the four in front of the Abode.

Fairy Eternity excitedly tapped Lu Yang’s shoulder: "I told you our fates were not shallow. Our hometowns are from the same place."

Lu Yang: "..."

He had never thought in his lifetime that he could become fellow townspeople with Fairy Eternity, separated by four hundred thousand years.

Lu Yang took a deep breath and pushed open the gate. A rich wave of Spiritual Energy hit them, like an ocean of Spiritual Energy.

Spiritual Energy was usually invisible and untouchable, but the energy inside this Abode was so dense that it had materialized, almost sweeping Xia He away.

Lu Yang raised his hand to create a Protective Shield around Xia He; ordinary people couldn’t withstand such a dense assault of Spiritual Energy.

On the other hand, Bai Shuang, upon contact with the Spiritual Energy, was incredibly excited. Being a Primordial Spirit, naturally powerful, she required Spiritual Energy the most. Subject to this surge of energy, her power increased by hundreds of times even without any guidance from a Cultivation Technique.

Lu Yang guessed that the Top-grade Spirit Stones inside the Abode had shattered and were sealed behind the stone gate, all blocked within the Abode. Now that they were opened, they gushed out like floodwaters.

...

"The car of the white phoenix, flying high and clear, perpetually bright and returning to true form, descending into the myriad powers within..."

As he meditated on Enlightenment, the hair of the Old Heavenly Master from the Zhengyi Sect had all turned white. His days were numbered, and he could even feel every moment of his lifespan dripping away, yet he displayed an unusual calm.

Since childhood, he had been accepted into the Zhengyi Sect, showcasing extraordinary talent, and quickly became an authorized Heavenly Master learning how to absorb Spiritual Energy and introducing it into his body to become a Qi Refiner.

Sadly, he could not progress further simply because the Zhengyi Sect did not have enough Spiritual Energy for his Cultivation. He could only stop at the initial three levels of Qi Refinement.

The foundations of the Zhengyi Sect were considered extensive, possessing dozens of Spirit Stones of varying quality. However, after two thousand years, the Spirit Stones had long been depleted, and the last one was used by the Old Heavenly Master during his Energy Introduction.

Thus, although the Old Heavenly Master was talented and possessed the Method of Cultivation, without the aid of Spiritual Energy, he could not break through the limit of his lifespan and could only calmly await death.

The Old Heavenly Master felt no regrets; in his youth, he witnessed the peak of Blue Star, in his middle age, he survived the zombie crisis, and in his later years, he assisted in the rebuilding of Blue Star. He had a rich life and would die with no regrets.

His only regret was that his low Cultivation Level was not very helpful in the apocalyptic situation; unable to withstand bullets even with the initial three levels of Qi Refinement, he was merely like an ordinary person with good martial abilities, only able to save people with his meager efforts.

Suddenly, his body shuddered, his eyes wide; just then, he felt a cool breath pass through his chest.

"Is it Spiritual Energy?!"

How was this possible? It had been many years since he last felt the rhythmic dance of Spiritual Energy.

One after another, waves of Spiritual Energy kept coming, nourishing his body.

The Old Heavenly Master confirmed it, tears of excitement uncontrollably streaming down his face. This was indeed Spiritual Energy!

"Has the End of Dharma Era ended?"

Simultaneously, those famous sects and temples all felt the wash of Spiritual Energy.

In Ancient Times, the people living at the North Star saw the outbreak of an Immortal Battle, and they packed up and fled from the North Star, leaving behind some Spirit Stones and Cultivation Methods hurriedly, which became the foundation of future sects. The Spirit Stones handed down over time had long been used up.

Now, as Fairy Eternity’s Abode opened, the bursting forth of Spiritual Energy was healing Blue Star, renewing these sects, and ushering Blue Star into a new era—the Era of Spiritual Energy Resurgence.

Chapter 1339: 1338: Fairy Eternity’s Immortal Fall Land

Tang Yu, exhausted from a day’s work, dragged himself home to find nobody there except for the black cat meowing.

Normally, Tang Yu would have the energy to play with his beloved pet, but right now he felt hollow inside, haunted by the day’s criticisms from his boss and the break-up text from his girlfriend.

"Tang Yu, after being together for two years, you really lack ambition. You don’t know how to charm your superiors; what’s the use of just burying yourself in work? I see no future with you, let’s break up."

Tang Yu sighed, truly unable to engage in the repulsive act of pleasing such an immoral boss.

He glanced over at a brass tripod sitting on top of the TV stand, palm-sized, engraved with inexplicable patterns.

"If only you could be sold for a good price," Tang Yu shook his head helplessly.

This was an artifact handed down from his great-grandfather’s generation, allegedly passed down even from his great-grandfather, extent of transmission unknown, but the craftsmanship was exquisite, definitely an antique with significant history.

However, after consulting several antique experts multiple times, they all concluded it was a modern craft, even younger than him.

"Pah, what damn experts, they don’t recognize the real value," Tang Yu spat.

Worn out from the day, Tang Yu, halfway through undressing, was simply too sleepy and immediately fell asleep.

After he fell asleep, as Spiritual Energy revived, the brass tripod slightly trembled, runes inside flickered, and by midnight, a drop of thick Spiritual Water gathered inside, the brass tripod’s Creation Divine Liquid.

The black cat climbed next to the tripod, licking up the drop of Creation Divine Liquid, its fur growing increasingly glossy.

Suddenly in its mind appeared the origin of the brass tripod, an Ancient Spiritual Treasure used by the ancient people to worship Immortals, capable of condensing Creation Divine Liquid, which had many divine effects like improving one’s constitution and activating bloodlines.

"Da, Luo, Jue..." The black cat, in a female voice, started to speak human language, stutteringly pronouncing the name of a cultivation technique that also appeared in its mind.

Like Tang Yu’s situation, there were many, where Ancient Spiritual Treasures had lost nourishment from Spiritual Energy, degraded to mundane objects, scattered in the Mortal World.

With the resurgence of Spiritual Energy, these Spirit Treasures gradually came to life, revealing various mighty powers.

...

Entering the Abode, everyone realized this place was a Cave Heaven.

The Abode was not barren like Kunlun Mountain, but instead was brimming with vitality, an aura of Immortal Spiritual Energy circulating, even a single breath was believed to extend life, this aura was still useful for Lu Yang now.

It was hard to imagine that this was an Abode that had gone through 400,000 years; everything was as immaculate as if untouched by time, as if the owner of the house had just left.

And now, the owner of the house had returned.

The soft big bed, casually placed chairs, a blanket made from top-grade Demon Beast fur, a decorative bookshelf... Lu Yang found the furnishings in the Abode strikingly familiar, exactly like his Spiritual Space!

"Woo-hoo, back home!" The first thing Fairy Eternity did upon returning home was to dive onto the soft big bed, feeling immensely at home.

"This is Immortal Fairy’s home then," Bai Shuang and Xia He marveled repeatedly, according to mythology, this place should be called an Immortal Mansion, incredibly marvelous.

The two women were quickly drawn to the artifacts on the bookshelf, the books on it were decorative, and so were these artifacts.

Although they could not determine the origin of these artifacts, their aesthetics resonated; with just one glance, they found the craftsmanship of these bronze mirrors, vases, and incense burners exquisitely detailed, with beautiful patterns, making them hard to part with.

"These are all top-level Immortal Treasures," Lu Yang sighed. Even Semi-Immortals cherish owning a single Immortal Treasure as much as their lives, yet here they lay piled up like cabbages at the Fairy’s place.

For instance, in the vase, there was some Immortal Liquid. Just a drop could transform a Demon Beast, allowing it to grow into a great demon of a thousand years. Such a thing was definitely useless for Fairy Eternity; it was merely placed here for aesthetic.

Lu Yang clicked his tongue; he was doing fairly well now, but his Abode was rather modest compared to the Fairy’s extravagant display.

"Take whatever you like," Fairy Eternity said generously, adhering to the saying that one who accepts another’s gift often finds it difficult to go against them later. Essentially saying don’t bother her Little Yang after taking the items.

"That doesn’t seem right," the two women hesitated, unaware of Fairy Eternity’s ulterior motives, merely knowing from Lu Yang that these items were extremely precious and thus felt embarrassed to take them.

"What are these?" They inadvertently touched some transparent stones on the ground, resembling glass. The ground was covered with these, seemingly arranged according to some pattern.

"Empty Spirit Stones." Lu Yang picked up one then casually threw it away. These were all Top-grade Spirit Stones; their Spiritual Energy had already been released when the Abode’s seal was broken, dispersing into the outside world and improving the environment of Blue Star.

Suddenly, Lu Yang paused, picked up a Spirit Stone to examine, then continuously picked up several more. He discovered that these Spirit Stones were intact and not as he initially thought - that the Spirit Stones broke and the Spiritual Energy leaked out and gathered in the Abode.

Does this mean that these Spirit Stones were once extracted by Fairy Eternity for her Cultivation?

Lu Yang quickly realized something; this Abode was not just Fairy Eternity’s home but also her place of Cultivation.

When Lu Yang was Crossing the Divinity Transformation Tribulation, he encountered an idea left in the Heavenly Tribulation by Yingtian Immortal, who said that half an hour after Fairy Eternity entered the Abode, she was attacked by someone hidden from sight, and she didn’t even realize how the attacker had managed to enter the Abode.

This meant that Fairy Eternity’s Abode was also her land of Immortal Fall!

The hidden attacker had made their move right here!

However, seeing Fairy Eternity’s relaxed demeanor upon returning home, she probably hadn’t realized this herself.

Lu Yang remembered that Fairy Eternity always had amnesia regarding the details of her assassination; she couldn’t recall what had happened at that time.

Originally lying on the bed, Fairy Eternity sat up, exclaimed softly, touched her chin, walked out of the Abode, then reentered, sat at a table, and made some gestures as if she was writing something. She then counted the Spirit Stones on the ground and sat cross-legged on the bed, adopting the standard posture for Enlightenment.

"Fairy, what’s going on with you?" Seeing Fairy Eternity’s strange behavior, Lu Yang pulled Bai Shuang and Xia He aside to prevent them from being bumped into by the Fairy.

Lu Yang faintly realized what Fairy Eternity was doing.

"I always feel like I’ve cultivated following this procedure before," she said, not hearing Lu Yang’s question, completely absorbed in her world, murmuring in a low voice, which made Lu Yang’s heart skip a beat.

Fairy Eternity sat cross-legged on the bed for a moment, then elongated her voice in realization, "Oh—"

"I remember now, at that time I had an epiphany and rushed to the Abode to meditate and cultivate."

"Let me think, what was the enlightenment about... Ah, I remember now, I figured out how to breakthrough to the All-Knowing Realm, aiming to breakthrough to that Realm in one go!"

Hearing this, Lu Yang felt as if struck by lightning, a dreadful thought flashed through his mind, the puzzle that had always confused him seemed clear at this moment.

He was wrong; his previous thoughts were all incorrect. The realm of the hidden attacker wasn’t just that of an Immortal, but Transcendence!

Chapter 1340: 1339: The Realm of the Mastermind Behind the Scenes

"Fairy, do you still remember how to break through to All-Knowing?" Lu Yang casually asked, after all, he wasn’t even an Immortal, so he didn’t consider such distant matters.

Immortal Fairy gently shook her head: "I can’t recall."

She could only remember so many things.

"Alright." Lu Yang didn’t show much disappointment, but his heart had already stirred up towering waves.

How had he never realized before that Transcendence and Omniscience were two completely opposite realms, and these two great realms simply couldn’t coexist!

The Transcended individuals severed Causality, making the world forget them, truly disappearing from the world.

The Omniscient knew everything, all beings and events, past occurrences in their grasp, including the existence of the Transcended!

Once an Omniscient appears, they would know about the Transcended, and Immortal Fairy had long said that "knowing" itself could establish causality!

Theoretically, if an Omniscient and a Transcended establish causality, the Transcended would fall from the "Transcendence" realm!

No wonder he wanted to kill Immortal Fairy; once the Fairy breaks through to the All-Knowing realm, the unlucky one would be the puppeteer behind the scenes!

Lu Yang guessed that at that time Immortal Fairy was amidst a breakthrough, in a mysterious state, unguarded against the hidden enemy, thus he succeeded easily.

Severing causality, the puppeteer cultivated to the Transcendence realm, definitely at a great cost, as seen from Ashy Bean destroying the Fire Dynasty and hiding ten thousand years of history.

Breaking off history is no easy task; is the history before Ancient Times vague really because it’s ancient and lacks recording methods?

Lu Yang still remembered his magic skill "Shrink Inch," speculating it might have been used by ancient ancestors to standardize measurements.

Could it be that measurements had been unified once in ancient times, only for the puppeteer to disrupt the era, concealing history, causing measurements to be chaotic, necessitating the Five Ancient Immortals to unify measurements again?

The ancient magic skills passed down were incorrect versions, likely also the work of the puppeteer.

Did Ashy Bean try so hard to achieve Transcendence because she realized this?!

Lu Yang shook his head; Ashy Bean had fallen, and no one would provide answers now.

What exactly were the Transcended like, Lu Yang did not know, everything was just theory, but hints could be seen from Ashy Bean, a Half-step Transcender.

Immortals possess characteristics of being forgotten, and Ashy Bean’s being forgotten was even more pronounced; those she had encountered, if not of sufficient realm, couldn’t remember her existence.

What would a true Transcended look like, having forgotten characteristics to the extreme, everything they do, regardless of realm, would the world forget them?

This implies, the world forgetting Immortal Fairy’s existence wasn’t the puppeteer’s Dao Fruit method, but a characteristic intrinsic to the Transcendence realm itself.

"How ridiculous I’ve been, speculating on the puppeteer’s Dao Fruit based on this characteristic," Lu Yang laughed at himself inwardly; what Causality Dao Fruit, what Verbal Spell Dao Fruit, all far from the truth, utterly absurdly wrong.

Transcendence, Omniscience, Harmonization, these three great realms should be parallel; the power shown by the puppeteer should absolutely not match that of Ancient Times, not an exchange with the Four Ancient Immortals but rather overwhelming domination.

He had never used his full strength all along!

Because he did not want to expose his own realm, revealing the realm would possibly let the Four Ancient Immortals deduce his origin, so he fashioned himself as a "powerful Immortal," preventing the Four Ancient Immortals from contemplating in that direction.

Destroying the Four Ancient Immortals is highly risky; seeing they could not beat the puppeteer, Time Immortal hid in the river of time, Ying Tian Immortal hid in heavenly tribulations, Jiuchong Immortal transformed into Yin and Yang, and Qilin Immortal turned into all things; these old cunning four had many escape techniques, and the puppeteer could not necessarily find them all.

The puppeteer acts cautiously, not taking such risks.

Fabricating identities was also something Ashy Bean had done; battling Meng Junzi in Buddha Country, she claimed to be "the founder of the Qi Cultivation System, the very first Immortal from Ancient Times," deceiving Meng Junzi.

This shows that a false identity can prevent establishing causality, therefore the puppeteer had hidden his strength, showing as the false identity of the Verbal Speller Yao Saint.

The real Yao Saint was killed by Immortal Fairy in Ancient Times; the Yao Saint appearing in Zhongshan County was but a false identity used by the puppeteer.

Initially, Lu Yang thought the puppeteer was the precursor of Time Immortal, Firmament Tree, but now it seems this deduction is also wrong.

If his deduction was right, akin to knowing the puppeteer’s identity, the puppeteer, sensing it, should have acted long ago.

Who could it be...

Lu Yang hastily stopped this thought; it was akin to declaring war on the puppeteer, his own Dao Fruit Prototype was not yet consummated, it wasn’t time to turn hostile.

"Not only human reincarnation, events also reincarnate," Lu Yang had an epiphany.

This was his insight when condensing the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype; now linking it with the puppeteer and Ashy Bean, his understanding deepened.

His long-held confusion dissolved, yet Lu Yang was not at ease; the puppeteer’s strength exceeded expectations.

"Fairy, should we move Mount Kunlun to the Cultivation World?" Seeing Immortal Fairy lazily lying on the bed, showing a very enjoyable expression, Lu Yang proactively suggested.

"Moving is such a hassle, just leave it here, it’s my territory anyway."

"That makes sense." Lu Yang nodded, not saying much more.

Though here was Immortal Fairy’s abode, there really wasn’t much worth staying for, Immortal Fairy lay a while longer and then yawned flying back into Lu Yang’s Spiritual Space.

Having spent a long time in Little Yang’s Spiritual Space, she found it more comfortable than the Abode.

As the four of them chatted and laughed, leaving Mount Kunlun, a dark figure appeared in the Abode, his eyes deep.

"Careless, I didn’t expect this boy’s old home to be North Star."

"Luckily, not completely careless." He chuckled picking up a tome of Cultivation Experience, insights by Immortal Fairy on breaking through to the All-Knowing Realm.

In Ancient Times, he had wanted to destroy this Cultivation Experience, but by then Immortal Fairy had been already a Half-step All-Knowing, with heavy Causality, even Transcendence characteristics couldn’t completely erase her existence, prompting the Four Ancient Immortals to furiously take action, and later Qinghe pushed away the North Star, losing the chance to destroy it.

Realizing this place was North Star, he sensed it first, thus he entered the Abode before Immortal Fairy, taking away this Cultivation Experience.

The Cultivation Experience read, I feel to become Omniscient, three conditions are needed:

To possess a Causality-related Dao Fruit, like my Eternity Dao Fruit.

To have lived two lives, I once self-severed in Half-Immortal Realm and was reborn.

Ensnared in causality.

A white flame ignited from the corner of the book, rapidly spreading, swallowing up this Cultivation Experience, burning it clean, as if it had never existed.

Chapter 1341: 1340: The Transcendent, Untouched by Causality

"Transcendence..."

The mastermind behind the scenes couldn’t help but recall events from the remote and ancient times as he watched the cultivation experiences burn to ashes.

After achieving transcendence, his very existence surpassed causality; his words and deeds untouched by it, unbound and undetectable by anyone, he could do whatever he desired.

He could incarnate into thousands, experiencing the myriad facets of life; he might become the leader of a tribe, ruling for hundreds of years, leading the tribe to grow and annexing others, or he could become a loose cultivator, calling friends for a drink and reveling, carefree for a millennium. Once he had had his fill and grown weary, he would vanish without a trace, and the world would self-correct what he had done.

In the tribe’s memory, there was someone else as their leader; his fellow loose cultivators also had no recollection of him, with other reasons explaining the events.
