He had numerous wives and had fathered many children, not needing to take responsibility for his actions.

He was lawless and indulged his desires, doing evil without restraint, also without needing to take responsibility for his actions.

"This is true Immortality, unbound by anything."

Even when he became aware that the Five Ancient Immortals had become Immortals one after another, he did not take them seriously.

What of becoming an Immortal? In the end, they would simply be transcendent like him; he was untouched by causality, and his existence would not affect other cultivators’ ability to achieve transcendence.

But, to his complete surprise, there turned out to be more than one path after becoming an Immortal; besides transcendence, there was also omniscience.

To avoid affecting himself, he attacked Fairy Eternity during her breakthrough, thinking it would be as usual—his actions would not attract causality, and people would forget about Fairy Eternity.

For ordinary Immortals, he could achieve no involvement with causality. For instance, during the Fire Dynasty era, he assassinated Time Immortal, and yet memories of Kirin Immortal himself killing Time Immortal automatically emerged, which is to transfer the causality he produced onto Kirin Immortal.

But he happened to target Fairy Eternity.

It was a long time before the mastermind realized that Fairy Eternity was half a step into the All-Knowing Realm, the causality was too significant to be completely eliminated, leading people to forget Fairy Eternity, but not the Four Ancient Immortals.

"At that time, I was not familiar enough with the All-Knowing Realm." The mastermind sighed—after all, it was a realm he had never encountered. If he had known more, he would definitely have chosen a different method to deal with Fairy Eternity, not such a crude act.

Forty thousand years have passed, and his understanding of the All-Knowing Realm is almost complete, knowing that there will be no one in this generation to ascend to transcendence and expose his identity.

"Although Lu Yang is entangled in causality, with every act impacting the world, the Trace Origin Dao Fruit is of the causality-severing kind; he cannot become all-knowing."

"Even though the Immortal Fairy possesses the Eternity Dao Fruit and had a breakthrough experience, that breakthrough was interrupted by me, and the accumulated causality also largely dispersed with the world’s fading memory."

"After her resurrection, she has been relying on Lu Yang’s spiritual space; the causality arising during this period transferred onto Lu Yang, making it difficult for her to achieve omniscience."

Naturally, he could not achieve omniscience either; he had already taken the path of transcendence, and to become all-knowing, he would have to relinquish his cultivation and start from scratch.

...

After the four returned to the villa, it took a few days to discover that the spiritual energy overflowing from the abode had actually brought Blue Star into an era of spiritual energy restoration.

Because the higher-ups approached Xia He, saying that recently a mysterious energy had appeared, according to the Zhengyi Sect and Dragon and Tiger Mountain among other famed Righteous Sects, this energy is called "Spiritual Energy," which can be used for cultivation to achieve immunity to all poisons and the healing of all diseases, there is a possibility that Xia He’s illness can be treated.

"It’s good news," Xia He said indifferently, without much response.

She guessed that the other party’s purpose was not only to tell her this good news but also wanted to see Lu Yang. Considering his special identity on Blue Star, which, in effect, once ruled over Blue Star and held a higher prestige than Xia He, meeting with the current rulers of Blue Star could be somewhat awkward, so he did not take the initiative to come forward.

The attention of the Blue Star’s senior officials was involuntarily drawn to the fruit platter on the table. Normally for meetings at their level, a fruit platter like this was merely decorative, but he couldn’t help feeling this platter was somehow "fragrant"?

It wasn’t the aroma of food; it felt more like a craving from the depths of the soul.

He had tasted a piece of peach with a toothpick and was amazed to feel his entire body become incredibly clear, his body also relaxed a lot, and various age-related diseases cured themselves.

Were these fruits brought by General Lu?

This made him think of the longevity peach from mythological legends, where the Old Heavenly Master from the Zhengyi Sect once said that the exotic treasures from the myths were not necessarily just myths, but things that had once existed, only to disappear with the arrival of the End of Dharma Era.

Old Xia couldn’t possibly be unaware of the effects of these fruits. Was she really still nearing the end of her life?

After the Blue Star’s high officials left, Lu Yang appeared.

"Wouldn’t have thought that the Zhengyi Sect and their lot knew about Spiritual Energy, they probably got their hands on some Ancient Texts," Lu Yang said with a smile. The revival of Spiritual Energy would surely be a good thing for Blue Star.

Originally his idea was to establish a Spatial Gate between the Cultivation World and Blue Star, but seeing Blue Star’s current situation, why not let Blue Star develop on its own for a while.

"I will give Blue Star a push," said Lu Yang. Right now, as Spiritual Energy is reviving, everyone is at the same starting line, including the government. There may be many problems during the revival stage of Spiritual Energy.

Cultivators are fine, as those with low cultivation levels won’t cause too much disturbance. The problem lies in those Spirit Treasures scattered across the lands, which are definitely considered superweapons. Given the current capabilities of the government, it’s hard to deal with them. Lu Yang decided to stay in his hometown for a while, helping Blue Star get through the initial stage.

"What do you plan to do, and what do you need me to do?" Xia He asked.

"Write some basic principles of utilizing Spiritual Energy and methods to test for Spirit Roots, and after you’ve written them, pass this information onto the higher-ups."

Then, Lu Yang took out a Storage Ring, which belonged to the Evil Monk Ban Bu.

"Let’s add a bit more Spiritual Energy to Blue Star," he said.

An old Transcendence Tribulation Stage cultivator’s Storage Ring held an impressive amount of Spirit Stones. Lu Yang forcefully wiped the Restrictions, took out piles of Spirit Stones, crushed them into gravel, and released the Spiritual Energy, making it denser on Blue Star.

"Wow, so many Spirit Stones." Bai Shuang’s eyes gleamed when she saw these stones. Ever since she came into contact with Spiritual Energy, she realized how hard her past life had been. As a Primordial Spirit, she couldn’t even use one ten-thousandth of her abilities, which was very shameful for a Primordial Spirit.

"Why are they all dispersing?" Bai Shuang watched helplessly as clusters of Spiritual Energy floated away, looking enviously on.

"With me here, how could Sister Bai lack for Spirit Stones?" Lu Yang joked, taking out piles of Top-grade Spirit Stones from his Identity Jade. Although he was as poor as a beggar compared to Fairy Eternity, compared to a Transcendance Tribulation Stage cultivator, he was rich enough to make them green with envy.

"From now on, I’ll take care of all of Sister Bai’s Cultivation Resources."

Bai Shuang had never known that being provided for could be such a blissful thing.

Chapter 1342: 1341: Time to Go Back

The night was deep and the world silent when Tang Yu took a quick shower and scrolled through some short videos.

Now that the revival of Spiritual Energy was no longer a secret, the internet was often filled with short videos of Cultivators clashing, drawing talismans with their fingers, calling the wind and summoning the rain in intensely exciting battles. The video he was watching was of two Cultivators dueling in such a manner.

Tang Yu watched with great envy. He had tested his aptitude and found he had no Spirit Root, rendering him incapable of Cultivation.

However, his life had improved—perhaps in a pang of conscience, his boss treated him gingerly, the company also highly valued him, promoting and making extensive use of him. His ex-girlfriend had also, for some reason, come back to him crying, apologizing and saying that she shouldn’t have abandoned him.

"Maybe it’s the good luck you brought me." Tang Yu said, smiling as he petted the black cat on its head. The cat nuzzled up to him and meowed affectionately a few times.

Tang Yu continued to scroll through several more short videos, the majority concerning Cultivators. Another news piece reported a suspected Cultivator serial killer who had fled to their city. The Cultivation Affairs Administration had already dispatched officers to track him down, advising everyone to go out less at night.

Possessing extraordinary powers often inflames one’s inner desires, which is why from sects of the Cultivation World to those on Blue Star, emphasis is placed on the nature of one’s character.

"Cultivators aren’t all good people, huh?" muttered Tang Yu before yawning and drifting off to sleep.

After he peacefully fell asleep, the black cat transformed into a cat-eared girl, quietly picked up the Brass Tripod, and climbed out the window, leaping nimbly from roof to roof between buildings.

Having achieved some proficiency in her Cultivation, she first intimidated Tang Yu’s ex-girlfriend and his supervisor, and since then, she patrolled the city every night, fighting evil and acting the hero, giving rise to urban legends about her.

Acting heroically was her hobby, and she discovered that ever since her urban legends began, the speed at which the Brass Tripod condensed the Creation Divine Liquid had increased, spurring her on to greater effort.

"Ah—!" A sharp scream drew the cat-eared girl’s attention. It came from several female students who had stayed out late.

A thug wielding the Cold Light Dagger charged at the girls.

Clang—

The Brass Tripod and the Cold Light Dagger collided.

The female students looked up and saw a figure in black with cat-like ears moving through the moonlight, descending from above.

"Get out of here!" the cat-eared girl shouted at them.

Hearing the reprimand, the students didn’t stop to take pictures, instead fleeing in panic.

"A cat from the Demon Race? Good, now they’re gone. Your demon blood will serve as a substitute!" the criminal growled in a raspy voice, as if he hadn’t spoken in a long time, licking the dagger before attacking the cat-eared girl.

The thug was skilled, making him the toughest opponent the cat-eared girl had faced, but she still prevailed and killed the attacker.

She hit the thug with the Brass Tripod a few times to make sure he was truly dead before crouching down to inspect him.

"Hmm, this doesn’t look like a fresh corpse," the cat-eared girl observed, realizing the thug had been dead for at least a month.

The thug’s right hand twitched, and the Cold Light Dagger flew towards the girl’s heart!

"This is bad!" The cat-eared girl inwardly cursed; the dagger was too close for her to dodge!

Suddenly, a figure appeared, and two fingers firmly caught the Cold Light Dagger.

"As a Spirit Device, you’ve sucked your master’s blood dry and now control his corpse to seek virgin blood," Lu Yang commented, clutching the quivering Cold Light Dagger.

"You’re not of much use anymore."

With a bit of force between his fingers, he snapped the Cold Light Dagger in two.

The cat-eared girl’s eyes widened: "Who are you?"

She knew the hardness of the Cold Light Dagger—it had clashed with the Brass Tripod several times without faltering, yet this man broke it effortlessly.

"The Creation God Tripod is in your possession; you seem to be quite lucky," Lu Yang said with a smile.

The cat-eared girl covertly activated the Brass Tripod, warily watching Lu Yang, shocked that he knew the real name of the Brass Tripod!

The Brass Tripod kept trembling, seemingly fearful of the person in front of it.

"The bloodline of the Nine-tailed Spirit Cat, cultivating the Daluo Technique, that’s not a bad combination, no wonder you’ve achieved today’s cultivation level."

"Relax a bit, the Creation God Tripod is indeed a fine Spirit Treasure, but it’s not that significant in my presence." Lu Yang soothed the cat-eared girl, having taken an interest in her for a while and deeming her a promising junior.

"How about considering joining the Cultivation Affairs Administration? Your cultivation level is top-notch among the commissioners."

"Cultivation Affairs Administration? You’re with the official organization?" The cat-eared girl was shocked; to think the officials had such a powerful expert.

"I’m sort of halfway in. The Cultivation Affairs Administration was established on my suggestion." Lu Yang said with a smile, dropping the bombshell news.

During these days, Lu Yang had been secretly scouting for talented cultivators, recruiting them into the official fold, boosting the official power, and the cat-eared girl’s talent was top-notch among those he had scouted.

The cat-eared girl loved her freedom and wasn’t too willing to join an official organization.

Seeing her hesitation, Lu Yang further added, "There are some Artificial Spiritual Roots in the administration that I’ve donated. As long as the contribution is significant enough, you can obtain them."

"Artificial Spiritual Roots?" The cat-eared girl’s heartbeat slowed for a beat—wasn’t that exactly what Tang Yu needed most?

Realizing the cat-eared girl’s reluctance to leave Tang Yu, Lu Yang continued, "The administration isn’t as restrictive as you think. There’s no need to clock in for work; you just need to finish assignments."

Upon hearing this, the cat-eared girl no longer hesitated: "Okay, I’ll join!"

"But won’t your Human race discriminate against us from the Demon Race?" The ancestral memories in the cat-eared girl’s bloodline contained some ancient fragments, hinting that the relationship between the Human race and the Demon Race wasn’t that great.

"The people I introduce are too busy welcoming you to discriminate. Come on, follow me to meet the other commissioners." Lu Yang walked slowly into the night with his hands behind his back, followed closely by the cat-eared girl.

...

"Oh, Elder Xia—"

"Can’t the revival of spiritual energy save Elder Xia?"

"We’re still one step too late; if only the spiritual energy had revived a bit earlier, perhaps Elder Xia could have held on!"

At Xia He’s Spirit Hall, people cried and wailed to the heavens, all genuinely saddened about Xia He’s departure.

Those who attended Xia He’s funeral were either political figures, former subordinates of Xia He, or elderly who had lived through the apocalyptic times; their cries were constant, and the atmosphere was somber.

They held lilies, Xia He’s favorite flower, lining up to place them one by one beside the coffin.

It seemed that even the heavens were reluctant to witness Xia He’s departure, as a light drizzle began to fall.

Not far from the Spirit Hall, two figures under umbrellas stood in the rain, unnoticed by anyone.

"What’s it like to attend your own funeral? Such an experience is quite rare." Lu Yang teased, standing beside was none other than the owner of the funeral, Xia He.

At that moment, Xia He removed her necklace and no longer disguised herself as an old woman, revealing her original pure and striking appearance.

She rolled her eyes: "I want to offer a bouquet to myself."

"Decided to leave Blue Star?" Lu Yang asked again to confirm, to avoid any regret from Xia He.

Xia He pointed at the Spirit Hall: "Do you think I still have a chance to back out?"

Having interacted for so long, Xia He had long understood Lu Yang’s attitude; Blue Star was Lu Yang’s "birthplace," but not his "home."

This was true for the Immortal ancestors as well; they couldn’t stay on Blue Star forever.

So, she decided to follow Lu Yang to the Cultivation World, along with Bai Shuang.

"Yes, it’s time to go back."

Chapter 1343: 1342: Lu Yang is Rarer than a Primordial Spirit

After several months, Lu Yang finally piloted the Black Canopy Boat back to the Cultivation World, more than doubling the time it took from the Cultivation World to Blue Star.

Xia He had never been to the Universe, the sensation of weightlessness, the close-up view of the sun, moon, and stars... What a rare experience this was, and even though Bai Shuang had traveled on a spacecraft, it could not compare to the novelty provided by the Black Canopy Boat.

Therefore, it took them a long time on the way.

Lu Yang could understand this feeling, after all, he had played around in the Universe for quite some time.

Besides, they had encountered quite a few troubles along the way: wormholes suddenly appeared, transporting them to unknown regions of the Universe, they rescued a silicon-based civilization, acted as gods in the kingdom of little people, encountered the orphan of a race of giants, killed a few space monsters, as well as a giant monster that formed after the smaller monsters unified, and then found their way back, smoothly returning to the Cultivation World.

"There are so many kinds of life in the Universe." After the trip, Xia He and Bai Shuang had their eyes widely opened; these were scenes they had only seen in science fiction movies, but now they had experienced them personally.

Lu Yang was muttering in his heart. Neither the Interstellar Alliance nor the Candle Sky Civilization, two powerful cosmic civilizations, had ever seen so many strange cosmic races. How come he encountered them all in one trip?

"It’s normal. I often come across these things when I traveled the Universe. It’s no longer strange to me." Lu Yang said casually, and from his tone, one could easily imagine how rich his past experiences were; these were nothing but travel anecdotes to him.

"Really? Then why haven’t you told us about these things before?" Xia He raised an eyebrow, keenly spotting the loophole.

Lu Yang coughed violently twice to cover up his embarrassment: "I’ve experienced too much, I was afraid you would get tired of hearing about it."

"Really?" Xia He looked at Lu Yang with a half smile, as if seeing through his lies.

"Truly, when have I ever lied to you?"

The conversation would have been fine if not for that comment. Upon hearing what Lu Yang said, Xia He immediately got irritated: "Have you lied to me only a few times? You told me the main street was very safe, and as it turned out, you tricked me to be the bait on the main street where zombies flocked out, treating me like it was mealtime in the canteen!"

"And you said you were going to the Corpse King competition, claiming you would quickly return after becoming the Corpse King. I was supposed to watch the safe zone for a bit. Was that ’quick’? You didn’t come back for so long, I even thought something happened to you outside!"

"And there was one time when you..."

"Stop talking, stop talking!" Lu Yang hurriedly interjected as Xia He recounted his unscrupulous deeds, his old face turning red.

Bai Shuang was listening with her head down, not daring to make a sound. She had witnessed all of these deeds by Lu Yang firsthand and had been thoroughly amused.

"Wow, we’re back at the Cultivation World, we’re home!" Fairy Eternity exclaimed with great exaggeration, seizing the opportunity to interrupt Xia He’s grievances.

Lu Yang was very grateful, not expecting Fairy Eternity to be so reliable at times.

This statement indeed captured Xia He’s attention. She looked up and saw the immeasurably vast Cultivation World, her heart skipping a beat.

Even after hearing from Lu Yang countless times how grand the Cultivation World was and how insignificant planets were in front of it, it was incomparable to seeing it with her own eyes.

"So this is the Cultivation World..."

The entrance to the Cultivation World bustled with spacecraft coming and going, all from other planets. All civilizations were intensely curious about the Cultivation World; any blogger who came here to shoot a video would garner an astonishing number of clicks, their follower count skyrocketing.

As for commercial collaborations, needless to say, the Cultivation World was a treasure that countless businessmen vied desperately to have a share of. The only problem was when these business magnates witnessed the Cultivation World’s ability to turn stone into gold, they were all dumbfounded.

Nowadays, traversing the Universe was no longer exclusive to Body Integration Realm cultivators; even Low-Level Cultivators and ordinary people could travel to other planets by spaceship.

The Black Canopy Boat entered the Cultivation World, and Xia He felt a bit dizzy due to the strange structure of the Cultivation World, but she quickly adapted.

Lu Yang urged the Black Canopy Boat forward, returning to Seek Dao Sect.

At the entrance to Seek Dao Sect, amidst the bamboo forest, two figures and a metallic box were sitting by the river playing games.

"Jump, jump!"

"Don’t rush, don’t rush."

With a snap, the game console was broken.

Daoist Xiantian looked innocently at the River Spirit: "You see, it’s almost all because you were rushing me."

The River Spirit blew his mustache and glared: "Can you blame me for rushing? You’ve died in this level so many times!"

Daoist Xiantian threw the broken game console into the river, and the River Spirit also jumped into the river.

Before long, mist rose from the surface of the river, and the River Spirit slowly ascended, revealing two game consoles in his palm, his expression gentle: "Daoist Xiantian, is this the broken game console you dropped, or is it this perfectly intact one?"

"The broken game console."

"Such an honest child, then this broken game console is for you."

"Hey, shouldn’t you give me the good game console, mom!" Daoist Xiantian was anxious,

The River Spirit couldn’t hear Daoist Xiantian and patted the Ether Computer next to him: "Let’s start a game."

While saying this, the River Spirit looked up and sensed the presence of Bai Shuang.

"What’s wrong?" the Ether Computer couldn’t understand.

"Where has that boy Lu Yang gone off to again, and now he’s brought back another Primordial Spirit?"

"Eh? Are we Primordial Spirits that common?" The Ether Computer had been proud when it heard Primordial Spirits were rare, but now it seemed Primordial Spirits weren’t that scarce after all.

"Anyway, not as rare as Lu Yang."

...

Atop the Sky Gate Mountain, Yun Mengmeng had just baked a batch of flower cakes for Qinghe to taste.

Qinghe had disappeared since before Lu Yang’s last seclusion and had only shown up today for a reason.

"Little Lotus, are you suggesting that our Secret Realm’s Mother-child River was created by the Chief Housekeeper?"

"Yes," Qinghe confidently answered. In search of the lost Mother-child River, she had traveled far and wide, visiting countless ancient relics and Secret Realms, until at last, she found clues to the Mother-child River and discovered it was Yun Mengmeng’s hometown.

Hearing this, Yun Mengmeng felt dizzy and couldn’t make sense of the relationships. She asked Qinghe to explain, but even Qinghe couldn’t make it clear.

"Forget it, I won’t think about it anymore. It’s enough knowing that I’m even closer to the Chief Housekeeper," Yun Mengmeng never troubled herself with things she couldn’t understand. It was better to enjoy life than to waste energy trying to figure things out.

"We’re back!" Lu Yang climbed up Sky Gate Mountain, smiling and greeting Yun Mengmeng and Qinghe.

"Sister Mengmeng, you’re back! And why have you brought a woman with you!" Yun Mengmeng was startled, in romance novels, usually, this behavior was called "village chief’s wife"?

Lu Yang had a full head of black lines: "Sister Mengmeng, you should read more books."

"But I learned this from reading books!" Yun Mengmeng argued righteously – how else could she know so much without reading?

"Chief Mom, you’re back!"

Xia He looked uncertainly at the panic-stricken Fairy Eternity: "Immortal Fairy Eternity, your child is already this big?"

I suppose as an Ancient Immortal, you really don’t show your age, whether it’s thousands or tens of thousands of years.

"I’m not, I don’t have, she’s talking nonsense!"

Chapter 1344: 1343: Meng Semi-Immortal

After a noisy fuss, the matter finally settled down with Fairy Eternity sternly warning Yun Mengmeng not to call her that way.

"Why shouldn’t I..." Yun Mengmeng didn’t understand; she felt that she hadn’t called her incorrectly.

"And don’t ask why either!" Fairy Eternity said fiercely.

Fairy Eternity felt that nothing had gone right since she left the Peach Blossom Secret Realm, with Xia He and Bai Shuang flirting and competing for Little Yang’s attention, followed by Yun Mengmeng calling her ’big mom’.

It must have been Miss Yun, that naughty girl, secretly casting minor curses on her, causing her such misfortune.

"Let’s go, time to settle the score with Miss Yun!"

Fairy Eternity spoke angrily, dragging Lu Yang off to find Yun Zhi.

Yun Zhi had already heard the noise when Lu Yang arrived; it would have been strange if she hadn’t heard such a commotion.

"Miss Yun, not even a welcome for our return!" Fairy Eternity stormed into the Yun Zhi Cave Mansion with what she thought was a formidable gait that was actually unrecognizable to even her closest relations.

Yun Zhi’s eyes twitched. She thought that her junior brother’s visit home could be extended a bit, perhaps for another ten or eight years.

Primarily because Fairy Eternity had teased her too ruthlessly in the Peach Blossom Secret Realm, and she still felt emotional fluctuations upon seeing Fairy Eternity.

"Big mom..."

"You’re not allowed to call me that either!" Fairy Eternity jumped in anger; this wasn’t like the time when she controlled Yun Zhi in the Peach Blossom Secret Realm. Now, she’d get irritated with anyone who called her that.

"Eldest Senior Sister, please come to the Spiritual Space for a moment," Lu Yang said.

Yun Zhi’s primordial spirit left her body and entered Lu Yang’s forehead.

"What’s the matter?" Yun Zhi knew that entering the Spiritual Space meant there was something they needed to discuss without letting the mastermind behind the scenes know.

"In fact, we have found the home of Fairy Eternity, the North Star, which is also my home..."

Lu Yang explained everything about discovering Fairy Eternity’s Cave Mansion on Blue Star, how Fairy recovered some of her memories, and their speculations about the mastermind’s realm.

"Is he Transcendence?" Fairy Eternity was very surprised; this was a thorny issue.

"Transcendence, indeed. This would explain most of the matters," Yun Zhi nodded, her expression turning serious. This was a significant discovery as they had underestimated the mastermind’s realm before.

"Omniscience, Transcendence, Harmonization, the three great realms above Immortals. Judging from the results, Transcendence and Harmonization do not interfere with each other, while Omniscience can cause those in the Transcendence realm to fall and those in Harmonization to retain their humanity." Yun Zhi pondered, realizing the extraordinary nature of Omniscience after considering the three realms together, which she hadn’t done before, until her junior brother pointed it out.

"However, it doesn’t affect the overall plan. Continue with the original plan," Yun Zhi said. The realm of Transcendence was indeed powerful, but she firmly believed there is a hierarchy within the three realms, with Harmonization being stronger than Transcendence.

From the methods they used, it appeared so.

To achieve Transcendence, one needed to sever ties with history, conceal oneself, and create endless killings.

Her path of Harmonization, however, didn’t rely on external forces and was detached from worldly matters, and she could reach the Harmonization Realm on her own abilities.

If they were pure Seekers who viewed the realm as everything, they would have already achieved Harmonization.

Although Yun Zhi was a Seeker and passionate about cultivation, that was her hobby.

She had her junior brother, Fairy Eternity, and the Seek Dao Sect... all things she couldn’t bear to lose or forget. Blindly pursuing realms was to neglect the essentials for the sake of trivialities and to confuse priorities.

"Can Fairy Eternity not remember how to break through the All-Knowing Realm?"

Fairy Eternity shook her head decisively: "I don’t remember."

"Since only those who have come into contact with Cause and Effect Dao Fruits can achieve Omniscience, this realm must be related to causality. Junior Brother, you might as well go and see Junior Brother Meng."

"Find Old Meng? Where is he now?"

"Selling divinations on the streets of the Imperial City."

...

"Take a look, the Iron Mouth of the Imperial City—double your money back if the prediction is wrong."

On the streets of the Imperial City, the fortune teller occasionally called out. Behind him stood a conspicuous white flag with the words "Meng Semi-Immortal" written in large characters.

The little chubby boy walked down the street, carrying a basket of freshly baked buns that were glistening with oil. His attire seemed ordinary but was in fact all high-grade defensive magic artifacts.

Little Fatty noticed the fortune-teller, his interest piqued. He found a little stool to sit on, and asked in a very casual tone: "Hey fortune-teller, are your predictions accurate?"

The fortune-teller pointed his thumb back at the gold-lettered sign: "See that? ’Iron-mouthed Fortune-teller of the Imperial City’. Could that be fake?"

"How much?"

The fortune-teller sized up Little Fatty: "Seeing as you haven’t started your cultivation, a single low-grade Spirit Stone will do."

Little Fatty opened his cloth pouch, revealing just a few pitiable low-grade Spirit Stones, and hesitantly took one out.

As the grandson of the Minister of Justice, he was raised with strict discipline and had very little pocket money.

"Here, you better make an accurate prediction."

The fortune-teller accepted the Spirit Stone: "So, what do you want me to predict?"

"Can you predict the final exam questions for my Sacrificial Officer teacher?" Little Fatty’s eyes gleamed with the idea that if he scored well on the final exam, his allowance might increase.

The fortune-teller silently returned two Spirit Stones to Little Fatty; this question was beyond his abilities.

"Before you go, let me give you a warning. You may face a disaster of bloodshed in the near future."

Little Fatty didn’t buy this at all, recognizing it as a scam to make money.

After Little Fatty left, the fortune-teller called over the housekeeper hiding nearby: "Go tell Master Xu, the Minister of Justice, that his grandson is on the streets asking fortune-tellers about final exam questions."

"Yes, sir."

Soon another customer sat down. It was the fortune-teller’s good friend: "Meng the Semi-Immortal, you can’t figure out anything and still you do fortune-telling?"

Meng the Semi-Immortal snorted coldly: "What does that brat know? I specialize in predicting marriage fortunes."

"Back from your hometown?" Meng Jingzhou noticed that Lu Yang appeared lively, probably having enjoyed himself there, perhaps even playing the saint in front of others every day.

"Why the heck are you setting up a stall and playing the charlatan?"

Meng Jingzhou glared: "What charlatan? Choose your words. This is a legitimate business!"

"Isn’t fortune-telling the business of the Heavenly Strategy Sect? Does the Heavenly Strategy Sect owe the Meng Family so much money that they taught you their specialty as a debt repayment?"

"You don’t understand." Meng Jingzhou pointed at the flag behind him, "See these four characters? I am now a Semi-Immortal!"

"You’ve really become a Semi-Immortal? What kind of Dao Fruit Outline have you formed?"

Meng Jingzhou smiled smugly: "The Outline of the Cause and Effect Dao Fruit. Do you think I’m just setting up a stall for fun? I’m cultivating the Outline of the Dao Fruit through fortune-telling!"

The Cause and Effect Dao Fruit Outline, a Dao Fruit Outline once possessed by the second Imperial Teacher of Great Yu, and Guan Shanhai knows the key to its formation.

Naturally, Meng Jingzhou also has a talent for this. He mastered the Causality Divine Ability sooner than Lu Yang, with the Single Curse Fist being an example.

"Right now, I’m mainly focusing on cultivating the marriage fortunes," Meng Jingzhou said.

"But I’ve encountered a little problem."

"What problem?"

"I can only predict marriage fortunes for others, not for myself." This baffled Meng Jingzhou quite a bit.

"...Is it possible that you have no marriage fortune in this lifetime?"

"Are you joking? What status do I hold, how can I have no marriage fortune? Old Lu, you’re just jealous because I have the Outline of the Cause and Effect Dao Fruit. Don’t believe me? I’ll show you."

"How will you show me?"

"Let’s look at your causality."

"Oh? Let’s try it then." Lu Yang became interested, now understanding why Eldest Senior Sister suggested he find Old Meng.

Meng Jingzhou put on a pose, activated the Outline of the Cause and Effect Dao Fruit, and mana converged in his eyes as he could see all causality in the world.

"Causality, appear!"

He suddenly opened his eyes, caught a glimpse of the dazzling and dense causality around Lu Yang, and screamed in pain.

"Ah, my eyes!"

Chapter 1345: 1344: Femme Fatale

Meng Jingzhou covered his eyes, screaming in agony, causing the people around to crowd and watch, which gave Lu Yang quite a scare.

"Hey, hey, hey, if you’re telling fortunes just stick to that, how come you’ve also learned to pull a scam, can’t you learn something decent?"

Passersby pointed and made comments about Meng Jingzhou; being the local populace, they often saw him around here.

"He’s definitely trying to swindle someone."

"Yeah, he’s been setting up his stall here for so long and hardly has any customers, he must be making money with these kinds of tricks."

"Swindle my ass." Meng Jingzhou retorted angrily. Normally, it was all smooth sailing, but today, of all days, he, a prestigious Semi-Immortal, was being mistaken for a street hustler just because he encountered Old Lu.

With a snap of his fingers, he temporarily shielded the passersby’s perception of the two of them. The passersby looked confused, forgetting what happened here and completely ignored the two living beings in front of them as they continued shopping.

Lu Yang secretly nodded in approval; this trick he also saw demonstrated by the second Imperial Teacher of Great Yu, a truly divine skill.

Meng Jingzhou rubbed his eyes; fortunately, he was only dazzled for a moment, and the impact wasn’t too serious—his eyes were just a bit red.

He shook his head slightly, his gaze somewhat hazy, seeing double visions of people.

"Old Lu, the Causality entangling you is ridiculously heavy, I’ve never seen this much even when looking at the Causality lines of Ancestor Jiang and Ancestor Meng."

After forming the outline of the Cause and Effect Dao Fruit, Meng Jingzhou tested it on Jiang Pingan and Meng Junzi, to study and observe the Causality on them.

Being an Immortal, the Dao Fruit Outline could barely affect them due to their high level of cultivation. It’s not easy to perceive Causality, and it took Meng Jingzhou several tries to get a rough idea, but the insights he gained were worth it.

When he observes beings at the same level as himself, there aren’t that many restrictions, and he could look freely.

For instance, he could clearly see the Causality entwining Lu Yang.

However, the price was obviously apparent: the extensive Causality around Lu Yang exceeded Meng Jingzhou’s capacity to bear.

Lu Yang felt a bit guilty, figuring that Meng Jingzhou’s eyesight wasn’t faked: "Could you have seen it wrong, why not take another look?"

"Get lost, jerk."

If it weren’t for the crowded street with many prying eyes, Meng Jingzhou would have loved to give the smug Lu Yang a good kick or two.

After a good while, Meng Jingzhou’s eyesight returned to normal.

"I was wondering why I, such an honest person, always find trouble wherever I go; turns out the reason was you!" Meng Jingzhou felt he could finally clear his name of these unjust accusations, having solidified the Cause and Effect Dao Fruit outline was indeed worthwhile.

Heaven be pitied, before he knew Lu Yang, he was at most a domineering figure in Imperial City, recklessly influential among his peers.

Ever since he got to know Lu Yang, he imprudently invited Eldest Senior Sister to discuss how to cheat on a horse carriage, followed by getting into fights involving Semi-Immortals and Immortals as if it were all as common as having dinner, and even his own ancestor couldn’t cover all the troubles that ensued.

"You weren’t faking just now, were you?" Lu Yang was skeptical. "When the second Imperial Teacher of Great Yu looked at my Causality, there wasn’t such a big reaction."

"Ptui, how can it be the same? Back then, you were at the Golden Core Stage, how much Causality could you have amassed."

"Old Lu, it’s not just the quantity of your Causality, the quality is also extraordinary. I can vaguely sense the Emperor’s Aura within your Causality."

"Emperor’s Aura?" Lu Yang’s expression became strange, "Where would I get any Emperor’s Aura from? Old Meng, are you sure your eyesight is fine?"

Meng Jingzhou nodded, this was his area of expertise, and he wouldn’t have seen it wrong: "This Emperor’s Aura, Ancestor Jiang has it too, but the one in your Causality is even denser, indicating that you will become an even mightier emperor than Ancestor Jiang in the future!"

"But rest easy, as a good brother, I’d definitely not disclose something like this. Just give me a heads-up when you start a rebellion, I haven’t tried rebelling yet." Meng Jingzhou winked and squirmed his eyebrows, very interested in this topic.

"It’s indeed strange when you think about it. You are not yet an emperor, but you have a very stable Emperor’s Causality. When did you become the Crown Prince?" Meng Jingzhou stroked his chin in thought. This made no sense; could it be that the Cause and Effect Dao Fruit Outline was not cultivated properly and made a mistake?

That shouldn’t be the case; he’s never made a mistake observing the Causality of Immortals.

Lu Yang remained silent, as he believed that the aura of the emperor he possessed was not because of some rebellion but because he was the next in line to succeed the throne of the Great Bean Dynasty.

Speaking of which, the political system of the Great Bean Dynasty was quite progressive. Throughout history, which dynasty did not pass on the throne to one’s offspring? Only the Great Bean Dynasty would pass the throne to a Pillar Minister with no blood relation at all.

To disregard bloodlines and select the most capable among the ministers as the heir, this system was definitely quite advanced.

"Little Yang, you have accidentally spoken your thoughts out loud," Fairy Eternity reminded him. Although Lu Yang’s inner monologue was inadvertently spoken and his voice was soft, how could it possibly escape the hearing of an Immortal?

Fairy Eternity was quite happy to hear Little Yang’s thoughts.

Lu Yang was mortified. As a senior minister of the dynasty, he should have been composed. It was a primitive mistake to let his emotions slip uncontrollably: "Whenever I think of how wise His Majesty is and how much more advanced our Great Bean Dynasty’s system is compared to those feudal and antiquated dynasties, I can’t help but get carried away and lose control of my emotions. I won’t let it happen again."

Lu Yang returned to the present and continued to ask, "You just mentioned you are good at seeing romantic connections. Can you tell me what my prospects look like?"

Meng Jingzhou patted his chest and gestured with a thumbs-up. "We are brothers; talking about seeing it or not makes us seem distant. Who is the girl you fancy? I’ll set you up directly."

He revealed a confident smile: "Bet you didn’t expect this, but I can not only see Causality but also change it."

"Tell me, who do you have your eye on?"

While speaking, Meng Jingzhou suddenly shuddered.

"What’s wrong?" Lu Yang asked.

Meng Jingzhou scratched his head in confusion. "I don’t know. I suddenly felt a murderous aura, but now it’s gone. Didn’t you feel it?"

Lu Yang shook his head, bewildered. "No."

"Strange," Meng Jingzhou muttered under his breath. He roamed the stars, condensed the Dao Fruit Outline, told fortunes on the streets, everything went smoothly, but why did a series of unfortunate events happen ever since he met Lu Yang today?

"Never mind, let me calculate your romantic destiny," Meng Jingzhou said cautiously about directly peeking at romantic destiny again, as doing so could risk damaging his eyes.

He shook the copper coins in his hand. Any connoisseur who saw these coins would be startled—these were the model coins of the Great Qian, Great Yu, and Great Xia dynasties. All the circulating copper coins in the populace were cast based on these models.

Meng Jingzhou chanted and used the Copper Coins to fixate the shadow of Lu Yang’s romantic destiny, indirectly deducing it.

Lu Yang felt something off about his body but couldn’t point out what it was. Only by Fairy Eternity’s reminder did he understand.

"You are sensing that the Meng Family Kid is calculating your Causality again."

After Meng Jingzhou calculated the result, his expression was peculiar.

"A destiny entangled with a femme fatale? Prone to causing disputes and chaos across the world?"

That didn’t seem right.

Concerned that Old Lu would ridicule him again for inaccuracies, Meng Jingzhou decided to reveal only part of the fortune: "Old Lu, your luck in love affairs is pretty strong. There are quite a few who take an interest in you."

"Oh, is that so." Lu Yang was happy, expecting this result because he wouldn’t be fooled by Old Meng on such matters. He was not surprised by this outcome; after all, he was very famous, and there were admirers everywhere in the Cultivation World.

"Master Meng, is there any way to increase the number of my Causal connections?" Lu Yang asked humbly for advice.

Since the All-Knowing Realm is related to Causality, perhaps having more Causal connections makes it easier to advance to the All-Knowing Realm?

Meng Jingzhou’s corner of the eye twitched: "With the number of Causal connections you have, you better not deliberately find ways to increase them. It’s prone to backlash. Just leave Causality alone; it will grow on its own."

Chapter 1346: 1345: A Bustling World

After bidding farewell to Master Meng, Lu Yang returned to the Seek Dao Sect, where his life returned to peace, and there were seldom any recent movements heard about Lu Yang in the cultivation world and even across the entire universe, which had entered a phase of peaceful development.

Upon returning to the Seek Dao Sect, Lu Yang had not left the sect again. Now that the cultivation world had combined spiritual energy with technology, he could stay indoors and still stay informed about news big and small from across the universe.

Besides the internet, Lu Yang also learned about the outside world from the disciples of the Seek Dao Sect who went out for training.

"Young people are quite active," Lu Yang commented with a laugh. Even though the cultivation world had long lifted its wartime readiness, the geniuses that rose during the preparation period were numerous. They seized opportunities to soar to the heavens and carve out great reputations in the universe.

In terms of the number of geniuses, there are several times more now than during Lu Yang’s era. Some say this is the reemergence of the struggle of the Ancient Era.

Moreover, many major powers joined forces to host a grand contest of geniuses. Cultivators under a hundred years old could participate. Not only were there substantial cultivation resources as prizes, but the competitors could also become famous throughout the whole universe. The contest was broadcast live universally, with many dark horses emerging, truly a grand spectacle of the universe.

None of the geniuses conceded to each other, and various lost magic skills reemerged, with several epic comebacks performed.

Even the citizens of the cultivation world, accustomed to watching contests amongst geniuses, were surprised by such sights, not to mention people from other planets.

Xin Ding, a young monk Lu Yang had met in the Western Paradise Temple of Buddha Country, had become the top of the heavenly listing in Buddha Country. Wielding a sealed Sky Opening Axe, he defeated and converted his opponents.

Then there was Song Tianjing who Lu Yang had encountered at the Spirit Chef Competition. He had truly comprehended something from the Immortal Fairy’s "Complete Culinary Compendium", and his divine poison technique made him an opponent that all geniuses were reluctant to face.

Despite being expelled from the Spirit Chef World because of his poison technique, he made his mark in the Poison World and became a dread-inspiring rising star.

The only issue was that during his competition, no poison practitioners were present; they were all huddled in the Great Xia prison, silently cheering for Song Tianjing.

When it came to noticeable participants in the competition, Meng Semi-Immortal stood out the most.

Meng Jingzhou, who read fortunes and cultivated the Outline of Dao Fruit, also had not had any news for many years. When he appeared on stage, the audience was stunned momentarily. Wasn’t this a contest for geniuses? Why was Meng Jingzhou participating?

Then they realized, Meng Jingzhou’s cultivation level was too high; he was amongst the strongest in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, which made people subconsciously overlook his age; Meng Jingzhou had not yet reached a hundred years old and thus was still within the range of geniuses!

Lu Yang not participating, who could rival Meng Semi-Immortal? Even if everyone teamed up, they couldn’t shake his form in the slightest.

Those known as "mini Lu Yang" or "Meng Jingzhou’s successor" truly realized the gap between themselves and Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou at that moment.

People thought that if Meng Jingzhou appeared, Lu Yang would too, but they were disappointed. From the beginning to the end of the competition, Lu Yang did not appear.

Unsurprisingly, Meng Jingzhou won the championship.

The only time Lu Yang publicly appeared was at the wedding of Vast Sea Daoist Lord and Ao Ya, one from the Seek Dao Sect and the other from the Dragon Palace of the East Sea, both famously aggressive. When the two became a Daoist couple, all powers of the cultivation world came to congratulate them.

The Vast Sea Daoist Lord fulfilled his promise and set the wedding location in the universe. The attending powers showed their Divine Abilities by arriving on boats, gourds, or riding great demons, resembling a scene from ancient mythology.

The wedding was broadcast live across the entire universe. When other civilizations witnessed hundreds of True Dragons navigating through space, their minds went blank, stunned by the sight.

Even immortals were present, not just one; this was the first time immortals appeared in front of the entire universe. Continuous prayers for good luck to the immortals filled the live chat, and the donations were nonstop, making it a very lively event.

At this moment, Lu Yang became the less noticeable one.

Beings across the universe were not very familiar with Lu Yang, and seeing him, they had a hard time matching him with the legends.

However, they could discern beauty, and Yun Zhi, Yun Mengmeng, Qinghe, Gan Tian, Tao Yaoye, Lan Ting, and others looked like celestial beings, each captivating in their own right, garnering countless fans upon their appearance.

Various major advertisers also had the idea of hiring them for advertisements, but once they knew their identities, no one ever thought of it again.

After the wedding, the attention of the world once again focused on the struggles among the geniuses.

However, there were two cultivators, not considered geniuses, who drew everyone’s attention.

"Fang Shan lost? Wasn’t he claimed to be unbeatable at the Divinity Transformation Stage, how could he lose to a veteran of the same stage?"

"Lost to whom?"

"To someone named Mo Xiaochou, I heard he is a cultivator from Buddha Country, but he’s not very famous there, and disappeared for a long time, only to reappear and defeat Mo Xiaochou."

"There also appeared a very strong veteran cultivator in the Body Integration Realm, named Duan Qingshui, who is also a cultivator from Buddha Country."

People were baffled, geniuses were called such because of their limitless potential, capable of fighting across levels, defeating veteran cultivators who have exhausted their potential, using them as stepping stones and backgroundboards.

But these two veterans were the exceptions.

Not only were their combat abilities strong, they were also versed in the Hundred Skills of Cultivation, not to say masters, but certainly knew more than most cultivators of the same level, making it very hard to defeat them by using the Hundred Skills of Cultivation.

Their combat consciousness was also incredible, not resembling what one would expect of their realms, even when facing numerous assailants, they handled it with ease.

"Even if it’s a group battle among Transcendance Tribulation semi-immortals, I’ve seen too many to count, and you think this few could trap me?"

The geniuses were dubious; in this era, Transcendance Tribulation semi-immortals seldom fought, not to mention frequently battled in groups, where had these two gained such experience?

Rumors said that both of them had entered an ancient secret realm, where countless ancient great powers had left clones and inheritances; they spent numerous years passing the trials, undergoing transformation, and obtaining ancient inheritances.

The rumor stirred up a frenzy in the Cultivation World, everyone hoping to become as powerful as Mo Xiaochou and Duan Qingshui.

Sadly, they found nothing.

When the prisoners saw the reports on these two, they cheerfully paused their battles, agreeing to continue fighting tomorrow.

"I knew Little Mo was capable!"

"Little Duan hasn’t tarnished the name of Great Qian!"

Lu Yang witnessed all these scenes but just smiled faintly without any intention to get involved.

He was very busy, trying to avoid getting involved in trivial matters; the most crucial thing for him now was cultivation.

"Lu Yang, River Spirit used water to create a body for me, how does it look?" Bai Shuang hopped around like a child, showing off her new body.

"Writing a cultivation technique is so hard, can you help me write it?" Xia He complained incessantly, how exactly to write a cultivation technique, that stinky Lu Yang didn’t even give a hint.

Lu Yang lay on a rocking chair, swaying and scrolling through the news, turning his head to take a bite: "It tastes good."

He used a little force to bounce forward, eating a few more bites of Yun Mengmeng’s new dish, relishing it.

"It’s time for closed-door breakthrough."

Chapter 1347: 1346: Breakthrough

In the abode, time seemed to lose its meaning; everything was flowing in reverse.

The tattered cultivation technique appeared as good as new, blossoming flowers reverted to buds, Lu Yang’s often-handled peach core transformed into an Immortal Peach, and even Lu Yang’s realm continuously regressed.

Pass Tribulation Stage, Refining the Void Stage... Foundation Establishment Stage, Qi Cultivation Stage, Mortal, appearance changed from a young man to a teenager, from a teenager to a child.

When he became an infant in swaddling, the changes continued, reverting back to a past life’s form, and then again to the peak of Pass Tribulation.

Everything inside the abode cycled through reincarnation, repeating endlessly without halting.

This signified that Lu Yang had taken another step forward in understanding the prototype of Trace Origin Dao Fruit.

Indeed, when Lu Yang in the Immortal Fairy’s cave mansion realized that the method of transcendence by the hidden mastermind was similar to Ashy Bean’s method of transcendence, and ancient times were replaying in the era of the Fire Dynasty, with all things in the world cycling in reincarnation, Lu Yang’s mastery of Trace Origin Dao Fruit’s prototype took a significant step forward.

However, worried that the hidden mastermind was nearby, he dared not reveal this and deliberately delayed for a long time, pretending that he went through many events, leading to his current understanding of Trace Origin Dao Fruit.

Now, with his state at consummation, his cultivation level continuously rising, having cultivated the Pass Tribulation Stage to its limit, perfectly synchronizing it with the Dao Fruit’s perfection, he was ready to achieve immortality!

Ability-wise, Lu Yang’s Trace Origin Dao Fruit shared many similarities with the Time Dao Fruit – both could reverse time and regress cultivation levels.

Dao Fruits often have similar capabilities, for instance, the Time Immortal can use Time Dao Fruit to halt time, and the Jiuchong Immortal can use Yin-Yang Dao Fruit to invoke the Extreme Cold Domain to freeze time, both cases involve stopping time, yet the methods employed are vastly different.

Similarly, Trace Origin Dao Fruit and Time Dao Fruit, though analogous, are two different paths. Time Dao Fruit, while regressing an opponent’s cultivation, also requires the spiritual energy used during the opponent’s cultivation to revert to its original place.

Whereas, Lu Yang can simply make the opponent’s cultivation regress without impacting the whole world; it is conceptually a "reversal".

Therefore, Lu Yang can revert magic skills to their original forms and revert himself to a previous life’s form—abilities beyond the reach of Time Immortal.

...

Outside the abode, Yun Mengmeng was humming a tune while baking cookies, Bai Shuang eagerly watched on.

"It’s about time now."

Yun Mengmeng opened the oven, pulled out the baking tray, and discovered the cookies had no signs of being baked, they were still dough.

"Huh? What happened here? Didn’t I light the fire?"

"My recently broken-through realm!" Xia He stomped, she had finally managed a breakthrough in a minor realm, and now it had regressed back to its prior form.

Yun Mengmeng baked the cookies as a celebration for her, now it turned out the cookies weren’t baked, and Xia He hadn’t broken through.

Not far away, Qinghe was compiling the history of the Great Bean Dynasty. As she wrote the last word, she sighed, lifted the history book, and admired her great work.

"Finally finished."

Qinghe put great effort into writing this book to glorify the Emperor, documenting Emperor Dou’s great exploits.

However, before she could be joyous for long, the words on the book swiftly disappeared, first by lines, then by volumes, until the entire book became clean and as new as it was initially.

"My book!" Qinghe rarely became emotionally excited, her pitch two decibels higher than usual.

It was not just Sky Gate Mountain; the entire Seek Dao Sect was engulfed in a bizarre state.

Li Haoran stayed awake around the clock, crafting a peerless magic artifact in forty-nine days. He was already a renowned Body Integration Realm Artifact Refining Master in the universe; cultivating artifacts that even those in Pass Tribulation Stage would vie to buy through connections.

Suddenly, inexplicable events happened; the peerless magic artifact suddenly reverted to raw materials.

"I’ll count down from three to one, and then we jump together." River Spirit, Ether Robot, and Daoist Xiantian, the trio playing an online game, finally reached the last level.

As they controlled their characters to jump to the opposite shore, a victory settlement screen appeared, and the game directly returned to the first level, erasing all their progress records.

"No—" River Spirit and Daoist Xiantian screamed in despair.

Seventh Elder, who was refining pills, and Daoist Buyu, who was rarely handling sect affairs, ... all encountered similar issues.

"Is it haunted?"

For a moment, everyone in Seek Dao Sect was panic-stricken.

Yun Zhi appeared in front of Lu Yang’s cave mansion’s entrance, revealing a trace of a smile.

"Xiao Zhi, what’s going on?"

"Junior Brother is about to have a breakthrough. The seal set by the cave mansion cannot contain the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype, and its power is leaking."

Yun Zhi raised her hand and set up an even more profound and mysterious array method in front of the cave mansion’s main entrance, thus sparing Seek Dao Sect from disaster.

"We don’t know when Junior Brother will break through, so you all should temporarily leave Sky Gate Mountain," said Yun Zhi, careful not to use her Spiritual Sense to probe inside the cave mansion, worrying it might affect Lu Yang’s cultivation.

As everyone hastily evacuated Sky Gate Mountain, intensely thick dark clouds appeared above Seek Dao Sect, pressing down like a heavy mountain, making everyone feel uneasy as if suffocated by the weight on their chests.

"Immortal Ascension Tribulation? Who’s ascending to immortality?!" Wu Yao, who was squatting in jail, looked up, his pupils shrinking, recognizing at a glance that this was the coveted Immortal Ascension Tribulation every cultivator dreamt of.

"It’s the Immortal Ascension Tribulation!" Someone shouted, and instantly everyone in Seek Dao Sect realized that this was the legendary Immortal Ascension Tribulation.

"Who is undergoing the tribulation to become immortal!"

"Looking at the direction of the Thunder Tribulation, it’s moving towards Sky Gate Mountain, it must be Elder Brother Lu Yang who’s undergoing the tribulation!" Tao Yaoye exclaimed excitedly, happy for Lu Yang.

"Is it Lu Yang?"

Everyone was immensely shocked. How much time had Lu Yang spent on his cultivation? His speed of progressing from a mortal to a Semi-Immortal had already made history. Everyone thought that becoming an immortal would be a hurdle that might slow down Lu Yang’s pace.

But Lu Yang was actually about to overcome this hurdle and become immortal!

Bang—

The cave mansion exploded, and Lu Yang flew out, facing the Heavenly Tribulation directly. The aura emanating from his body broke through the limits of the Pass Tribulation Stage, precisely this force that had drawn the Immortal Ascension Tribulation!

"Everyone, stay away from Sky Gate Mountain!" Daoist Buyu shouted urgently. Although the Immortal Ascension Tribulation wouldn’t target the bystanders, there was always a chance that a stray thunderbolt might stray their way, and that would be disastrous.

Upon hearing this, everyone quickly evacuated Seek Dao Sect, simultaneously messaging friends and even starting live streams, all immensely excited, sharing this joyous occasion.

The first to sense the appearance of the Immortal Ascension Tribulation was Yingtian Immortal.

Yingtian Immortal stood above the clouds, looking down at Lu Yang, feeling somewhat troubled.

"Immortal Fairy, stand back a bit. According to the rules, the Immortal Ascension Tribulation should be faced alone, you being here counts as two people, which would increase its power," he warned.

Immortal Fairy Eternity waved impatiently, fiercely warning Yingtian Immortal, "Go away, I’m just standing here, strike down based on one person’s measurement, don’t strike extra!"

"Eh, never mind, I’ll try my best."

Chapter 1348: 1347: Immortal Ascension Tribulation

The news of Lu Yang’s imminent ascension to immortality during the Seek Dao Sect’s Immortal Ascension Tribulation spread rapidly across the universe.

Unlike the last time Wu Yao passed his tribulation, when the Cultivation World’s spiritual energy technology was just starting, the speed of information transmission was very limited.

With the rapid development of spiritual energy technology over the years, communication technology has reached an extremely terrifying level, with no delay even in light-year communications.

The live broadcast of Lu Yang crossing the Immortal Ascension Tribulation quickly climbed to the number one spot in popularity, followed by several other broadcasts showing different angles of the Immortal Ascension Tribulation.

"We don’t even need to do anything." The Thief Ancestor remarked, as a major shareholder in the universal broadcasting, manipulating the popularity of a stream was an easy task.

As it turned out, he was worried for nothing. An Immortal Ascension Tribulation, a rarity since ancient times, easily claimed the top spot without any manipulation needed.

The Thief Ancestor watched enviously; he had been stuck in the Half-Immortal Realm for eighty-thousand years without even touching the threshold of immortality, while this up-and-comer was about to ascend.

"What is this live room, and why is it so lively?" people from the Interstellar Alliance entered the broadcast, shocked by the unprecedented popularity and barrage of comments.

"Can’t you read the title? Someone in the Cultivation World is becoming an Immortal!!!" others from the Interstellar Alliance excitedly replied with barrage comments.

"Becoming immortal? Is that the so-called physical limit talked about in the Cultivation World?"

"Bullshit physical limit, do you even understand cultivation? This is a completely different level of life."

"Just another one brainwashed by marketing accounts."

The Salvation Team from Sky Sea Star, saved by Lu Yang, came forward one after another, stating how powerful and the pinnacle of life evolution Lu Yang was.

The team leader Zhan Po said nothing, simply rewarding silently in deep silence.

In addition to the Salvation Team, there were others with strange names like "Prisoner Peak True Inheritance," "Thankful for Prisoner Peak’s Nurturing," "Immortal Fourth," and "Ancient Barbarian Tribe," explaining to onlookers just how powerful Lu Yang was.

"Fun fact: When Heavenly Emperor Wu Yao underwent his Immortal Ascension Tribulation, he shattered an entire star system."

"This Immortal Ascension Tribulation is not so powerful; it didn’t even damage the surrounding mountains because the Heavenly Tribulation was all concentrated on Lu Yang."

"Why is the quality so blurry? I can’t see clearly."

"Naive child, stop your nonsense. What blurry quality? The Immortal Ascension Tribulation has distorted space, and it would look the same even if you were on the scene." A Cultivator stepped forward to explain, a main profile view, a Great Cultivator in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage.

"Is it really that amazing?" Even with the reality in front of them, some remained skeptical, thinking it was just a few lightning strikes; their mechanically augmented bodies could withstand it as well.

"You can try the holographic broadcast room." Someone sneered. Doubtful, huh? Then have a taste of the hardship.

"Holographic broadcast room?" That person quickly found the holographic broadcast room. Strangely enough, despite all being broadcasts of the Immortal Ascension Tribulation, this one had very low popularity.

He connected to the port, as if he had truly arrived at the Seek Dao Sect, facing the rolling thunder of the heavens up close.

Only then did he realize the horror of the Immortal Ascension Tribulation; entirely different from the lightning and thunder seen in nature, it was a tribulation beyond natural phenomena, capable of annihilating everything. Any being facing the Immortal Ascension Tribulation has only one outcome: to turn into ashes in the tribulation.

Just as he was about to speak, he bled from all seven orifices and was ejected from the holographic state.

"The danger alert has been activated!" The person was incredibly shocked; this was the holographic system’s safety mechanism, designed to check the user’s health and eject them before any mishap.

The holographic system could only mimic less than one ten-thousandth of the Immortal Ascension Tribulation’s power, which meant facing even that trivial amount was unbearable and a risk to his life.

Having only witnessed the Immortal Ascension Tribulation for a split second, he was profoundly shaken and finally understood why the holographic broadcast room was so unpopular.

Ordinary people didn’t even qualify to watch.

"Lu Yang is awesome!" That person immediately sent a barrage message and became a fan of Lu Yang.

This is an immortal who can withstand the Immortal Ascension Tribulation without flinching; there’s no shame in acknowledging this.

In the eyes of people from other civilizations, Lu Yang is no different from the celestial beings of their legends and myths.

"May Lu Yang, the Great Immortal, bless my exams to go smoothly."

"May Lu Yang, the Great Immortal, bless me with good health."

"May Lu Yang, the Great Immortal, bless me in finding a good wife."

Suddenly, the live stream’s barrage turned into a wishing well, leaving Lu Yang utterly bemused.

"Where is this Power of Faith coming from?" Lu Yang, who was in the midst of Crossing the Immortal Tribulation, suddenly felt the Power of Faith surging from all directions.

After entering a new era, many cultivators in the Cultivation World liked to take shortcuts, showing off their Divine Abilities in live streams, gathering the Power of Faith to advance at a rapid pace. The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and The Controller, upon learning of this, lamented not being born in such a fortunate era.

However, Lu Yang clearly wasn’t one to follow this path. He drew a ring with his sword tip, isolating himself from all the Power of Faith.

The dark clouds split open, unleashing terrifying Chaotic Divine Light, which left the onlookers in awe, but Lu Yang showed no sign of panic. With a single sword strike, he shattered the Chaotic Divine Light, handling it with ease.

The Vast Sea Daoist Lord, who was enjoying his honeymoon, heard that Lu Yang was Passing the Immortal Tribulation and immediately opened a spatial channel to arrive, laughing and scolding, "This little brat’s cultivation speed is truly fast."

Celestial Master Guiyuan invited a few of his old brothers to the Seek Dao Sect to see how outstanding their junior brothers were, and whether other sects could even compare.

The Demon Emperor Zhu Tian called his ministers to the Imperial Palace to watch Lu Yang Passing the Tribulation together.

As the initiator of the Great World Struggle and the son of the era’s destiny, Zhu Tian felt that he had the potential to become immortal, but the existence of the Qilin Immortal above him meant he would definitely not achieve immortality.

He regretfully hit his leg: "I chose the wrong Dao Fruit!"

If he had known, he would have chosen a different Dao Fruit.

The Sword Tower Supreme, watching through the live broadcast, exclaimed in admiration: "As expected of Lu Yang, the one with the greatest talent since ancient times."

She failed the Immortal Ascension Tribulation and survived by a stroke of luck; she knew all too well the terrifying tribulation one must endure to become immortal.

Thinking back to the extreme torment she experienced during her own tribulation, and then seeing how Lu Yang seemed to be taking a leisurely walk, a gap surfaced in her heart, being a fellow Sword Cultivator, and myriad emotions condensed into a sigh: "I cannot compare."

The Four Ancient Immortals had arrived at the Seek Dao Sect and were equally moved by the sight; they did not have it as easy as Lu Yang when they themselves Passed the Immortal Ascension Tribulation.

"It’s almost like passing the Immortal Ascension as an Immortal." Immortal Jiuchong lamented; among those he knew, only the Immortal Fairy could Pass the Tribulation with such ease.

"After all, he was seen by the Immortal and has known the Immortal since the Foundation Establishment Stage," said the Qilin Immortal. This was tantamount to having an immortal accompany him from the beginning of his cultivation; whose cultivation conditions could be so favorable.

"Indeed, it would be strange if he turned out weak with such nurturing."

Lu Yang weighed the Green Peak Sword in his hand, then stored it in his Identity Jade Pendant. Using the Immortal Sword for the tribulation felt like cheating; he preferred to let go of it to refine his swordsmanship.

Without the Green Peak Sword to dissolve the Heavenly Tribulation, the pressure on Lu Yang doubled.

A muffled rumbling sound came from the sky; it was the new wave of attack being brewed by the Heavenly Tribulation.

The lightning was endless, turning into Hongmeng Purple Qi and smashing towards Lu Yang, vast and unrivaled.

Lu Yang bit his tongue tip, and with his fingers together, he shot out a vibrating Sword Qi, slashing through in one swoop, cutting the Hongmeng Purple Qi in two; his Sword Qi’s might remained undiminished, splitting the Immortal Ascension Tribulation in half!

Chapter 1349: 1348: Becoming an Immortal

"The Heavenly Tribulation was cleaved asunder?" The people watching were horrified, as never before in history had anyone heard of someone who could reverse the Immortal Ascension Tribulation and actually split the Heavenly Tribulation.

"Is the first Sword Immortal about to emerge?" whispered an ancient figure who was at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage.

Although the Green Peak Sword was an Immortal Artifact forged by Wu Yao, Wu Yao was not a Sword Cultivator but simply used the sword as a weapon, which is why he incorporated the Small World function into the Green Peak Sword; it was not pure enough.

The Immortal Ascension Tribulation reconverged, with lightning raging, and the earth, fire, wind, and water reshaping heaven and earth. A world-shocking great calamity descended, and even watching through the screen one feels a suffocation in their chest and difficulty breathing, a suppression of all living beings by the Heavenly Tribulation.

The Sword Qi that Lu Yang took pride in was slashed into luminous points amidst the sky-filled tribulations, and Lu Yang’s own body was cracking open.

Those at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage and Semi-Immortals looked on solemnly. Lu Yang’s strength was well-known, yet still, he was injured severely.

Lu Yang’s eyes sparkled brilliantly, not at all indicative of someone gravely injured. He was simultaneously channeling both the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype and the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, not for healing his body, but for shaping the Sword Qi.

Without the Green Peak Sword, the Sword Qi unleashed was a terrifying blow against those at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, but against the Immortal Ascension Tribulation, it was merely capable of cleaving through once before shattering.

With the Sword Qi blessed by the dual Dao Fruit Outlines, it became increasingly distinct and visible, no longer resembling Sword Qi, but a real, tangible Immortal Sword!

Boom——

Lightning struck once more, making everyone’s heart tremble; other civilizations could not imagine such terrifying calamities being withstood by fragile flesh and blood.

Thump——

Like a droplet disturbing the peaceful surface of a lake, Lu Yang’s heart was as still as still water. He stepped forward, with Sword Qi flourishing beneath his feet, transforming into an ocean of Swordsmanship, colliding with the lightning of the Heavenly Tribulation, tearing through space, shaking the universe.

Countless Swordsmanship Practitioners, upon seeing this scene, were greatly excited. To contend against the Heavenly Tribulation is the epitome of Swordsmanship.

They observed Lu Yang’s Swordsmanship, gaining much insight, and hurriedly sat down to meditate and comprehend.

Above and below the Sword Tower, Sword Qi soared to the sky — it turned out that all the Sword Tower Practitioners were engaged in Enlightenment!

Those who had never before encountered mortals, a small number of them, suddenly had a moment of enlightenment, developing a longing for Swordsmanship.

They were innately talented in Swordsmanship, their circumstances never allowed it, they had never encountered Swordsmanship. Now, Lu Yang’s demonstration of Swordsmanship awakened their deep desire for it, helping them step onto the great path of becoming Sword Cultivators!

The power of the Immortal Ascension Tribulation continued to increase, yet Lu Yang’s Sword Qi became increasingly sharp and indestructible. Within the range of the Swordmanship Domain, everything was being annihilated, including the Heavenly Tribulation.

The world was astounded; even Immortals were no exception. From ancient times to the present, it was always the Semi-Immortals who desperately endured the tribulation to achieve Immortal Fruit Position. Who had ever seen someone relying on human power to challenge the Heavenly Tribulation and take the initiative?!

"The power of the two Dao Fruit Outlines is immense," sighed the Yingtian Immortal. He was all too aware of the power of the Immortal Ascension Tribulation; to possess two Dao Fruit Outlines at the same time was an unprecedented event.

Lu Yang’s shattered body healed, untainted by worldly dust, and his soul grew stronger and transformed, becoming crystalline and flawless, perfect without blemish.

Ascending to the Immortal rank, he underwent a complete transformation.

Lu Yang was effervescent, and his body glowed, like a sun that was unspeakably sacred, making even the statues in temples pale in comparison to Lu Yang at this moment.

He was radiant and attracted the attention of everyone, becoming the focus of the entire Universe.

Lu Yang actively dispersed all Sword Qi, spread his arms wide, and an invisible force emanated from him, surging outward.

The final phase of the Immortal Ascension Tribulation, still brewing, was directly scattered by this force, unable to condense any further.

All was silent in the world.

Lu Yang let out a long breath, feeling a sense of consummation at this moment, looking down upon the world with detachment and transcendence.

Unlike temporarily becoming an Immortal with the aid of a body built from the faith of Buddha Country, back then his Spiritual Sense was weak, not strong enough to control the Immortal Body, and he needed the help of Fairy Eternity’s Immortal Sense.

Now his Immortal Sense is complete, his Dao Fruit is consummated, no longer needing the strength of others, standing tall at the end of the Immortal Dao, achieving the goal that all Cultivators aspire to their entire lives—Immortalhood.

"Did Lu Yang... succeed?" Finally, someone broke the silence; it was people from other civilizations. They did not have a profound concept of immortality and were the first to awaken to this reality.

"He has become an Immortal! Lu Yang has become an Immortal!" A Cultivator shouted this fact, followed by a series of jubilant cries one after another.

Unlike other Immortals, Lu Yang’s ascension process was live-streamed, watched by the entire universe, every move he made during his Tribulation touching the hearts of everyone.

Thus, as he gained success and fame, achieving the Immortal Fruit Position, the whole universe erupted in celebration and cheers, sharing the joy with Lu Yang.

The news of Lu Yang’s Immortal Ascension immediately dominated headlines across major platforms, nobody had the mind to discuss anything else, all focused on talking about this fact, discussing just how formidable the Cultivation World is.

"Congratulations to Senior Brother Lu on becoming an Immortal!" Junior Brothers and Sisters came one after another to congratulate him, their faces brimming with radiant smiles, genuinely happy from the heart.

"Second-in-command, I knew you could do it!" Yun Mengmeng said she believed in Lu Yang, but in reality, when Lu Yang was facing his Tribulation, she was tightly clutching onto Yun Zhi’s sleeve, terrified that something might go wrong.

"Congratulations." Qinghe said tersely. With only two words, it sounded without emotion, but having known her for so long, Lu Yang understood Qinghe’s nature; she was truly happy, just shy to express her emotions.

"Not bad, you’ve finally become an Immortal." Yun Zhi remarked, revealing a rare smile that vanished in a blink, as if she had never smiled before.

"I still have to thank Eldest Senior Sister for nurturing me..."

Yun Zhi waved her hand dismissively: "There’s no need for such formalities between you and me."

"My good disciple, don’t forget your master now that you’re an Immortal." Daoist Buyu cheekily drew close, feeling that the best two things he had done in his life were to take on Yun Zhi and Lu Yang as disciples.

With such accomplished disciples, he could just lay back for the rest of his life.

Lu Yang fell silent for a moment, remembering the words of his Eldest Senior Sister just now, feeling that their master-disciple relationship was so good there was no need for formality: "My Immortality is all thanks to my own effort."

Daoist Buyu: "..."

I recall you haven’t had much interaction with your Second Senior Brother.

Ao Ling, Jiang Lianyi, Jin Caiwei, Zhu Tian, and others also arrived. Some offered verbal congratulations, while others presented generous gifts. The weight of the gift was not important; it was the thought that counted.

With Lu Yang’s becoming an Immortal, the entire universe was celebrating, and the Seek Dao Sect was decorated with bright lights and colors, many times livelier than the New Year.

Seeing this, Yun Zhi’s eyes twitched slightly, feeling relieved that her breakthrough didn’t cause such a stir. Few people knew of her strength, otherwise she would have to face a situation like her Junior Brother with the whole Sect celebrating.

Although she was reluctant to leave the mortal world and did not wish to Harmonize, she felt out of place and uncomfortable in such lively environments, preferring to stay away from them.

The celebration continued for several days before the excitement finally began to subside, allowing Lu Yang to break free and do what he wanted.

Fairy Eternity floated out of Lu Yang’s body and offered her individual congratulations, "Little Yang, congratulations on becoming an Immortal..."

"Fairy, let me help you restore your body."

"Ah?"

Chapter 1350: 1349: Making Trouble

After Lu Yang became an Immortal, he not only felt that his Immortal Sense had immensely expanded to envelop the universe, but the Trace Origin Dao Fruit had also become extremely powerful, able to affect other Immortals.

For instance, it healed Fairy Eternity’s path injuries, restoring her to the state she was in when she fought Ashy Bean, giving her an Immortal Body.

"Ah? Wait..."

Fairy Eternity was stunned, and before she could react, she saw Lu Yang activating the Trace Origin Dao Fruit. The powerful rule force reversed the world, disregarded causality, and Fairy Eternity’s slightly transparent soul gradually solidified, becoming flesh and blood, possessing temperature and touch.

Fairy Eternity felt inner turmoil, unable to control her emotions for a moment. The eternal aura spread around, and the plants at her feet were baptized by this eternal aura, transforming completely. Golden lines appeared on their leaves, signifying their transition from Spirit Grass to Immortal Herbs, which could grant longevity if consumed.

Her single drop of blood, a tear, were all objects of longevity that people pursued, incredibly precious.
