Fairy Eternity was tearful yet tearless, preoccupied with being happy for Little Yang, never having expected that after Little Yang became Immortal, the Trace Origin Dao Fruit would have such capabilities.

I, the Immortal, do not wish to leave the comfortable Spiritual Space. I want to reside here forever.

Fairy Eternity was also secretly pleased, which indicated that Little Yang always had her in his mind.

But still, I don’t want to leave!

"Fairy, congratulations!" Lu Yang excitedly embraced Fairy Eternity, causing her immense panic.

Every time Lu Yang thought of Fairy Eternity’s tragic state after battling with Ashy Bean, his heart tightened with guilt, feeling it was due to his own weakness.

After becoming an Immortal, he could finally make up for past regrets, allowing the Fairy to regain her Immortal Body.

But as the Spiritual Space quietened down, Lu Yang felt somewhat unaccustomed, missing the noisy days of the past.

"The Chief Housekeeper has an Immortal Body now?" Qinghe, upon hearing the news, hurried over first, crying tears of joy. However, seeing the Chief Housekeeper’s panicked expression while being embraced by Lu Yang, she felt as if the sky was falling.

Seeing that there were outsiders present, Fairy Eternity quickly pushed Lu Yang away.

Yun Mengmeng, Ao Ling, and others also rushed over upon hearing the news. Yun Mengmeng, curiously pinching Fairy Eternity’s arm, found it icily soft.

"So this is what the Chief Housekeeper feels like."

"Stop, stop touching." Fairy Eternity hid beside Lu Yang, secretly poking his waist, mumbling softly, "It’s all your fault, all your fault."

Making Lu Yang baffled.

"Has the senior Fairy recovered?" Yun Zhi showed a smile, its angle peculiar, visible only to Fairy Eternity, frustrating Fairy Eternity who turned her head pouting, unwilling to respond to Yun Zhi.

"I was just about to gather some people to celebrate the Junior Brother becoming an Immortal. Now that double happiness has arrived, we can also celebrate for the senior Fairy’s recovery."

...

At the peak of Sky Gate Mountain, in front of the Soybean Dynasty Ancestor Temple, Immortal Energy swirled around, creating a peaceful and joyous atmosphere with constant laughter and chatter.

Yun Mengmeng showcased her skills while Qinghe assisted in the kitchen, bustling with activity, looking very lively.

"Why won’t they let me be the main chef?" Now that she has regained her Immortal Body, Fairy Eternity planned to show off her skills, but was stopped by Lu Yang, puffing up with anger, seeing how Little Yang had grown bold after becoming an Immortal.

"Won’t you persuade Little Yang, don’t you want to taste my cuisine?" Fairy Eternity sought help from the Four Ancient Immortals, who each played deaf and dumb, hesitating without speaking.

Lu Yang gently soothed her: "Fairy, you are the star of today, finally having recovered your body, how could we let you toil so much?"

Fairy Eternity thought about it, realized it made sense, and decided not to do any work as advised.

The Four Ancient Immortals sighed in relief at this.

"In this world, only Little Friend Lu can persuade Fairy Eternity."

In Ancient Times, no matter how they tried to persuade, Fairy Eternity wouldn’t listen and would do whatever she wanted, which scared them beyond measure.

"I think Little Friend Lu getting along with Fairy Eternity is quite good."

"Indeed, I support it."

Finally, someone could tame Fairy Eternity, and the Four Ancient Immortals were happy to see it happen.

"Even though we knew he would become an immortal, we didn’t expect it to happen so fast." Jiang Pingan and Meng Junzi, who were invited, saw Lu Yang surrounded by people, chatting merrily, and marveled at how they had aged.

"Here come the buns!" Yun Mengmeng came out with a big steamer full of steaming buns, round and plump, their fragrance wafting from afar; Qinghe followed with a second steamer of buns.

Lu Yang couldn’t wait to take a bite, and although he couldn’t tell the filling, it was so delicious and oily that he couldn’t help but smile with joy.

Every time he tasted Yun Mengmeng’s cooking, Lu Yang inwardly lamented this was the consequence of self-learning and not following the Fairy’s instructions.

The celebration banquet was of the highest standard, starting with Semi-Immortals, and eating buns might seem inappropriate, but those present were not particular about formalities.

Even the Yingtian Immortal didn’t think there was anything wrong with eating buns at this occasion, as long as Fairy Eternity wasn’t the chef, anything was fine.

Next to the buns was freshly squeezed peach juice from the Heavenly Root, which Jiang Pingan and Meng Junzi, after drinking, deemed extraordinary, ranking it among the top of all the Immortal products they had tasted.

Midway through the celebration, people started to encourage Lu Yang to say a few words.

Unable to refuse, Lu Yang reluctantly stepped up, holding peach juice and moving among the crowd, offering thanks to everyone.

"Qilin Immortal, I toast to you. Thanks to your trade with Wu Yao, I was exchanged here, and could start a new life. You are my lifesaver." After saying that, Lu Yang downed the peach juice in one go.

Qilin Immortal clinked his cup with Lu Yang’s and also drank in one gulp: "No need to mention it, you revived the Fairy Eternity, we should be thanking you instead."

"Immortal Jiuchong, I salute you. If you hadn’t intervened timely during my battle with The Controller in the Extreme Northern Lands, I might have perished there. I haven’t formally thanked you, so thank you."

Immortal Jiuchong was a bit taken aback by Lu Yang’s sincerity, and seeing him so flustered made everyone else laugh.

"Jiuchong, he’s thanking you, say a few words." Yingtian Immortal teased from the side.

Immortal Jiuchong glared at Yingtian Immortal, and the laughter around grew even louder.

"Time Immortal, I salute you. The way you used the Power of Faith to mold an Immortal Body and Golden Body was truly unexpected. Considering all the shameful past I know about you, and you haven’t troubled me, shows your magnanimity. It’s no wonder you are the oldest of the four seniors, such dignity."

Time Immortal didn’t react much, but Yingtian Immortal’s face darkened upon hearing that.

"Ying Tian, Little Lu is pointing at you, always intentionally troubling others." Now it was Immortal Jiuchong’s turn to tease, laughing loudly.

"Get lost, get lost, get lost." Yingtian Immortal, not one to be easily ridiculed, retorted, "I don’t believe you didn’t have ideas about fixing Little Lu; it’s only after seeing how powerful Daoist Yun Zhi is that you all chickened out."

"That’s called knowing the situation." Immortal Jiuchong said with pride.

"Yingtian Immortal, I toast to you, precisely because you assassinated the Fairy, possessed the real Yingtian Immortal, polluted the faith’s source during Ashy Bean’s revival, which led to all subsequent events."
